+// Anthropic's stop_reason "refusal" (category "bio"); code_boss read it as an EMPTY reply, nudged "Empty again" twice,
+// said "rephrase" - and every later request, carrying that message, was refused too).
+//   [1] the Anthropic adapter maps stop_reason "refusal" to finish_reason "content_filter" - whole, and streamed
+//       (the stream now ends with its finish reason, as an OpenAI stream does)
+//   [2] through the real server: a refused reply is not nudged; the developer is told; a provider-refused event
+//   [3] the next request leaves the refused message out, and says so - so the conversation goes on

+console.log('\n[2] through the real server');
+const agentReqs = [];
+const backend = async (messages) => {
+  const all = messages.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+  const last = String(messages[messages.length - 1].content || '');
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  if (/Bring the record up to date for the new items/.test(all)) return { content: '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' + close };
+  if (/Found in the earlier work by the words of these/.test(all)) return { content: 'none' + close };
+  agentReqs.push(last);
+  const heads = [...last.matchAll(/(?:^|\n)--- (?:developer|code_boss) [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n/g)];
+  const now = heads.length ? last.slice(heads[heads.length - 1].index) : last;
+  if (/REFUSE ME/.test(last)) return { content: '', finish_reason: 'content_filter' };   // the provider refuses while that message is in the conversation
+  if (/Second question/.test(now)) return { content: 'Answered the second question. <hand-over/>' + close };
+  return { content: 'Answered. <hand-over/>' + close };
+};

+  console.log('\n[3] the conversation goes on');

+  ok(/the developer's message here is left out - the model's provider refused/.test(next), 'and says a message was left out, and why');
+  ok((await evs()).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /Answered the second question/.test(String(e.content))), 'and it is answered');

+  ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e));

+/**
+ * A MESSAGE TYPED DURING A QUIET RUN IS ANSWERED (src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs; found live, message by message,
+ * 2026-11-03: recall woke the agent in a quiet run, the developer typed "rounded?" meanwhile - Enter sends it at the next
+ * step - the run had nothing to add and ended leaving no trace, and the message went with it, never answered).
+ *   [1] nothing to add, nothing typed: the quiet run leaves no trace, as before
+ *   [2] nothing to add, a message typed meanwhile: the run takes it and answers it - and is not quiet from there, so an
+ *       answer that is "nothing to add" again does not take the message away
+ * Run: node test/test-quiet-run-steer.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-quiet-home-'));
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-quiet-'));
+
+// `late`: the message arrives while the model is answering (taken after the reply), not before the first call
+const run = async (replies, typed, late = false) => {
+  let i = 0; const sent = [];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { sent.push(req.messages.map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n')); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };
+  const events = [];
+  let steers = typed ? [typed] : [], asked = 0;
+  const result = await runMinimalWorker({
+    adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: '[code_boss: earlier work came back into view after your last reply: e2. If it changes your answer, say so; if not, answer <nothing-to-add/>]' }],
+    projectDir: dir, quiet: true, drainSteer: () => (late && asked++ === 0 ? [] : steers.splice(0)),
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+  });
+  return { result, events, sent };
+};
+
+console.log('\n[1] nothing to add, nothing typed');
+{
+  const r = await run(['<nothing-to-add/>'], null);
+  ok(r.result.nothingToAdd === true, 'the quiet run has nothing to add, and says so (it leaves no trace)');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] nothing to add, a message typed meanwhile');
+{
+  // (typed before the first call: it goes with it, and the model answers it there)
+  const r = await run(['Rounded: $21,161.'], 'rounded?');
+  ok(!r.result.nothingToAdd, 'not "nothing to add": a message was typed');
+  ok(r.events.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && e.content === 'rounded?') && /rounded\?/.test(r.sent[0]), 'the message is taken into the run and sent to the model');

=== add test/test-rate-limit-retry-probe.mjs
new f03ca0f4ee29630b62363f0e
+/**
+ * Rate-limit (429) patience — BOTH HTTP adapters must ride out a sustained 429 (the quota refills) by
+ * pausing + retrying for up to a cumulative budget, instead of failing the run after a few tries. This is the
+ * production (genai.mil → Gemini, openai-http) path the developer hit: repeated 429s (amplified by sub-agent
+ * fan-out) were failing every run. Applies to EVERY LLM call (main agent + sub-agents).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-rate-limit-retry-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { createHttpAdapter } from '../src/llm/openai-http.mjs';
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// A fetch that returns 429 the first N calls, then a valid completion. Counts calls + records sleeps via timing.
+function make429Fetch(n429, shape) {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const fn = async () => {
+    calls++;
+    if (calls <= n429) {
+      return { ok: false, status: 429, statusText: 'Too Many Requests', headers: { get: () => '0' }, async text() { return JSON.stringify({ error: { message: 'rate limited' } }); }, async json() { return { error: { message: 'rate limited' } }; } };
+    }
+    return { ok: true, status: 200, headers: { get: () => null }, async text() { return JSON.stringify(shape); }, async json() { return shape; } };
+  };
+  fn.calls = () => calls;
+  return fn;
+}
+
+/** A fetch that answers with `status` the first n calls, then a valid completion. */
+function makeStatusFetch(status, nBad, shape, statusText = 'Overloaded') {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const body = { type: 'error', error: { type: 'overloaded_error', message: 'Server is at capacity (4 active). Please retry shortly.' } };
+  const fn = async () => {
+    calls++;
+    if (calls <= nBad) return { ok: false, status, statusText, headers: { get: () => null }, async text() { return JSON.stringify(body); }, async json() { return body; } };
+    return { ok: true, status: 200, headers: { get: () => null }, async text() { return JSON.stringify(shape); }, async json() { return shape; } };
+  };
+  fn.calls = () => calls;
+  return fn;
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const OPENAI_OK = { id: 'x', object: 'chat.completion', choices: [{ index: 0, message: { role: 'assistant', content: 'hello' }, finish_reason: 'stop' }], usage: { prompt_tokens: 1, completion_tokens: 1 } };
+  const ANTH_OK = { id: 'x', type: 'message', role: 'assistant', content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'hello' }], stop_reason: 'end_turn', usage: { input_tokens: 1, output_tokens: 1 } };
+
+  console.log('\n[openai-http / genai.mil prod path] rides out 4 sequential 429s then succeeds');
+  {
+    const f = make429Fetch(4, OPENAI_OK);
+    const rl = [];
+    // tiny budget + the 429s have retry-after 0 so backoff is the floor; budget generous enough for 8 short sleeps
+    const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://genai.test/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 3, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 5 * 60 * 1000, onRateLimit: (ms) => rl.push(ms) });
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const r = await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] });
+    ok(/hello/.test(r.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || ''), 'completed successfully AFTER 4 rate-limit retries (did not fail the run)');
+    ok(f.calls() === 5, `retried past maxRetries=3 — ${f.calls()} calls total (4 × 429 + 1 success)`);
+    ok(rl.length === 4, `onRateLimit fired for each wait (${rl.length})`);
+    console.log('    (took ' + (Date.now() - t0) + 'ms)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[openai-http] a 429 that NEVER clears throws once the patience budget is exhausted (no infinite loop)');
+  {
+    const f = make429Fetch(9999, OPENAI_OK);
+    // tiny budget so the test is fast: ~3s total → a couple of 1s-floor sleeps then give up
+    const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://genai.test/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 3, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 3000, onRateLimit: () => {} });
+    let threw = false;
+    try { await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] }); } catch (e) { threw = /429|rate|too many/i.test(e.message) || e.status === 429; }
+    ok(threw, 'eventually throws a 429 error after exhausting the budget (bounded, not infinite)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[openai-http] Esc/abort interrupts a rate-limit wait');
+  {
+    const f = make429Fetch(9999, OPENAI_OK);
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://genai.test/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 3, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 5 * 60 * 1000, onRateLimit: () => { setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), 50); } });
+    let aborted = false;
+    try { await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }], signal: ac.signal }); } catch (e) { aborted = e.name === 'AbortError' || /abort/i.test(e.message); }
+    ok(aborted, 'a long rate-limit wait is interrupted by abort (Esc stays responsive)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[anthropic-http] also rides out 4 sequential 429s then succeeds');
+  {
+    const f = make429Fetch(4, ANTH_OK);
+    const rl = [];
+    const a = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://proxy.test', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 3, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 5 * 60 * 1000, onRateLimit: (ms) => rl.push(ms) });
+    const r = await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] });
+    ok(/hello/.test(r.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || ''), 'completed after 4 rate-limit retries');
+    ok(f.calls() === 5, `retried past maxRetries=3 — ${f.calls()} calls`);
+  }
+
+  // AT CAPACITY. A 529/503 is a 429 said differently — the endpoint is busy, not broken — and code_boss's own
+  // fan-out is what causes it. Before this, 529 shared the small network-retry budget, so a sub-agent died after
+  // about a minute of "Server is at capacity": exactly what a batch left to run overnight would do.
+  console.log('\n[anthropic-http] a 529 "at capacity" is ridden out like a 429, past maxRetries');
+  {
+    const f = makeStatusFetch(529, 2, ANTH_OK);
+    const rl = [];
+    const a = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://proxy.test', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 1, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 5 * 60 * 1000, onRateLimit: (ms) => rl.push(ms) });
+    const r = await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] });
+    ok(/hello/.test(r.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || ''), 'completed after two 529s');
+    ok(f.calls() === 3, `retried past maxRetries=1 — ${f.calls()} calls (the old budget gave up here)`);
+    ok(rl.every((ms) => ms <= 30000), `each wait is capped, so a freed slot is taken soon (${rl.join(', ')}ms)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[anthropic-http] a 529 that never clears still gives up at the end of the budget');
+  {
+    const f = makeStatusFetch(529, 9999, ANTH_OK);
+    const a = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://proxy.test', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 1, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 2000, onRateLimit: () => {} });
+    let threw = false;
+    try { await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] }); } catch (e) { threw = e.status === 529 || /capacity|overload/i.test(e.message); }
+    ok(threw, 'bounded, not infinite');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[anthropic-http] a 500 is NOT given the patient budget (a broken upstream answers 500 every time)');
+  {
+    const f = makeStatusFetch(500, 9999, ANTH_OK, 'Internal Server Error');
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const a = createAnthropicAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://proxy.test', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 1, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 60 * 60 * 1000, onRateLimit: () => {} });
+    let threw = false;
+    try { await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] }); } catch (e) { threw = e.status === 500; }
+    ok(threw && Date.now() - t0 < 20000, `gave up quickly (${Date.now() - t0}ms) rather than sitting on the rate-limit budget`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[openai-http] the same for a 503 at capacity');
+  {
+    const f = makeStatusFetch(503, 2, OPENAI_OK, 'Service Unavailable');
+    const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'https://genai.test/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl: f, maxRetries: 1, rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 5 * 60 * 1000, onRateLimit: () => {} });
+    const r = await a.complete({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] });
+    ok(/hello/.test(r.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '') && f.calls() === 3, `completed after two 503s, past maxRetries=1 (${f.calls()} calls)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/test-raw-call-marker.mjs
new a1e6d2087734e5cd21fe1ce7
+/**
+ * The 'raw-call' marker is RETIRED (2026-07-22): recordRawTx now emits a muted llm tool-row PAIR for EVERY LLM
+ * transaction (capture always-on), so an invisible call can no longer exist — the marker's whole reason. This
+ * suite pins the SUPERSEDING contract on the marker's old trigger scenario (a bare orphan verb whose first call
+ * renders no visible message):
+ *   - NO raw-call markers are emitted any more (regardless of the display toggle)
+ *   - the otherwise-invisible call IS reachable: its llm row carries the rawId → GET /api/raw-log resolves it
+ *   - the harness nudge that recovered the orphan is visible as a nudge row
+ * Run: node test/test-raw-call-marker.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+// Isolate CB_HOME BEFORE the server import — the raw display toggle persists to ~/.code_boss/prefs.json, and
+// without this the /api/raw-log-enabled POSTs below clobber the developer's real preference.
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rcm-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+function scriptedAdapter(replies) {
+  let i = 0; const next = () => replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() { const t = next(); for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; },
+    async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+  };
+}
+
+// Run one message on a fresh server with the given raw DISPLAY state; return the resulting chat events + base url.
+async function runOnce(port, rawLogOn) {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rcm-')); await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'a.txt'), 'x');
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
+  const post = (p, b) => fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(b || {}) }).then((r) => r.json());
+  // first reply is a BARE orphan verb (no prose) → the worker nudges + retries → that first call renders no
+  // assistant text (the old "invisible call" shape the marker existed for).
+  const { server } = await startServer({

+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';

+let pass = 0, fail = 0;

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

+// One SLOW prose turn (~4s) so the agent is still running while we open the popup + press Escape.
+function slowAdapter() {
+  return {
+    async *completeStream() { for (const p of ['The ', 'answer ', 'is ', 'streaming ', 'slowly ', 'right ', 'now ', 'here.']) { await sleep(500); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: p } }] }; } },
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rawesc-'));
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');

+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    await sleep(200);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] with a run in flight, open the raw-log popup, then press Escape');
+    await page.type('#input', 'answer slowly');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'running', { timeout: 10000 });
+    await sleep(300);   // ensure we're mid-stream
+    // Open the raw-log popup directly (the bug is purely in the Escape handler, not the open path).

+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.getElementById('rawLogModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'), { timeout: 6000 });

+
+    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+    await sleep(300);
+    const afterEsc = await page.evaluate(() => ({ popup: document.getElementById('rawLogModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'), status: window.__sessionRef?.status }));
+    ok(afterEsc.popup === false, 'Escape CLOSED the raw-log popup');
+    ok(afterEsc.status === 'running', `Escape did NOT cancel the running agent (status still ${afterEsc.status})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] the run completes normally (never cancelled)');

+    ok(/streaming slowly right now here/.test(finished.replace(/\s+/g, ' ')), 'the slow turn finished + its answer is shown (run was not aborted)');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 3))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }

=== add test/test-rawlog-stream-fields.mjs
new 7d6df319406d89467defc2c2
+/**
+ * THE JOIN BETWEEN THE ADAPTER AND THE RAW LOG — through the real server, to the real file.
+ *
+ * For two builds the streaming adapter emitted `streamed`, `endedEarly` and `streamShape` on every transaction,
+ * the scanner behind Snapshot #77 read all three, and every unit test on both sides passed — while the raw-log
+ * writer in between built its entry from a fixed list of fields and dropped them on the floor. The first run on
+ * the production box showed rows with none of them, after two messages had already been spent generating data
+ * that could not be read.
+ *
+ * So this drives a message through startServer with an adapter that streams a known shape, reads the
+ * raw-log.jsonl the server wrote, and asks #77 about it over HTTP. If any link in that chain drops a field
+ * again, this is where it shows.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-rawlog-stream-fields.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rawfields-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { scanRawLogForPreambles } = await import('../src/agent/preamble-scan.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29417;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// The production shape: the same plan stated several ways before the first block. Here it is deliberately
+// split across TWO completion ids, which is what a gateway gluing two model responses together would send.
+const HALF_A = 'I will write a script to apply all of these updates:\n1. Rename the button.\n2. Table the pool.\n\n';
+const HALF_B = 'I will update index.html to address all of those requests.\n\nThis includes:\n1. Renaming.\n\n'
+  + '<remote-workspace><read path="index.html"/></remote-workspace>';
+const chunk = (id, delta, extra = {}) => ({ id, object: 'chat.completion.chunk', choices: [{ index: 0, delta, ...extra }] });
+
+/** Streams the glued reply on the first call of a run, then a plain text sign-off so the run ends. */
+function makeAdapter() {
+  const state = { mode: 'glued', calls: 0 };
+  return {
+    state,
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Done.' } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream() {
+      state.calls++;
+      if (state.mode === 'glued' && state.calls === 1) {
+        yield chunk('c1', { role: 'assistant' });
+        yield chunk('c1', { content: HALF_A });
+        yield chunk('c1', {}, { finish_reason: 'stop' });
+        yield chunk('c2', { role: 'assistant' });
+        yield chunk('c2', { content: HALF_B });
+        yield chunk('c2', {}, { finish_reason: 'stop' });
+        return;
+      }
+      if (state.mode === 'forged' && state.calls === 1) {
+        // A forged turn boundary: the client-side cut breaks out of the stream here, which is the consumer
+        // stopping — the path a cancellation takes through the wrapper.
+        yield chunk('c3', { role: 'assistant' });
+        yield chunk('c3', { content: 'I will do the thing.\n\n' });
+        yield chunk('c3', { content: '--- developer ---\nok, go ahead\n' });
+        yield chunk('c3', { content: 'And here is much more that should never be read.' });
+        return;
+      }
+      yield chunk('cx', { role: 'assistant' });
+      yield chunk('cx', { content: 'Done.' });
+      yield chunk('cx', {}, { finish_reason: 'stop' });
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+async function send(text) {
+  await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+}
+async function waitSettled() {
+  for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
+    const s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json());
+    if (s.status !== 'running') return s.status;
+    await sleep(50);
+  }
+  return 'timeout';
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rawfields-'));
+  const adapter = makeAdapter();
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const logPath = path.join(projectDir, '.code_boss', 'raw-log.jsonl');
+  const entries = async () => (await readFile(logPath, 'utf8')).split('\n').filter(Boolean).map((l) => JSON.parse(l)).filter((e) => e.type === 'chat_completions');
+
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[1] a streamed call reaches the file with its shape intact');
+    await send('please update the page');
+    const st = await waitSettled();
+    ok(st !== 'timeout', `the run settled (${st})`);
+    let rows = await entries();
+    const glued = rows.find((e) => e.streamShape && e.streamShape.ids === 2);
+    ok(!!glued, `an entry carries streamShape with two completion ids (${rows.map((e) => e.streamShape && e.streamShape.ids).join(',')})`);
+    ok(glued && glued.streamed === true, 'and streamed:true');
+    ok(glued && glued.streamShape.roles === 2 && glued.streamShape.finishes === 2, `two role deltas, two finishes (${glued && JSON.stringify(glued.streamShape)})`);
+    ok(glued && !('endedEarly' in glued), 'a stream that ran to its end is not marked early');
+    // The response is the synthesized completion shape, so finish_reason sits where the scanner looks.
+    let body = glued && glued.response; if (typeof body === 'string') { try { body = JSON.parse(body); } catch {} }
+    ok(body && body.choices && body.choices[0].finish_reason === 'stop', 'finish_reason is inside the recorded response');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] a stream the client cut short is recorded as cut short');
+    adapter.state.mode = 'forged'; adapter.state.calls = 0;
+    await send('and again');
+    await waitSettled();
+    rows = await entries();
+    const cut = rows.find((e) => e.endedEarly);
+    ok(!!cut, 'an entry is marked endedEarly');
+    ok(cut && cut.endedBecause === 'consumer-stopped', `because the consumer stopped reading (${cut && cut.endedBecause})`);
+    ok(cut && cut.streamed === true, 'and it was streamed');
+    let cb = cut && cut.response; if (typeof cb === 'string') { try { cb = JSON.parse(cb); } catch {} }
+    ok(cb && cb.choices && cb.choices[0].finish_reason === null, 'with finish_reason null rather than an invented stop');
+    ok(cb && !/never be read/.test(String(cb.choices[0].message.content)), 'and the text past the boundary was not consumed');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] #77 reads the same file and says what the shape means');
+    const scan = scanRawLogForPreambles(logPath);
+    const shaped = scan.rows.filter((r) => r.shape);
+    ok(shaped.length >= 2, `the scanner sees the shape on the recorded rows (${shaped.length})`);
+    const q = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/queries?ids=77`)).json();
+    const tile = q.tiles && q.tiles[0];
+    ok(tile && /MORE THAN ONE completion id/.test(tile.body || ''), `over HTTP, the glued reply is named as concatenation (${(tile && tile.body || '').slice(0, 60)})`);
+    ok(tile && /2id/.test(tile.body || ''), 'and the row shows the count');
+    ok(tile && /cut     /.test(tile.body || ''), 'and the cut call is marked on its row');
+    ok(tile && !/cannot be read from this log/.test(tile.body || ''), 'nothing claims the shape is missing');
+    ok(tile && !/index\.html|Rename the button/.test(tile.body || ''), 'and no content from the replies is in the tile');
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await rm(process.env.CB_HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/test-readbody-limit.mjs
new a59a556f821bf91420ee0572
+/**
+ * readBody() must cap the accumulated request body so a multi-GB POST can't
+ * exhaust memory and crash the process (localhost-only, but still a DoS). The
+ * cap is configurable via CB_MAX_BODY_BYTES; over it, the socket is destroyed
+ * and the request rejected (the handler's outer catch returns 500), and the
+ * server stays alive for subsequent requests.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-readbody-limit.mjs
+ */
+process.env.CB_MAX_BODY_BYTES = '2048';   // tiny cap so the probe needn't stream 64 MB
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const PORT = 28991;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok.' } }] }; } };
+
+async function post(p, body) {
+  try {
+    const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}${p}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body });
+    return { status: r.status };
+  } catch (e) { return { status: 'neterr', err: e.message }; }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rbl-'));
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+  });
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[A] an over-cap body is rejected (not accepted/processed)');
+    const big = JSON.stringify({ path: 'x'.repeat(8192) });   // ~8 KB > 2 KB cap
+    const r1 = await post('/api/open-project', big);
+    ok(r1.status === 500 || r1.status === 'neterr', `over-cap POST rejected (got ${JSON.stringify(r1)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] an under-cap body is processed normally (server alive, readBody returns)');
+    const r2 = await post('/api/open-project', JSON.stringify({ path: '/no-such-dir-xyz-123' }));
+    ok(r2.status === 400, `under-cap POST reaches the handler (400 invalid path, got ${JSON.stringify(r2)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[C] the server survived the oversized request');
+    const h = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/health`).then((r) => r.status).catch(() => 'down');
+    ok(h === 200, `server still healthy after the DoS attempt (got ${h})`);
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * RECALL (recall.mjs) over what faded out of the conversation (ONE STREAM: stream.mjs streamFadedDocs) - the developer,
+ * 2026-10-24: "I said let's find another family and it started trying to figure out how to build a query instead of

+function exchange(text, result, reply) {
+  const dev = `e${++n}`;

+  events.push({ id: `e${++n}`, type: 'assistant-text', content: reply });
+  return dev;

+// (no tasks since 2026-10-27: an item is labelled by the developer's request it came after)

+  const stb = s.newStreamMarks();

+  const was = s.streamRecallBoost(stb, evs, [oldReply], ['family', 'hmo']);
+  ok(was[oldReply] === 'summary' && !stb.faded[oldReply] && stb.faded[old] === 'faded', `the item kept is taken out of what faded (it was ${was[oldReply]}); one not kept stays faded`);

+  const units = s.streamUnits(evs), ctx = s.streamContext(stb, units);
+  const wb = s.streamWeight(stb, units.find((u) => u.id === oldReply), ctx);

+  ok(stb.faded[oldReply] !== 'faded' && stb.faded[oldReply] !== 'summary' && r.faded + r.summarized > 0, `over the goal, others fade and the one brought back stays in view (${r.faded} faded, ${r.summarized} summarized)`);

+  say('user-message', 'and on');

+/**
+ * THE PAGE LOSES ITS SERVER: A RED "RECONNECTING" LINE, AND IT COMES BACK BY ITSELF (the developer, 2026-11-02: "when the
+ * front-end loses contact w/ the backend, it says so and asks the developer to refresh the frontend, but it's not
+ * obvious how to do that since we are hiding the browser chrome. Instead, I think we should retry and maybe show a red
+ * version of the agent working loading line, but have it say reconnecting"). Through the real page:
+ *   [1] the server stops answering: the working line turns red, its ring turning, says it is reconnecting and for how
+ *       long - and asks for no reload
+ *   [2] a server answers on that port again: the line goes by itself, the conversation is there, a message goes through
+ * Run: node test/test-reconnect-line-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reconn-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const PORT = 29451 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reconn-proj-'));
+await mkdir(path.join(proj, '.code_boss'), { recursive: true });
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Answered.' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+const start = () => startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+let { server } = await start();
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  const errors = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(String(e.message || e)));
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+  await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('input').value = 'first message'; send(); });
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /Answered\./.test(document.getElementById('log').textContent), { timeout: 10000 });
+  await sleep(1200);
+
+  console.log('\n[1] the server stops answering');
+  await new Promise((r) => { server.close(() => r()); try { server.closeAllConnections(); } catch {} });
+  await page.evaluate(() => refetchAndReconnect());   // (what the watchdog does after its silence)
+  await page.waitForSelector('#log .status.conn-lost', { timeout: 20000 }).catch(() => {});
+  await sleep(2500);
+  const lost = await page.evaluate(() => { const r = document.querySelector('#log .status.conn-lost'); const sp = r && r.querySelector('.cb-spin'); return r ? { text: r.textContent, color: getComputedStyle(r).color, spin: sp && getComputedStyle(sp).animationName } : null; });
+  ok(lost && /Reconnecting to the code_boss server/.test(lost.text) && !/reload|refresh/i.test(lost.text), `a "reconnecting" line, asking for no reload (${lost && lost.text.slice(0, 100)})`);
+  ok(lost && lost.color === 'rgb(241, 93, 135)' && lost.spin === 'cb-spin', `red, its ring turning (${lost && lost.color}, ${lost && lost.spin})`);
+  const t1 = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#log .status.conn-lost')?.textContent || '');
+  await sleep(1300);
+  const t2 = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('#log .status.conn-lost')?.textContent || '');
+  ok(/\d+s · /.test(t2) && t1 !== t2, `it says how long it has been trying (${t2.match(/\d+s/)?.[0]})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a server answers there again');
+  ({ server } = await start());
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => !document.querySelector('#log .status.conn-lost'), { timeout: 20000 }).catch(() => {});
+  const back = await page.evaluate(() => ({ lost: !!document.querySelector('#log .status.conn-lost'), had: /first message/.test(document.getElementById('log').textContent) }));
+  ok(!back.lost && back.had, `the line goes by itself, no reload, and the conversation is there (${JSON.stringify(back)})`);
+  await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('input').value = 'after it came back'; send(); });
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => (document.getElementById('log').textContent.match(/Answered\./g) || []).length >= 2, { timeout: 15000 }).catch(() => {});
+  ok(await page.evaluate(() => (document.getElementById('log').textContent.match(/Answered\./g) || []).length >= 2), 'a message goes through after it');
+  ok(!errors.length, `no page errors (${errors.join('; ').slice(0, 200)})`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-release-channel-probe.mjs
new dd1336d4aedad7108acaf734
+/**
+ * test-release-channel-probe.mjs — dev-test vs production release channel. Deterministic, no network.
+ *
+ * The setup tool deploys to a CHANNEL: a bare run → 'devtest' (test it yourself first), --prod →
+ * 'production' (what users get). It force-pushes that channel's moving tag and writes a per-machine
+ * pointer (<home>/.code_boss/channel) the launcher + check-update read to follow that tag — while
+ * the baked UPDATE_TAG stays 'production' so users (who never run setup → no pointer) are unaffected.
+ *
+ * This tests the pure pieces: setupChannel(flags) + effectiveChannelTag(fallback) reading the pointer.
+ *
+ *   node test/test-release-channel-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { setupChannel, effectiveChannelTag } from '../src/deploy-cmd.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (m) => { pass++; console.log('  ok', m); };
+const bad = (m) => { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', m); };
+const eq = (a, b, m) => (a === b ? ok(m) : bad(`${m} (got ${JSON.stringify(a)}, want ${JSON.stringify(b)})`));
+
+async function main() {
+  // ── setupChannel: --prod → production; bare → devtest ────────────────────────
+  eq(setupChannel({}), 'devtest', 'bare run → devtest (test-first default)');
+  eq(setupChannel({ prod: true }), 'production', '--prod → production');
+  eq(setupChannel({ prod: false }), 'devtest', 'prod:false → devtest');
+  eq(setupChannel({ 'no-push': true }), 'devtest', 'other flags don\'t change the default channel');
+
+  // ── effectiveChannelTag: reads <CB_HOME>/.code_boss/channel, else the fallback ──
+  const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-chan-'));
+  const prevHome = process.env.CB_HOME;
+  process.env.CB_HOME = home;   // cbHomeDir() = CB_HOME || homedir()
+  try {
+    const chFile = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'channel');
+    // no pointer → the baked fallback (production for users)
+    eq(await effectiveChannelTag('production'), 'production', 'no channel file → fallback (production — users unaffected)');
+    eq(await effectiveChannelTag('production-X'), 'production-X', 'no channel file → whatever fallback is passed');
+
+    await mkdir(path.dirname(chFile), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(chFile, 'devtest\n', 'utf8');   // setup writes "devtest\n"
+    eq(await effectiveChannelTag('production'), 'devtest', 'channel file "devtest" → devtest (overrides the baked default)');
+
+    await writeFile(chFile, 'production\n', 'utf8');
+    eq(await effectiveChannelTag('production'), 'production', 'channel file "production" → production');
+
+    await writeFile(chFile, '   \n', 'utf8');   // empty/whitespace → ignore, use fallback
+    eq(await effectiveChannelTag('production'), 'production', 'empty channel file → fallback (not blank)');
+
+    await writeFile(chFile, '  devtest  \n', 'utf8');   // trimmed
+    eq(await effectiveChannelTag('production'), 'devtest', 'channel file is trimmed');
+  } finally {
+    if (prevHome === undefined) delete process.env.CB_HOME; else process.env.CB_HOME = prevHome;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { try { await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200)); } }
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/test-reload-midstream.mjs
new e9a50b9c0c53145ec690a9c7
+/**
+ * RELOADING THE PAGE MID-RUN SHOWED A REPLY STARTING MID-WORD.
+ *
+ * Found by driving a live session. Reload while the agent is streaming and the reply rendered as:
+ *
+ *   ● ished). Called in the finally block of withLock, so it runs even if the wrapped function
+ *
+ * Chunk events are ephemeral — the server never persists them — so /api/state carries none of the prose already
+ * streamed. The first chunk to arrive after the reload then started a fresh bubble from wherever the stream
+ * happened to be, and the developer got a sentence fragment beginning in the middle of a word.
+ *
+ * A client that joined late must not build a bubble from chunks. The finalising assistant-text event carries the
+ * WHOLE segment, so waiting for it costs a moment of liveness and gains a complete sentence.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-reload-midstream.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reload-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29307;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+// One long sentence, streamed slowly in small pieces. A reload lands in the middle of a word, which is what
+// makes the fragment so obviously wrong on screen.
+// LONG ENOUGH THAT THE FRAGMENT IS STILL ON SCREEN WHEN WE LOOK. A short sentence finished streaming before
+// the check ran, the finalising event replaced the fragment, and the test passed against the broken code.
+const SENTENCE = ('The lock is released in the finally block of withLock so that it runs even when the wrapped function throws, '
+  + 'and a stale lock older than five hundred milliseconds is cleared by the next writer that finds it there. ').repeat(6);
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() {
+    for (let i = 0; i < SENTENCE.length; i += 6) { await sleep(90); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: SENTENCE.slice(i, i + 6) } }] }; }
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};
+
+// innerText carries the row's leading bullet, so strip it before comparing against the sentence - otherwise
+// every prefix check is off by the bullet and reads as a fragment.
+const shownText = (page) => page.evaluate(() =>
+  [...document.querySelectorAll('#log [class*="assistant"]')]
+    .map((e) => (e.innerText || '').replace(/^[\s●•]+/, '').trim())
+    .filter(Boolean).join(' | '));
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reload-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[1] reload while it is streaming');
+    await page.type('#input', 'explain the concurrency design');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'running', { timeout: 15000 });
+    // Let a good chunk of the sentence stream, so a mid-join is genuinely mid-word.
+    await sleep(3000);
+    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status) === 'running', 'the run is still going after the reload');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] nothing renders as a fragment while the rest streams in');
+    {
+      // Look while the stream is still going - that is the only moment the fragment exists.
+      await sleep(1500);
+      const stillRunning = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'running');
+      ok(stillRunning, 'still streaming at the moment we look (otherwise this proves nothing)');
+      const mid = await shownText(page);
+      // THE bug: a bubble built from chunks that arrived after the reload starts wherever the stream was.
+      const startsMidSentence = mid && !SENTENCE.startsWith(mid.slice(0, 25)) && mid.length > 3;
+      ok(!startsMidSentence, `no fragment on screen mid-stream (${JSON.stringify(mid.slice(0, 60))})`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3] and the finished reply arrives complete');
+    {
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => ['done', 'idle'].includes((window.__sessionRef || {}).status), { timeout: 40000 });
+      await sleep(800);
+      const text = await shownText(page);
+      ok(text.includes('The lock is released'), 'it starts at the beginning of the sentence');
+      ok(text.includes('the next writer that finds it there'), 'and runs to the end');
+      // The whole point: nothing lost, nothing truncated at the front.
+      ok(!/^\s*[a-z]{2,}\)\./.test(text), `and does not begin mid-word (${JSON.stringify(text.slice(0, 40))})`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[4] a run started AFTER the reload streams live as normal');
+    {
+      // The guard must not outlive the stream it was protecting — otherwise every later reply would stop being
+      // live, which would be a worse regression than the fragment.
+      await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+      await page.type('#input', 'again please');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'running', { timeout: 15000 });
+      // (the opening of a reply is held from the chat until 200 characters are in - the "I cannot execute" check, turn-stream.mjs CANT_RUN_PHRASES)
+      await sleep(4500);
+      const streaming = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .row.assistant[data-streaming="true"]').length);
+      ok(streaming > 0, `a fresh run still streams into a live bubble (${streaming})`);
+    }
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close().catch(() => {}); server.close(); await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+
+  console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+

+/**
+ * THE >_ CONSOLE — a second conversation, with the machine instead of the model.
+ *
+ * What it is for: when the token budget is gone the machine can still do the work, and an agent in another window
+ * can write a snippet that this runs, with one click to copy the result back to it. So the checks are about that
+ * loop working end to end, through the real UI:
+ *   A. the toggle swaps surfaces, and what you type runs as a snippet — WITHOUT a model, which is the whole point
+ *      (the test's adapter throws if anything asks it for a completion).
+ *   B. the result is the same text the agent would have been sent: the value AND every call it made.
+ *   C. one click puts exactly that on the clipboard.
+ *   D. the agent's conversation is untouched — a console run is not a turn the model took.
+ *   E. Settings hands over the reference an agent elsewhere needs in order to write one.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-repl-console-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-repl-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29277;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// If the console ever needs the model, this fails the run loudly rather than quietly costing tokens.
+let modelCalls = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { modelCalls++; throw new Error('the >_ console must not call the model'); },
+  async complete() { modelCalls++; throw new Error('the >_ console must not call the model'); },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-repl-proj-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'notes.txt'), 'alpha\nbeta\ngamma\n');
+  // what the console's own examples look at: a README, a TODO to find (no pom: Maven's example must still end, and say how)
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), '# the example project\n\nTODO: write this\n');

=== add test/test-replay-probe.mjs
new 0a1b494010e626d4a62327cd
+/**
+ * SNAPSHOT #78 — replay a call that repeated, and ask the model to mark where its reply ends.
+ *
+ * The decisive question about the repeating replies is whether one HTTP response held ONE generation that
+ * paused (the model thinking, thoughts hidden) or SEVERAL joined together (a layer upstream re-running the
+ * model). The log cannot say; a marker can. This test's endpoint is a mock that behaves each way in turn, and
+ * the thing under test is the VERDICT: it must name the right one, never leak the conversation it replayed,
+ * and keep its own two calls out of the raw log and the transcript.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-replay-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile, rm, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-replay-home-'));
+const { findReplayTarget, withEndMarker, withTail, withContinuation, continuationReading, commonPrefixLength, markerReading, runReplayProbe, formatReplayProbe, resetReplayProbe, replayProbeState } = await import('../src/agent/replay-probe.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29483;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
+
+// A recorded conversation with the developer's data in it, and a reply that repeated.
+const SECRET = 'PATIENT_SECRET_ZQX';
+const PADDED = 'I will write a script to apply all of these updates:' + NL + '1. Rename ' + SECRET + '.' + NL + NL
+  + 'I will update index.html to address all of those requests.' + NL + NL + 'This includes:' + NL + '1. Renaming.' + NL + NL
+  + '<remote-workspace><read path="index.html"/></remote-workspace>';
+const TIGHT = 'Reading the file first.' + NL + '<remote-workspace><read path="index.html"/></remote-workspace>';
+// `signal: {}` is what a recorded request carries: the worker's AbortSignal, flattened by JSON. Production rows all have it.
+// The flat transport ends the prompt on the model's OPEN turn, tagged; the closing-reminder variant needs that tag.
+const TAG = 'k7q2';
+const request = (text, { tagged = true } = {}) => ({ model: 'm', temperature: 0, signal: {}, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'PROMPT ' + SECRET + ' ' + text + (tagged ? NL + NL + '--- you ' + TAG + ' ---' : '') }] });
+const entry = (content, req, extra = {}) => JSON.stringify({
+  id: 'rl_x', type: 'chat_completions', at: Date.now(), durationMs: 41000, model: 'm',
+  request: JSON.stringify(req), response: { choices: [{ message: { role: 'assistant', content }, finish_reason: 'stop' }] }, streamed: true, ...extra,
+});
+
+/** A mock endpoint that, given the marker instruction, answers as ONE generation or as TWO joined. */
+function makeAdapter() {
+  const state = { mode: 'one', seen: [] };
+  const chunk = (s) => ({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: { content: s } }] });
+  return {
+    state,
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+    async *completeStream(req) {
+      state.seen.push(req);
+      const last = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+      const m = /<<END-[a-z0-9]+>>/.exec(last);
+      const marker = m ? m[0] : null;
+      const A = 'I will update the file to rename the button and table the pool.' + NL;
+      const B = 'I\'ll update the file now, renaming the button and converting the pool to a table.' + NL;
+      const BLOCK = '<remote-workspace><read path="index.html"/></remote-workspace>';
+      // The stall-cut continuation: its own cut-off reply, a note, a fresh turn. Continued, or started over.
+      if (/Your previous reply went silent/.test(last)) {
+        if (state.mode === 'cont-restate') { yield chunk(A + B + BLOCK + NL + '/--- you ' + TAG + ' ---'); return; }
+        if (state.mode === 'cont-complete') { yield chunk('/--- you ' + TAG + ' ---'); return; }             // nothing to add: closes at once
+        if (state.mode === 'cont-sorry') { yield chunk('Sorry, my previous reply was cut off. the pool into a table.' + NL + BLOCK + NL + '/--- you ' + TAG + ' ---'); return; }
+        yield chunk('the pool into a table, then expand the note.' + NL + BLOCK + NL + '/--- you ' + TAG + ' ---'); return;
+      }
+      // A closing reminder at the tail: this endpoint answers cleanly - one statement, no stall, closes its turn.
+      if (/close your turn with "\/--- you/.test(last)) { yield chunk(A + BLOCK + NL + '/--- you ' + TAG + ' ---'); return; }
+      // A neutral nudge, or nothing at all: it repeats, with a real stall between the statements.
+      if (!marker) { yield chunk(A); await sleep(1600); yield chunk(B + BLOCK); return; }
+      if (state.mode === 'one-clean') { yield chunk(A + BLOCK + NL + marker); return; }   // one generation, no stall at all
+      if (state.mode === 'one') {            // one generation: a pause, then it carries on, marker once at the end
+        yield chunk(A); await sleep(1600); yield chunk(B + BLOCK + NL + marker); return;
+      }
+      // two generations joined: the first ends with the marker, then a second attempt is appended
+      yield chunk(A + marker); await sleep(1600); yield chunk(NL + B + BLOCK + NL + marker);
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+async function withLog(lines, fn) {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-replay-'));
+  await mkdir(path.join(dir, '.code_boss'), { recursive: true });
+  const file = path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'raw-log.jsonl');
+  await writeFile(file, lines.join(NL) + NL, 'utf8');
+  try { return await fn(file, dir); } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log(NL + '[1] the target is the LAST recorded reply that repeated, with its request intact');
+  await withLog([entry(TIGHT, request('first')), entry(PADDED, request('second')), entry(PADDED, request('third')), entry(TIGHT, request('fourth'))], async (file) => {
+    const t = findReplayTarget(file);
+    ok(!!t && t.intents >= 2, `a repeating reply was chosen (${t && t.intents} intents)`);
+    ok(t && /third/.test(t.request.messages[0].content), 'and it is the last one that repeated, not the first');
+    ok(t && t.request.temperature === 0 && t.request.model === 'm', 'with the request parameters as recorded');
+  });
+  await withLog([entry(PADDED, request('x'), { request: '{"model":"m","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"…[truncated 99 more bytes]' })], async (file) => {
+    ok(findReplayTarget(file) === null, 'a clipped request is not replayable, and is skipped rather than half-sent');
+  });
+  await withLog([entry(TIGHT, request('only clean'))], async (file) => {
+    ok(findReplayTarget(file) === null, 'no repeat, no target');
+    const f = formatReplayProbe({ status: 'done', target: null, runs: [] });
+    ok(/NOTHING TO REPLAY/.test(f.body), 'and the tile says so plainly');
+  });
+
+  console.log(NL + '[2] the marker instruction lands on the last user message and changes nothing else');
+  {
+    const req = { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'a' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'b' }, { role: 'user', content: 'c' }] };
+    const marked = withEndMarker(req, '<<END-abc123>>');
+    ok(marked.messages[2].content.startsWith('c') && marked.messages[2].content.includes('<<END-abc123>>'), 'appended to the last user message');
+    ok(marked.messages[0].content === 'a' && marked.messages[1].content === 'b', 'earlier messages untouched');
+    ok(req.messages[2].content === 'c', 'and the original request object is not mutated');
+  }
+
+  console.log(NL + '[3] reading the marker: once at the end, or one per attempt');
+  {
+    const r1 = markerReading('I will do it.' + NL + 'I\'ll do it now.' + NL + '<<END-x>>', '<<END-x>>');
+    ok(r1.markers === 1 && r1.atEnd && r1.mid === 0, 'one at the end reads as one');
+    const r2 = markerReading('I will do it.<<END-x>>' + NL + 'I will do it again.' + NL + '<<END-x>>', '<<END-x>>');
+    ok(r2.markers === 2 && r2.mid === 1 && r2.restatedAfter === 1, `two, one mid-reply followed by a restatement (${JSON.stringify(r2)})`);
+    const r3 = markerReading('I will do it.<<END-x>> and then more text after it', '<<END-x>>');
+    ok(r3.markers === 1 && !r3.atEnd && r3.trailingChars > 0, 'one marker with text after it is not "at the end"');

+/**
+ * ONE REPLY'S RESULTS, AND THE REQUEST, KEEP TO A SIZE (src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs), after live 2401: one reply ran
+ * about 70 snippets, their results came back as one message of some 700k characters, the request was 272k tokens and
+ * the endpoint refused it - and no fold can shrink a single message.
+ *
+ *   [1] a reply runs at most CB_REPLY_MAX_SNIPPETS snippets; the rest do not run, and the next request says so
+ *   [2] its results stop at CB_REPLY_RESULTS_TOKENS: the one that crosses is cut, the ones after do not run
+ *   [3] with the stream, at the model's limit: this run's oldest step results to one line each, no model
+ *   [4] the last guard: a request over requestMaxTokens has its biggest message cut until it fits
+ * Run: node test/test-reply-limits.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_TURN_CEILING = '12';
+process.env.CB_REPLY_MAX_SNIPPETS = '10';
+process.env.CB_REPLY_RESULTS_TOKENS = '30000';
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+const { estTokens } = await import('../src/agent/compaction.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const textOf = (req) => (req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content || ''))).join('\n');
+
+function adapter(script, requests) {
+  let i = 0;
+  return {
+    async complete(req) {
+      // the worker's own non-turn calls (a fold's summary) are answered, and counted apart
+      if (req && req.purpose === 'summary') return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'SUMMARY' } }] };
+      requests.push(textOf(req));
+      const c = script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: c } }], usage: { total_tokens: 5 } };
+    },
+  };
+}
+async function run(dir, script, extra = {}) {
+  const requests = [], logs = [], events = [];
+  const result = await runMinimalWorker({
+    adapter: adapter(script, requests), model: 'test', signal: { aborted: false },
+    messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'do the thing' }], projectDir: dir,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true,
+    onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: (m) => logs.push(m),
+    ...extra,
+  });
+  return { result, requests, logs, events };
+}
+const withDir = async (fn) => { const d = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-limits-')); try { await fn(d); } finally { await rm(d, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); } };
+const block = (body) => `<remote-workspace><js>${body}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+
+console.log('\n[1] at most ten snippets a reply');
+await withDir(async (dir) => {
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'a.txt'), 'A');
+  const many = Array.from({ length: 14 }, (_, k) => `Step ${k}.\n${block(`return "res-" + (${k} * 1);`)}`).join('\n');
+  const { result, requests, logs } = await run(dir, [many, 'Done.']);
+  ok(result.stats.toolCalls === 10, `ten of the fourteen ran (${result.stats.toolCalls})`);
+  const next = requests[1] || '';
+  ok(/res-9\b/.test(next) && !/res-10\b/.test(next), 'the tenth result is there, no eleventh');
+  ok(/NOT RUN: the last 4 snippet\(s\) of your reply did not run - one reply runs at most 10/.test(next), 'the next request says four did not run, and why');
+  ok(logs.some((l) => /14 tool calls: 10 ran, 4 did not/.test(l)), 'and the log says so');
+});
+
+console.log('\n[2] a reply\'s results stop at their allowance');
+await withDir(async (dir) => {
+  // each under the size a single result goes to a file at (48 KB), as in the live run; together far over
+  const four = [0, 1, 2, 3].map((k) => block(`return "R${k}:" + "y".repeat(45000);`)).join('\nand\n');
+  const { result, requests, events } = await run(dir, [four, 'Done.']);
+  const next = requests[1] || '';

+  ok(/R0:y/.test(next) && /R1:y/.test(next) && /R2:y/.test(next) && !/R3:y/.test(next), 'the first three results are in the next request, not the fourth');
+  ok(/characters of this result are left out here: one reply's results stay under 30000 tokens together/.test(next), 'the one that crossed is cut, and says why');
+  ok(/NOT RUN: the last 1 snippet\(s\)[^\n]*reached 30000 tokens/.test(next), 'the one after did not run, and says why');
+  ok(estTokens(next) < 45000, `the next request is ${estTokens(next)} tokens`);
+  const r1 = events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result')[2];
+  ok(r1 && /left out here/.test(r1.content), 'the chat event the next message is rebuilt from holds the same cut result');

+await withDir(async (dir) => {

+  ok(/\[code_boss: this result \(\d+ characters\) was shortened to this line - the request reached the model's limit of 25000 tokens/.test(last), 'an old result is one line, saying why');
+  ok(/three three three/.test(last), 'the newest result is whole');
+  ok(logs.some((l) => /request guard: this run's oldest step results shortened to one line each/.test(l)) && !logs.some((l) => /^compacted:/.test(l)), 'the log says so, and nothing was folded');
+});
+
+console.log('\n[4] the last guard');
+await withDir(async (dir) => {
+  const history = [{ role: 'user', content: 'BIG ' + 'z'.repeat(200000) }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'ok' }];
+  const { requests, logs } = await run(dir, ['Done.'], { history, compactOn: false, requestMaxTokens: 20000 });
+  const first = requests[0] || '';
+  ok(estTokens(first) <= 20000, `the request is ${estTokens(first)} tokens, under the 20000 limit`);
+  ok(/more than the model takes/.test(first), 'the cut message says why');
+  ok(logs.some((l) => /request guard: message \d+ cut/.test(l)), 'and the log says so');
+});

+/**
+ * WHO MAY CALL code_boss's own server. It listens on this machine only, but any web page in the developer's browser
+ * can make the browser send a request to localhost - and /api/run-snippet runs code. A request is refused unless it
+ * is from this app's own page or from a program on this machine (cbRequestRefusal in src/server.mjs).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-request-origin.mjs
+ */
+import http from 'node:http';
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer, cbRequestRefusal } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const R = (headers, url = '/api/state') => cbRequestRefusal({ headers, url });
+
+console.log('\n[1] the rule');
+ok(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888' }) === '' && R({ host: 'localhost:18988' }) === '' && R({ host: '[::1]:18888' }) === '', 'a program on this machine (no Origin) is let in, by any of localhost\'s names and any port');
+ok(R({ host: 'localhost:18988', origin: 'http://localhost:18988', 'sec-fetch-site': 'same-origin' }) === '', 'the app\'s own page is let in - also through the container\'s bridge, where the port is not the server\'s own');
+ok(/another site/.test(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', origin: 'https://evil.example' })), 'a page from another site is refused (its Origin is not ours)');
+ok(/another site/.test(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', origin: 'http://localhost:3000' })) && /another site/.test(R({ host: 'localhost:18888', origin: 'http://127.0.0.1:18888' })), 'so is another LOCAL page: a different port, or a different name for this machine, is a different origin');
+ok(/another site/.test(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', origin: 'null' })), 'and a sandboxed frame or a file:// page (Origin: null)');
+ok(/only as localhost/.test(R({ host: 'attacker.example:18888' })) && /only as localhost/.test(R({ host: '' })) && /only as localhost/.test(R({ host: '127.0.0.1.evil.example' })), 'a name that merely resolves here (DNS rebinding) is refused, as is no Host at all');
+ok(/sec-fetch-site/.test(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', 'sec-fetch-site': 'cross-site' })) && /sec-fetch-site/.test(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', 'sec-fetch-site': 'same-site' })), 'a cross-site GET of an /api/ address carries no Origin: the browser\'s own Sec-Fetch-Site refuses it');
+ok(R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', 'sec-fetch-site': 'cross-site' }, '/') === '' && R({ host: '127.0.0.1:18888', 'sec-fetch-site': 'none' }) === '', 'the page itself can still be opened from a link, and an address typed by the developer is theirs');
+
+console.log('\n[2] the server');
+{
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-origin-'));
+  process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-origin-home-'));
+  const PORT = 29166;
+  const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'hi' } }] }; } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const call = (method, p, headers, body) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+    const data = body ? JSON.stringify(body) : null;
+    const r = http.request({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: PORT, method, path: p, agent: false, headers: { ...(data ? { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'content-length': Buffer.byteLength(data) } : {}), ...headers } }, (res) => { let t = ''; res.on('data', (d) => { t += d; }); res.on('end', () => resolve({ status: res.statusCode, text: t })); });
+    r.on('error', (e) => resolve({ error: e.message })); r.end(data || undefined);
+  });
+  const mark = path.join(proj, 'ran.txt').replace(/\\/g, '\\\\');
+  const code = `await write("${mark}", "x"); return 1;`;
+  const evil = await call('POST', '/api/run-snippet', { Host: `127.0.0.1:${PORT}`, Origin: 'https://evil.example' }, { code });
+  ok(evil.status === 403 && /refused/.test(evil.text) && !existsSync(path.join(proj, 'ran.txt')), `a snippet posted by another site's page is refused, and nothing ran (${evil.status} ${evil.text.slice(0, 80)})`);
+  const rebind = await call('GET', '/api/state', { Host: 'attacker.example' });
+  ok(rebind.status === 403, `a request naming another host is refused (${rebind.status})`);
+  const cross = await call('GET', '/api/state', { Host: `127.0.0.1:${PORT}`, 'Sec-Fetch-Site': 'cross-site' });
+  ok(cross.status === 403, `a cross-site GET of the state is refused (${cross.status})`);
+  const own = await call('GET', '/api/state', { Host: `127.0.0.1:${PORT}`, Origin: `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`, 'Sec-Fetch-Site': 'same-origin' });
+  const prog = await call('GET', '/api/state', { Host: `127.0.0.1:${PORT}` });
+  ok(own.status === 200 && prog.status === 200, `the app's own page and a local program are answered (${own.status}, ${prog.status})`);
+  const mine = await call('POST', '/api/run-snippet', { Host: `127.0.0.1:${PORT}`, Origin: `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}` }, { code });
+  ok(mine.status === 200 && existsSync(path.join(proj, 'ran.txt')), `and the app's own page can run a snippet (${mine.status})`);
+  try { server.close(); } catch { }
+}

=== add test/test-retry-collapse-replay-e2e.mjs
new fa49fc5cf8780c256d8dad18
+/**
+ * End-to-end (bug review #5): a NON-edit recoverable retry collapses to ONE tool row — LIVE and, critically, ON
+ * REPLAY (reload). A snippet repeated 3× in a block loops on the 3rd (LOOP DETECTED = kind='retry'); the next turn's
+ * snippet retries it (retryOf) and re-keys the SAME box. Before the fix, replay rebuilt it as TWO rows. A snippet's
+ * box is its .js-group (one collapsed line per call), so that is what is counted. Drives the real worker + UI.
+ * Run: node test/test-retry-collapse-replay-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29146;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0; const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return { async *completeStream() { for (const c of next()) { await sleep(4); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next().join('') } }] }; } };
+}
+// One box per snippet call: the .js-group (its summary line + the folded js row, result and step rows). The llm
+// transparency receipts (meta-row) are not tool boxes and were never part of this contract; a snippet's inner
+// step rows are contents of its box, not boxes — so the group, not .row.tool, is the unit counted.
+const countTools = (page) => page.$$eval('.js-group', (els) => els.length);
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-retrep-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'f.txt'), 'alpha', 'utf8');
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'f2.txt'), 'beta', 'utf8');
+  const responses = [
+    // 3 identical snippets in one block → the 3rd LOOP-DETECTs (recoverable, kind='retry')
+    ['<remote-workspace><js>return await read("f.txt");</js><js>return await read("f.txt");</js><js>return await read("f.txt");</js></remote-workspace>'],
+    // next turn: a snippet (same verb) → retryOf re-keys the looped box into ONE row
+    ['<remote-workspace><js>return await read("f2.txt");</js></remote-workspace>'],
+    ['Done reading.'],
+    [''],
+  ];
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] LIVE: the looped snippet + its retry are ONE box → 3 snippet boxes (js×2 ok + 1 collapsed retry), not 4');
+    await page.type('#input', 'read it');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    // status is already 'idle' before the send, so waiting for done-or-idle returned AT ONCE and the count was
+    // taken against an empty transcript (got 0) - this suite's flake. Wait for the boxes themselves: the run has
+    // produced its snippet boxes when there are at least three, and a fourth (the bug) still fails the assertion.
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('.js-group').length >= 3, { timeout: 25000 });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle', { timeout: 20000 });
+    await sleep(500);
+    const liveCount = await countTools(page);
+    ok(liveCount === 3, `live: 3 snippet boxes (the retry collapsed into the looped box), not 4 (got ${liveCount})`);
+    // the collapse must be a real one: the 3rd call LOOP-DETECTed (kind retry) and the 4th re-keyed it (retryOf)
+    const ev = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json().then((s) => s.events || []).catch(() => []);
+    const looped = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.kind === 'retry' && /LOOP DETECTED/.test(String(e.content || '')));
+    const rekey = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'js' && e.retryOf);
+    ok(looped.length === 1 && rekey.length === 1 && rekey[0].retryOf === looped[0].callId, `the 3rd snippet loop-detected and the 4th re-keyed THAT box (looped=${looped.length}, retryOf=${rekey.length})`);
+    ok(ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'js').length === 4, 'four snippet calls were made in all (3 + the retry)');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] REPLAY: after a reload the collapse holds — still 3, not 4 (bug #5 was 4 on replay)');
+    await page.reload();
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(700);
+    const replayCount = await countTools(page);
+    ok(replayCount === 3, `after reload: still 3 snippet boxes — the retry did NOT split into two on replay (got ${replayCount})`);
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 3))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+

+/**
+ * REAL VALUES ON THE DEVELOPER'S SCREEN, ON BY DEFAULT — the model still only sees tokens (build 1981).
+ *
+ * The masking is between the app and the MODEL. The chat used to show the same tokens the model saw ([PII:SSN#…] from
+ * piiscan, @@@(…) from pii-testdata), and the developer, who has every right to the data, could not read their own
+ * conversation. Each token on screen is now replaced by the value it stands for, unless Settings -> "Real values for
+ * masked tokens" is switched off. On by default (the developer's call, 2026-09-26).
+ *
+ *   [1] the default: both kinds of token are replaced by their values, underlined, the token in the tooltip - live,
+ *       and after the fact; nothing token-shaped is left visible in the chat; Settings (first tab) says so
+ *   [2] display only: no request to the model, no event, and nothing on disk ever holds a real value
+ *   [3] switched off (Settings, first tab - there is no footer chip any more): every token is back, no real value stays
+ *       in the page, Settings says Off, the server refuses
+ *   [4] the off setting survives a reload; switched on again, the values return
+ *   [5] the server translates only whole tokens
+ *   [6] the audit surfaces outside the chat (the LLM activity tab) keep the tokens
+ * Run: node test/test-reveal-masked-e2e.mjs
+ */

+const rawIn = (s) => RAW.some((v) => String(s).includes(v));
