+const reqs = [];
+const tokenRe = { adhoc: /#@(?:last_name|ssn)(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/g, piiscan: /#000-00-0000#[0-9a-z]{7}/g };   // (one number, one token: 2026-10-14)   // the shipped filter (piishape): three runs of digits, each its own token
+function reply(t) {
+  const ms = [...t.matchAll(/\b(zebra|walrus|okapi)\b/g)]; const q = ms.length ? ms[ms.length - 1][1] : '';
+  const after = t.slice(ms.length ? ms[ms.length - 1].index : 0);
+  if (q === 'zebra') {
+    if (!/ROWS /.test(after)) return 'Querying. <remote-workspace><js>return await sql_query({ sql: "SELECT * FROM enrollees" });</js></remote-workspace>';
+    const toks = after.match(tokenRe.adhoc) || [];
+    return `The row is ${toks.join(' and ')}. zebra_DONE\n${C}`;
+  }
+  if (q === 'walrus') {
+    if (!/SSN:/.test(after)) return 'Reading. <remote-workspace><js>return await read("people.txt");</js></remote-workspace>';
+    const toks = after.match(tokenRe.piiscan) || [];
+    return `people.txt holds ${toks.join(', ')}. walrus_DONE\n${C}`;
+  }
+  if (q === 'okapi') {   // an edit beside the SSN: the diff's context line carries its token
+    if (!/edited-ok/.test(after)) return 'Editing. <remote-workspace><js>await edit("people.txt", "Status: active", "Status: retired"); return "edited-ok";</js></remote-workspace>';
+    return `Done. okapi_DONE\n${C}`;
+  }
+  return 'ready\n' + C;
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const text = (req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n');
+    reqs.push(text);
+    const body = reply(text);
+    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i += 40) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body.slice(i, i + 40) } }] }; await sleep(3); }
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },

+// THREE MODEL CHOICES, through the REAL server (the developer, 2026-10-25: "let's have separate model selection for main,
+// bookkeeper and heads up/code boss agents"):
+//   1. Settings takes a bookkeeper model and a side agents' model ('' = the main one), and remembers them
+//   2. the agent runs on the main model; the bookkeeper's pass (ONE STREAM: stream-pass.mjs) on the bookkeeper's
+//   3. back to '': everything on the main model
+// Run: node test/test-role-models-server.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-models-home-'));
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';   // (the session transport is standard since 2026-10-28; this test's model answers whole requests)
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const MAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview', BK = 'gemini-3.8-flash', SIDE = 'gemini-2.5-pro';
+
+const seen = [];   // [kind, model]
+const script = (msgs, { model }) => {
+  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content;
+  const all = msgs.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  if (/Bring the record up to date for the new items/.test(all)) { seen.push(['pass', model]); return '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' + close; }
+  seen.push(['agent', model]);
+  return 'Noted. <no-hand-over/>' + close;
+};
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-models-proj-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'notes.txt'), 'notes\n');
+const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+const PORT = 29501, BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 1 });
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><body>x</body>', baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: MAIN, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: MAIN }, { id: BK }, { id: SIDE }], initialProject: proj, apiFormat: 'openai' });
+const post = async (p, body) => { const r = await fetch(BASE + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return { status: r.status, json: await r.json().catch(() => null) }; };
+const state = async () => (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=500')).json();
+const runOnce = async (text) => {
+  const before = seen.length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text, model: MAIN });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { await sleep(100); const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running' && seen.slice(before).some((x) => x[0] === 'pass')) break; }
+  await sleep(300);
+  return seen.slice(before);
+};
+
+try {
+  // ── 1 ──
+  ok((await post('/api/tuning', { bookkeeperModel: 'no-such-model' })).status === 400, '1: a model the endpoint does not offer is refused');
+  ok((await post('/api/tuning', { bookkeeperModel: BK, sideModel: SIDE, sideChatModel: BK })).status === 200, '1: the bookkeeper\'s, Heads up\'s and the side chat\'s models are taken');
+  const s1 = await state();
+  ok(s1.bookkeeperModel === BK && s1.sideModel === SIDE && s1.sideChatModel === BK, `1: the page is told them, the side chat's apart from Heads up's (${s1.bookkeeperModel}, ${s1.sideModel}, ${s1.sideChatModel})`);
+  const prefs = JSON.parse(await (await import('node:fs/promises')).readFile(path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'prefs.json'), 'utf8'));
+  ok(prefs.bookkeeperModel === BK && prefs.sideModel === SIDE && prefs.sideChatModel === BK, '1: and remembers them');
+  // ── 2 ──
+  const r2 = await runOnce('Please note this.');
+  ok(r2.filter((x) => x[0] === 'agent').every((x) => x[1] === MAIN) && r2.some((x) => x[0] === 'agent'), `2: the agent on the main model (${JSON.stringify(r2)})`);
+  ok(r2.some((x) => x[0] === 'pass') && r2.filter((x) => x[0] === 'pass').every((x) => x[1] === BK), '2: the bookkeeper\'s pass on the bookkeeper\'s model');
+  // ── 3 ──
+  await post('/api/tuning', { bookkeeperModel: '', sideModel: '', sideChatModel: '' });
+  const r3 = await runOnce('And this.');
+  ok(r3.length && r3.every((x) => x[1] === MAIN), `3: back to '': everything on the main model (${JSON.stringify(r3)})`);
+} finally {
+  try { server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {}

+// The one button's plan. There were five probe buttons and two panels of checkboxes, and the honest result was the
+// developer asking which one to press — so what runs is decided here, per release, and they press one thing.

+

+// runner, not the spec.

+const step = (id, impl) => ({ id, what: `the ${id} step`, why: `because of ${id}`, est: '~1 min', run: impl });
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[1] the plan is the decision, and it is readable\n');

+    const realRun = await import('../src/agent/prod-spec.mjs');
+    // Drive the real step against an adapter that answers nothing, and read which rungs ran.
+    const out = await RUN_PLAN[0].run({ adapter: { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', complete: async () => ({ choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }) }, model: 'm' });
+    asked = out.run.results.map((r) => r.id);
+    ok(asked.length === realRun.buildProdSpec(realRun.buildSpecContext()).filter((a) => a.layer === 9).length && asked.every((id) => id.startsWith('9')), `the step runs layer 9 and nothing else (${asked.join(',')})`);
+  }
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[2] steps run in order and the report is one document\n');
+{
+  const order = [];
+  RUN_PLAN.push(
+    step('first', async () => { order.push('first'); return { report: 'FIRST OUTPUT' }; }),
+    step('second', async () => { order.push('second'); return { report: 'SECOND OUTPUT' }; }),
+  );
+  const seen = [];
+  const plan = await runPlan({ adapter: { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', complete: async () => ({}) }, model: 'm', onStep: ({ steps }) => seen.push(steps.length) });
+  ok(order.join(',') === 'first,second', 'in the order the plan lists them');
+  ok(plan.steps.length === 2 && plan.steps[0].id === 'first', 'and every step is recorded');
+  const rep = formatPlanReport(plan);
+  ok(/RUN PLAN m via x\.test/.test(rep), 'the report names the model and endpoint');
+  ok(/1\. the first step/.test(rep) && /2\. the second step/.test(rep), 'with a contents page, so a long report can be navigated');
+  ok(/FIRST OUTPUT/.test(rep) && /SECOND OUTPUT/.test(rep), 'and each step\'s own output underneath');
+  ok(/because of first/.test(rep), 'each section carries its WHY, for whoever reads it later and wonders why this and not something else');
+  ok(seen.length >= 2, 'and it publishes after each step, so a three-hour run is readable while it goes');
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[3] one step failing does not cost the others\n');
+{
+  RUN_PLAN.push(
+    step('boom', async () => { throw new Error('HTTP 429 rate limited'); }),
+    step('after', async () => ({ report: 'RAN ANYWAY' })),
+  );
+  const plan = await runPlan({ adapter: { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', complete: async () => ({}) }, model: 'm' });
+  // A rate limit three hours into a run must not throw away everything that already ran.
+  ok(plan.steps.length === 2, 'the run continues past a failed step');
+  ok(/HTTP 429/.test(plan.steps[0].error), 'with the error kept on the step it happened to');
+  ok(plan.steps[1].report === 'RAN ANYWAY', 'and the later step still produced its output');
+  const rep = formatPlanReport(plan);
+  ok(/!! HTTP 429/.test(rep), 'the contents page flags which step failed');
+  ok(/this step failed/.test(rep) && /RAN ANYWAY/.test(rep), 'and the body says so where the output would have been');
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[4] a partial report says what is still coming\n');
+{
+  RUN_PLAN.push(step('done', async () => ({ report: 'X' })), step('later', async () => ({ report: 'Y' })));
+  const partial = formatPlanReport({ model: 'm', steps: [{ id: 'done', what: 'the done step', report: 'X', ms: 1000 }] });
+  ok(/1\/2 step\(s\) RUNNING/.test(partial), 'a half-finished plan says so rather than looking complete');
+  ok(/the later step  — not started/.test(partial), 'and names what has not started, with its estimate');
+  ok(/~1 min/.test(partial), 'so a report caught mid-run cannot be mistaken for the whole answer');
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[5] progress says which STEP, not just how far through one\n');
+{
+  const labels = [];
+  RUN_PLAN.push(
+    step('alpha', async ({ onProgress }) => { onProgress?.({ done: 3, total: 9, label: 'rung 3' }); return { report: '' }; }),
+    step('beta', async ({ onProgress }) => { onProgress?.({ done: 1, total: 2 }); return { report: '' }; }),
+  );
+  await runPlan({ adapter: { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', complete: async () => ({}) }, model: 'm', onProgress: (p) => labels.push(p.label) });
+  ok(/^\[1\/2\] the alpha step · rung 3$/.test(labels[0]), `a step's own progress is nested under its position in the plan (${labels[0]})`);
+  ok(/^\[2\/2\] the beta step$/.test(labels[1]), 'and a step with no label of its own still says where it is');
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[6] cancel stops it\n');
+{
+  let ran = 0;
+  RUN_PLAN.push(step('one', async () => { ran++; return { report: '' }; }), step('two', async () => { ran++; return { report: '' }; }));
+  const ac = new AbortController(); ac.abort();
+  const plan = await runPlan({ adapter: { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', complete: async () => ({}) }, model: 'm', signal: ac.signal });
+  ok(ran === 0 && plan.steps.length === 0, 'an already-cancelled plan runs nothing');
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+  RUN_PLAN.push(...original);
+  ok(RUN_PLAN.length === original.length, 'and the real plan is restored for anyone importing after this file');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

new dad3eccd733b8ac00eb6ab92
+/**
+ * A CALL THAT RETURNED IS NOT "DID NOT FINISH" IN THE RUN RECORD (prod's dev check, 2026-11-02: "[1] read(.code_boss/logs/
+ * call_….log) ... → did not finish (the snippet stopped first)" of calls that had completed and handed their value to
+ * the snippet). How a call ended was written into the record after the masking of its label, arguments and diff - which
+ * takes its time on the box - so a snippet that returned right after its last call had its record written first.
+ * Through the real worker, with a filter that takes its time:
+ *   [1] every call the snippet made and got back from reads as ended, not "did not finish"
+ * Run: node test/test-run-record-finished.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rrfin-home-'));
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+const { createFilterPipeline } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rrfin-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'a.txt'), 'alpha\n');
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'b.txt'), 'beta\n');
+
+// a filter that masks nothing, slowly - as piishape does a big label or diff on the box
+const slow = { name: 'slow', mask: async (s) => { await sleep(300); return s; }, unmask: async (s) => s };
+const filter = createFilterPipeline(() => [slow]);
+const replies = [
+  '<remote-workspace><js>const a = await read("a.txt"); console.log("got", a.length); const b = await read("b.txt"); throw new Error("stop here, after both reads");</js></remote-workspace>',
+  'Done.\n' + C,
+];
+let i = 0; const sent = [];
+const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { sent.push(JSON.stringify(req.messages)); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };
+const events = [];
+await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: dir,
+  requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {}, contentFilter: filter });
+
+console.log('\n[1] the calls that returned read as ended');
+const r = events.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval');
+const text = String(r && r.content || '');
+const rec = text.slice(text.indexOf('What the snippet did'));
+ok(/\[1\] read\(a\.txt\)/.test(rec) && /\[2\] read\(b\.txt\)/.test(rec), `the record lists both reads (${rec.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 220)})`);
+ok(!/did not finish/.test(rec), 'neither is "did not finish (the snippet stopped first)" - both returned before the snippet threw');
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-run-stdin.mjs
new 6aee2670d67a772d00b1d341
+/**
+ * STDIN for a process: a snippet's process runners take `{ stdin }` and pipe it to the child's STDIN with NO
+ * shell escaping (quotes, $, backticks, newlines all literal) — the way `git commit -F -` / `git apply` want it —
+ * and ALWAYS close the pipe, so a command that ignores stdin, or gets none, never hangs.
+ *
+ * Covers real piping through the live path (a <js> snippet → node([...], { stdin })), including parse → execute
+ * end-to-end. (The <run><stdin> tag grammar this once also covered went with the tag verbs.)
+ * Run: node test/test-run-stdin.mjs   (Windows/PowerShell)
+ */
+import { parseToolCalls } from '../src/agent/tool-parser.mjs';
+import { execute, extractContent } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stdin-'));
+const ctx = { cwd: dir, projectDir: dir, onLog: () => {} };
+const js = (code) => execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, ctx).then(extractContent).catch((e) => 'THREW: ' + e.message);
+// a node one-liner that echoes its whole stdin (then a marker, so "EOF reached" is observable too)
+const ECHO = "process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8'); let s=''; process.stdin.on('data', d => s += d); process.stdin.on('end', () => process.stdout.write(s + '<EOF>'));";
+const echoStdin = (payload) => `const r = await node(["-e", ${JSON.stringify(ECHO)}], { stdin: ${JSON.stringify(payload)} }); return r.stdout;`;
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] stdin reaches the command verbatim (quotes/$/backtick, no escaping)');
+  {
+    const payload = 'Fix: "quoted" and $var and `backtick` and newline\nsecond line';
+    const out = await js(echoStdin(payload));
+    ok(out.includes('"quoted" and $var and `backtick`'), `special chars survived verbatim (got: ${out.slice(0, 160)})`);
+    ok(out.includes('second line'), 'multi-line stdin delivered');
+    ok(out.startsWith(payload), 'the payload arrives exactly as given (no added escaping, nothing dropped)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] end-to-end: parse the reply, then execute the parsed call');
+  {
+    const reply = `<remote-workspace><js>\n${echoStdin('STDIN_MARKER_9137\n')}\n</js></remote-workspace>`;
+    const c = parseToolCalls(reply).find((x) => x.name === 'js_eval');
+    ok(!!c && c.verb === 'js', `the reply parses to one js_eval call (got: ${c ? c.verb : 'none'})`);
+    const r = c ? extractContent(await execute(c.name, c.args, {}, ctx)) : '';
+    ok(r.includes('STDIN_MARKER_9137'), 'the parsed stdin option was piped to the command');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a command that IGNORES stdin still runs (stdin is closed — no hang)');
+  {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const out = await js('const r = await node(["-e", "process.stdout.write(\'ignores-stdin\')"], { stdin: "unused payload" }); return r.stdout;');
+    ok(/ignores-stdin/.test(out), 'command ran normally despite an unread stdin');
+    ok(Date.now() - t0 < 10000, `did not hang waiting on the pipe (${Date.now() - t0}ms)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] no stdin at all: a stdin-reading command gets EOF immediately (no hang)');
+  {
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const out = await js(`const r = await node(["-e", ${JSON.stringify(ECHO)}]); return r.stdout;`);
+    ok(/<EOF>/.test(out), 'the reader saw EOF (stdin closed even with no payload)');
+    ok(Date.now() - t0 < 10000, `did not hang (${Date.now() - t0}ms)`);
+  }
+} finally {
+  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { try { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200)); } }
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+

+/**

+const c = pcContainerHost(process.env);
+const [PAGE, CHROME_PORT] = [process.argv[2], Number(process.argv[3])];
+const out = {};
+const step = async (name, fn) => { const t0 = Date.now(); console.log('STEP ' + name); try { out[name] = await Promise.race([fn(), new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error('no answer in 60 s')), 60000))]); } catch (e) { out[name] = { error: String(e && e.stack || e).slice(0, 400) }; } console.log('STEP ' + name + ' ' + (Date.now() - t0) + ' ms ' + JSON.stringify(out[name]).slice(0, 300)); };
+const b = await c.browser.open({ url: PAGE, label: 't' });

+/**
+ * THE `sandbox` COMMAND, END TO END - a local bare repository standing in for GitLab, an image with the REAL compiled
+ * launcher in it, and the command run the way a developer runs it.
+ *   [1] the first run pulls the image into the agent folder and starts the manager from the launcher IN the image
+ *   [2] a second run starts nothing: the manager already running is found and its page is offered again
+ *   [3] a newer image: the running manager is asked to quit, the image is checked out, a new manager starts on it
+ *   [4] --no-pull starts with what is there; the folder is remembered; the channel follows the app's
+ *   [5] what is missing is said, and nothing is started: no repository, no image on the channel, no launcher
+ *   [6] --uninstall: asks first, only touches a container's folder, leaves the folder free for a fresh install
+ *   [7] the Desktop shortcuts (made in [1]-[3], used in [4b], removed in [6]) beside the app's own and another folder's
+ * The manager runs on javaw.exe, with no console ([1]); --kill ends it and starts nothing, as the "Kill Container"
+ * shortcut does when it is opened through Windows' shell ([4b]). The Desktop is a folder of this test's own.
+ * Needs a JDK and git. No tray, no browser. Run: node test/test-sandbox-cmd-e2e.mjs
+ */

+const env = { ...process.env, CB_HOME: HOME, CB_SANDBOX_JAVA: jdk.java, USERPROFILE: path.join(root, 'profile') };   // the test image carries no JDK: use this box's
+delete env.CB_SANDBOX_TREE; delete env.CB_UPDATE_SOURCE; delete env.CB_GITLAB_TOKEN; delete env.CB_GITLAB_USER;
+await mkdir(env.USERPROFILE, { recursive: true });
+// THE DESKTOP the shortcuts go on is a folder of this test's own - never this machine's real one
+const DESK = path.join(root, 'Desktop');
+env.CB_SANDBOX_DESKTOP = DESK;
+await mkdir(DESK, { recursive: true });
+// what the shortcuts run: this repo's own runner, as the setup carrier's extracted source has it
+const SELF = [process.execPath, fileURLToPath(new URL('../src/agent/sandbox-cli.mjs', import.meta.url))];
+const sandbox = async (args) => { const lines = []; const r = await runSandbox([...args, '--yes', '--no-browser', '--no-tray'], { log: (s) => lines.push(s), env, home: HOME, self: SELF }); return { ...r, text: lines.join('\n') }; };
+const psJson = (ps) => { const r = spawnSync('powershell', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', ps], { encoding: 'utf8', windowsHide: true, timeout: 60000 }); try { return JSON.parse(String(r.stdout || '').trim()); } catch { return null; } };
+// a .lnk read back the way it was written: the WScript.Shell object (a test only)
+const readLnk = (f) => psJson(`$s=(New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell).CreateShortcut('${f.replace(/'/g, "''")}'); [pscustomobject]@{target=$s.TargetPath;args=$s.Arguments;dir=$s.WorkingDirectory;icon=$s.IconLocation;style=$s.WindowStyle;desc=$s.Description} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress`);
+// a process: its program, whether it has a console (a conhost.exe of its own), and whether it shows a window
+const procInfo = (pid) => psJson(`$p=Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter 'ProcessId=${Number(pid)}'; $c=@(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter 'ParentProcessId=${Number(pid)}' | Where-Object { $_.Name -eq 'conhost.exe' }).Count; $h=0; try { $h=[int64](Get-Process -Id ${Number(pid)} -ErrorAction Stop).MainWindowHandle } catch {}; [pscustomobject]@{name=[string]$p.Name;conhost=$c;window=$h} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress`);
+const lnk = (name) => path.join(DESK, name + '.lnk');
+const ask = (url, method, p) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+  const u = new URL(url), token = u.searchParams.get('t');
+  const r = http.request({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: u.port, method, path: p, timeout: 3000, headers: { Host: u.host, 'X-CB-Token': token, 'Content-Length': 0 } }, (res) => { let t = ''; res.on('data', (d) => { t += d; }); res.on('end', () => { try { resolve(JSON.parse(t)); } catch { resolve(null); } }); });
+  r.on('error', () => resolve(null)); r.on('timeout', () => { r.destroy(); resolve(null); }); r.end();
+});
+const urls = [];
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] the first run');
+  const r1 = await sandbox(['--tree', TREE, '--source', REMOTE]);
+  urls.push(r1.url);

+  ok(existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'tools', 'launcher', 'classes', 'cb', 'sandbox', 'Manager.class')) && !existsSync(path.join(TREE, '.git')) && !existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'agent')), 'the image is the agent folder\'s root, with no .git in it');

+    `"Code Boss": node ${lk.cb && lk.cb.args}, minimised, in .code_boss, with code_boss's icon (${lk.cb && lk.cb.icon})`);
+  ok(lk.start && lk.start.target.toLowerCase() === process.execPath.toLowerCase() && lk.start.args === ARGS('--no-pull --start-container') && lk.start.icon.toLowerCase() === (path.join(ICONS, 'container.ico') + ',0').toLowerCase() && lk.start.style === 7, `"Start Container": node ${lk.start && lk.start.args}, with the GRAY star (the container's pages carry it)`);
+  ok(lk.kill && lk.kill.target.toLowerCase() === process.execPath.toLowerCase() && lk.kill.args === ARGS('--kill') && lk.kill.icon.toLowerCase() === (path.join(ICONS, 'kill-container.ico') + ',0').toLowerCase() && lk.kill.style === 7, `"Kill Container": node ${lk.kill && lk.kill.args}, with the stop icon`);

+  console.log('\n[4] --no-pull, and the channel');
+  await ask(r3.url, 'POST', '/api/quit');
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && (await ask(r3.url, 'GET', '/api/status')); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+  ok((await sbcRunning(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-manager.json'), TREE)) === null, 'after Quit, no manager is found (its state file is gone or stale)');
+  const r4 = await sandbox(['--no-pull']);
+  urls.push(r4.url);
+  ok(r4.code === 0 && r4.started === true && /using the image already here \(build 2057\)/.test(r4.text), '--no-pull starts with the image already on the box');
+  // quit, and wait until its PROCESS is gone: it runs from files this test deletes further down

+  await ask(r4.url, 'POST', '/api/quit');
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && (await ask(r4.url, 'GET', '/api/status')); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+  for (let i = 0; i < 100 && sbcAlive(pid4); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+  ok(pid4 > 0 && !sbcAlive(pid4), `a manager says which process it is (${pid4}), so "it has quit" can be checked: that process is gone`);
+  const r4b = await sandbox(['--channel', 'devtest']);
+  ok(r4b.code === 2 && /could not get the image at 'sandbox-devtest'/.test(r4b.text) && /no image has been published to that channel yet/.test(r4b.text) && !/--prod/.test(r4b.text), `a channel with no image says so, and how to make one: ${r4b.text.split('\n').slice(-1)[0].trim().slice(0, 80)}`);
+  ok(sbcJava(TREE, {}) === '' && sbcJava(TREE, env) === jdk.java, 'the java is the image\'s own (none in this test image, so the override is what ran)');

+  console.log('\n[4b] one manager per folder; and --kill-existing');
+  {

+    const pidA = ((await ask(first.url, 'GET', '/api/status')) || {}).pid;
+    // what happened on the production box: the command lost track of a manager that was still running (there, it
+    // was slow to answer; here, its record is taken away) - and started a second one beside it
+    await rm(STATE, { force: true });
+    const second = await sandbox(['--no-pull']);
+    ok(first.code === 0 && pidA > 0 && second.code !== 0 && /a manager is already running for this folder, but it is not answering/.test(second.text) && /--kill-existing/.test(second.text) && sbcAlive(pidA),
+      `a second manager for the same folder does NOT come up beside the first - the command says one is there, and how to end it: ${second.text.split('\n').pop().trim().slice(0, 130)}`);
+    const third = await sandbox(['--no-pull', '--kill-existing']);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 50 && sbcAlive(pidA); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    const pidB = third.url ? ((await ask(third.url, 'GET', '/api/status')) || {}).pid : 0;
+    ok(third.code === 0 && /--kill-existing ended 1 manager that was running for/.test(third.text) && !sbcAlive(pidA) && pidB > 0 && pidB !== pidA, `--kill-existing: the one that was there is ended (process ${pidA}) and a fresh one is running (${pidB})`);
+    const none = await sandbox(['--no-pull', '--kill-existing']);
+    const pidC = none.url ? ((await ask(none.url, 'GET', '/api/status')) || {}).pid : 0;
+    ok(none.code === 0 && /--kill-existing ended 1 manager/.test(none.text) && !sbcAlive(pidB) && pidC > 0, 'given again, it ends that one too and starts another: it always means "start clean"');
+    await ask(none.url, 'POST', '/api/quit');
+    for (let i = 0; i < 100 && sbcAlive(pidC); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    const quiet = await sandbox(['--no-pull', '--kill-existing']);

+    // --kill ("Kill Container"): the same ending, and NOTHING is started afterwards
+    const k1 = await sandbox(['--kill']);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 50 && sbcAlive(pidD); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    ok(k1.code === 0 && k1.killed === 1 && /--kill - asking the running manager to quit/.test(k1.text) && /--kill ended 1 manager that was running for/.test(k1.text) && !sbcAlive(pidD) && !k1.url && (await sbcRunning(STATE, TREE)) === null && !existsSync(STATE),
+      `--kill: the manager is asked to quit, then whatever is left is ended by PID - and no new one is started (${pidD} gone, nothing running)`);
+    const k2 = await sandbox(['--kill']);
+    ok(k2.code === 0 && /--kill found no manager running for/.test(k2.text) && (await sbcRunning(STATE, TREE)) === null, 'with nothing running, --kill says so, starts nothing, and is not an error');
+    const kBad = await sandbox(['--kill', '--open-window']);
+    ok(kBad.code === 2 && /--kill stops the container and starts nothing/.test(kBad.text), '--kill with a switch that starts something is refused, nothing done');
+    // THE "Kill Container" SHORTCUT ITSELF, opened the way a double-click opens it (Windows' shell, on the .lnk)
+    const dc = await sandbox(['--no-pull']);

+    spawnSync('powershell', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', `Start-Process -FilePath '${lnk('Kill Container').replace(/'/g, "''")}'`], { env, windowsHide: true, timeout: 60000 });
+    for (let i = 0; i < 300 && sbcAlive(pidE); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+    ok(pidE > 0 && !sbcAlive(pidE) && (await sbcRunning(STATE, TREE)) === null, `the "Kill Container" shortcut, opened from the Desktop folder, ends the running manager (${pidE}) and starts none`);
+    // --open-window ("Code Boss") on an image with no code_boss in it: said, and the container is NOT started for it

+    ok(ow.code === 2 && ow.url && /this image has no code_boss in it/.test(ow.text) && owS && owS.container.state === 'stopped', `--open-window with no code_boss in the image: said, the manager is up, the container left alone (${owS && owS.container.state})`);
+    const pidF = owS && owS.pid;
+    await ask(ow.url, 'POST', '/api/quit');
+    for (let i = 0; i < 100 && sbcAlive(pidF); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] what is missing is said');

+  console.log('\n[6] --uninstall');
+  const un = async (args, confirm) => { const out = []; const r = await runSandbox([...args, '--no-tray'], { log: (x) => out.push(x), env, home: HOME, confirm }); return { ...r, text: out.join('\n') }; };
+  const settings = async () => JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox.json'), 'utf8'));
+  // (a) a folder that is not a container's - a mistyped --tree - is not emptied, and is not remembered either

+  ok(existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'tools', 'launcher', 'classes', 'cb', 'sandbox', 'Manager.class')) && !!(await sbcRunning(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-manager.json'), TREE)), '(installed again, a manager is running)');
+  let asked = '';
+  const ub = await un(['--uninstall'], async (q) => { asked = q; return false; });

+  ok((await sbcRunning(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-manager.json'), TREE)) === null && !('tree' in (await settings())) && (await settings()).source === REMOTE, 'no manager is left, the folder is forgotten as "where the container is", the repository is still known');
+  ok(sbcFindShortcuts(DESK, TREE).length === 0 && !existsSync(lnk('Code Boss')) && !existsSync(lnk('Kill Container')) && /the Desktop shortcuts are removed \(Code Boss, Start Container, Kill Container\)/.test(uc.text) && !existsSync(ICONS), 'and the Desktop shortcuts are gone, with their icons');
+  // (d) the same folder takes a fresh install - and an uninstall with NO manager running starts one for its half
+  const again = await sandbox(['--tree', TREE]);
+  urls.push(again.url);
+  ok(again.code === 0 && existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'image.json')) && !existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'projects', 'work')), 'the folder is reused: a fresh install goes into it');
+  await ask(again.url, 'POST', '/api/quit');
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && (await ask(again.url, 'GET', '/api/status')); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
+  const udNo = await un(['--uninstall', '--yes']);
+  ok(udNo.code === 2 && /--uninstall --yes deletes without asking, so it has to be told which folder/.test(udNo.text) && existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'image.json')), '--uninstall --yes with no --tree is refused: unasked deletion has to NAME its folder, never the remembered one');

+  ok(udBad.code === 2 && /-yes is not a switch of this command \(did you mean --yes\?\)/.test(udBad.text) && existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'image.json')), 'a word that is not a switch (-yes) stops the command, with the switch it probably meant');
+  const ud = await un(['--uninstall', '--yes', '--keep-download', '--tree', TREE]);
+  ok(existsSync(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-git', 'HEAD')) && /downloaded image in your profile is KEPT/.test(ud.text), '--keep-download: the downloaded image stays in the profile, for the reinstall that follows (what `image --uninstall` asks for)');
+  ok(ud.code === 0 && !/asking the running manager/.test(ud.text) && /stopping the container and removing it from Windows/.test(ud.text) && !existsSync(TREE) && (await sbcRunning(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-manager.json'), TREE)) === null, 'with no manager running, one is started for its half, and quits; --yes asks nothing');
+  ok(/deleting the files in .* it says how far it is as it goes/.test(ud.text) && /sandbox: [\d,]+ files deleted in [\d.]+ s/.test(ud.text), `the delete - the long part - is announced, and says what it did: ${(ud.text.match(/sandbox: [\d,]+ files deleted.*/) || [''])[0]}`);
+  // an uninstall that was INTERRUPTED: the launcher and image.json are gone, big folders are not. Run again, it finishes.
+  const HALF = path.join(root, 'half gone');
+  await mk(path.join(HALF, '.cb-uninstalling'), 'started');
+  await mk(path.join(HALF, 'tools', 'node', 'node_modules', 'npm', 'x.js'), 'left');
+  await mk(path.join(HALF, 'projects', 'work', 'Main.java'), 'left');
+  const uh = await un(['--uninstall', '--yes', '--tree', HALF]);
+  ok(uh.code === 0 && !existsSync(HALF) && /the container's Windows profile could not be removed/.test(uh.text) && /2 files deleted/.test(uh.text), 'a folder left half-deleted by an interrupted uninstall is still known as the container\'s (its marker): the same command finishes it');
+  // THE UNINSTALL'S OWN PAGE (what the manager's Uninstall button moves its window to): how far it is, then
+  // "Install again" or "Close". The window is named in the ENVIRONMENT: "<port>:<token>:<the Chrome's pid>".
+  {
+    const net = await import('node:net'), http = (await import('node:http')).default;
+    const freePort = () => new Promise((r) => { const sv = net.createServer(); sv.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => { const p = sv.address().port; sv.close(() => r(p)); }); });
+    const TOKEN = 'ab'.repeat(18);
+    const ask = (port, method, p, tok = TOKEN) => new Promise((resolve) => { const rq = http.request({ host: '127.0.0.1', port, method, path: p, agent: false, timeout: 3000, headers: { Host: `127.0.0.1:${port}`, 'X-CB-Token': tok } }, (res) => { let t = ''; res.on('data', (d) => { t += d; }); res.on('end', () => { let j = null; try { j = JSON.parse(t); } catch { } resolve({ status: res.statusCode, text: t, json: j }); }); }); rq.on('error', () => resolve({ status: 0 })); rq.on('timeout', () => { rq.destroy(); resolve({ status: 0 }); }); rq.end(); });
+    const untilState = async (port, pred, ms = 60000) => { const end = Date.now() + ms; let st = null; while (Date.now() < end) { const r = await ask(port, 'GET', '/api/state'); if (r.json) { st = r.json; if (pred(st)) return st; } await new Promise((r2) => setTimeout(r2, 60)); } return st; };

+/**
+ * THE CONTAINER, FOR REAL - a real Windows AppContainer on this machine, started, used and stopped through the
+ * manager the way its page does it, and then through the page itself in a real browser.
+ *   [1] stopped: nothing runs, a command is refused, the page says so
+ *   [2] start: the steps are reported, it ends "running" on a drive letter, a process inside is the container's
+ *   [3] a command inside: its output, its exit code, where it runs
+ *   [4] the environment: the container's own home, temp and Java; the developer's env file sets and REMOVES
+ *   [5] the boundary: it reads its folder, writes home\ and projects\, and can NOT write tools\ or the image's files,
+ *       nor read a file outside the folder
+ *   [6] the real tools: java (with our agent on JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS) and node starting a child process (the shim)
+ *   [7] a command that keeps going: its output arrives while it runs, and Stop ends it
+ *   [8] what cmd does with a line: one command with brackets in its text; two commands; a line cmd refuses
+ *   [9] a known limit is named
+ *   [10] the page: Start and Stop in a real browser - and NO way to run a command of one's own
+ *   (the commands in [3]-[9] go through a request the manager answers only for this test: --test-run-api)
+ *   [11] stop, start again (seconds: the access is kept), quit while running (nothing left), forget
+ *   [12] uninstall, the manager's half, with the container RUNNING: everything of it that is not a file is gone
+ * Needs Windows, a JDK, Chrome. Leaves nothing: the container's profile and ACEs are removed at the end.
+ * Run: node test/test-sandbox-container-e2e.mjs [folder for screenshots]
+ */

+const startManager = (testApi = true) => {
+  const child = spawn(jdk.java, ['-cp', `${plan.classesDir};${plan.jnaPath}`, 'cb.sandbox.Manager', '--tree', tree, '--profile', profile, '--state', stateFile, '--no-tray', '--no-browser', '--uninstall-arg', process.execPath, '--uninstall-arg', SAYS, ...(testApi ? ['--test-run-api'] : [])], { stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], env: { ...process.env, CB_TEST_SECRET: 'top-secret-from-the-machine', CB_TEST_KEPT: 'kept-from-the-machine' } });
+  const m = { child, said: '', url: '', port: 0, token: '' };
+  child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { m.said += d; }); child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { m.said += d; });
+  m.exited = new Promise((r) => child.on('exit', (code) => r(code)));
+  return m;
+};
+const up = async (m) => { for (let i = 0; i < 200 && !m.url; i++) { const x = /MANAGER (http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:(\d+)\/\?t=([0-9a-f]+))/.exec(m.said); if (x) { m.url = x[1]; m.port = Number(x[2]); m.token = x[3]; } else await sleep(100); } return !!m.url; };
+const call = (m, method, p, body, timeout = 20000) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+  const data = body === undefined ? '' : JSON.stringify(body);
+  const r = http.request({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: m.port, method, path: p, timeout, headers: { Host: `127.0.0.1:${m.port}`, 'X-CB-Token': m.token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Content-Length': Buffer.byteLength(data) } }, (res) => { let t = ''; res.on('data', (d) => { t += d; }); res.on('end', () => { try { resolve(JSON.parse(t)); } catch { resolve(null); } }); });
+  r.on('error', () => resolve(null)); r.on('timeout', () => { r.destroy(); resolve(null); });
+  r.end(data);
+});
+const box = async (m) => ((await call(m, 'GET', '/api/container')) || {}).container || {};
+const until = async (m, want, ms, seen = []) => { const end = Date.now() + ms; let c = {}; while (Date.now() < end) { c = await box(m); if (c.detail && !seen.includes(c.detail)) seen.push(c.detail); if (want.includes(c.state)) return c; await sleep(150); } return c; };
+/** Run one command inside and wait for it: { exit, text, run, limit, ms }. */
+const run = async (m, command, jdkName) => {
+  const s = await call(m, 'POST', '/api/container/run', { command, jdk: jdkName || '' });
+  if (!s || !s.ok) return { refused: s ? s.error : 'no answer', text: '' };
+  let text = '', from = 0, last = null;
+  for (let i = 0; i < 1200; i++) {
+    last = await call(m, 'GET', `/api/container/run?id=${s.run.id}&from=${from}`);
+    if (!last || !last.ok) break;
+    text += last.text; from = last.next;
+    if (last.done) break;
+    await sleep(100);
+  }
+  return { id: s.run.id, exit: last && last.run ? last.run.exit : null, text: text.replace(/\r/g, ''), run: last && last.run, limit: last && last.limit };
+};

+  // never serves one, so that wait runs its full length (9 to 24 s, by how fast this machine refuses a connection)
+  const uCode = await Promise.race([m.exited, sleep(45000).then(() => 'still running')]);
+  const aclU = spawnSync('icacls', [tree], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout + spawnSync('icacls', [path.join(tree, 'home')], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout + spawnSync('icacls', [path.join(tree, 'projects')], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout;
+  ok(u1 && u1.ok === true && uCode === 0 && !existsSync(`${drive}\\image.json`) && profileBefore && !existsSync(profileDir), `asked while the container RUNS with something in it: it is stopped, the drive letter is given back, its Windows profile (${c0.name}) is gone - and the manager has quit (exit ${uCode})`);
+  let started = null; for (let i = 0; i < 50 && !started; i++) { try { started = JSON.parse(await readFile(SAID, 'utf8')); } catch { await sleep(100); } }
+  ok(started && started.args.join(' ') === ['--uninstall', '--yes', '--no-tray', '--tree', tree].join(' ') && started.cwd.toLowerCase() !== tree.toLowerCase() && !started.cwd.toLowerCase().startsWith(tree.toLowerCase() + path.sep) && u1.log && !existsSync(stateFile), `then it started the command it was given, OUTSIDE the folder, on exactly: ${started && started.args.join(' ').replace(tree, '<the folder>')}`);
+  ok(/S-1-15-2-/.test(aclU), 'its access is NOT taken off the files one by one first: they are about to be deleted, and it goes with them');
+  ok(existsSync(path.join(tree, 'image.json')) && existsSync(path.join(tree, 'home', 'note.txt')) && existsSync(path.join(tree, 'tools', 'node', 'node.exe')), 'the FILES are all still there: they are the sandbox command\'s to delete, once the manager that runs from them is gone');
+} catch (e) {
+  ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e));
+} finally {
+  // leave nothing on this machine: stop, take the container's ACEs and profile away, quit
+  try {
+    if (m.url && m.child.exitCode == null) {
+      await call(m, 'POST', '/api/container/stop');
+      await until(m, ['stopped', 'failed'], 30000);
+      const f = await call(m, 'POST', '/api/container/forget');
+      const acl = spawnSync('icacls', [tree], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout + spawnSync('icacls', [path.join(tree, 'home')], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout;
+      ok(f && f.ok && !/S-1-15-2-/.test(acl), 'forget: the container\'s ACEs are off the folder and its profile is deleted');
+      await call(m, 'POST', '/api/quit');
+      await Promise.race([m.exited, sleep(8000)]);
+    }
+  } catch { /* reported above */ }
+  if (m.child.exitCode == null) m.child.kill();
+  if (drive && existsSync(`${drive}\\image.json`)) spawnSync('subst', [drive, '/D']);
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * The developer's ~/.code_boss copied into the container's home on install AND on every update (sbuCopySettings).

+ * THE CONTAINER'S WEBLOGIC DOMAIN - made from the developer's own "basic" domain, deleted, made again - through the

+ * the production box can show is whether Oracle's own pack and unpack accept all this.
+ *
+ *   [0] the path rewrite in Java gives what the creator's JS gives, spelling for spelling
+ *   [1] before: no domain; Create is refused until the container runs
+ *   [2] what stops it is said first, and nothing is changed: no domain of the developer's; pack fails; unpack fails
+ *   [3] create: the tools get the right arguments, the developer's domain is only read

+/**
+ * THE CONTAINER'S ENVIRONMENT, IN JAVA, AGAINST THE JS IT IS A PORT OF. The manager (Java) builds the environment of
+ * every process it starts inside; src/agent/sandbox-env.mjs is where those rules were written down and measured.
+ * Two implementations of one rule drift, so each case here is given to BOTH and the answers must be the same:
+ *   [1] the env file: what is set, what is removed, which lines are neither
+ *   [2] the environment: the three layers, the merges, the replacements, names without regard to case
+ *   [3] what is refused: a path that would go unquoted into JVM or node options with a space in it
+ *   [4] the known limits: the same output names the same limit
+ * Needs a JDK. Run: node test/test-sandbox-env-java.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { sbxEnv, sbxParseEnvFile, sbxLimit } from '../src/agent/sandbox-env.mjs';
+import { sblFindJdk, sblWrite, sblCompileArgs, sblJavacMajor } from '../src/agent/sandbox-launcher.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const jdk = sblFindJdk();
+if (!jdk.ok) { console.log('SKIP: needs a JDK'); process.exit(0); }
+const work = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-envjava-'));
+const plan = sblWrite(path.join(work, 'launcher'));
+const jv = spawnSync(jdk.javac, ['-version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+const jc = spawnSync(jdk.javac, sblCompileArgs(plan, { release8: sblJavacMajor(`${jv.stdout} ${jv.stderr}`) >= 9 }), { encoding: 'utf8' });
+if (jc.status !== 0) { console.log('FAIL javac: ' + String(jc.stderr || jc.stdout).slice(0, 600)); process.exit(1); }
+const java = (what, input) => {
+  const r = spawnSync(jdk.java, ['-cp', `${plan.classesDir};${plan.jnaPath}`, 'cb.sandbox.Env', what], { input: JSON.stringify(input), encoding: 'utf8' });
+  try { return JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { return { broken: String(r.stdout || r.stderr).slice(0, 300) }; }
+};
+const same = (a, b) => JSON.stringify(Object.keys(a).sort().map((k) => [k, a[k]])) === JSON.stringify(Object.keys(b).sort().map((k) => [k, b[k]]));
+const diff = (a, b) => [...new Set([...Object.keys(a), ...Object.keys(b)])].filter((k) => a[k] !== b[k]).slice(0, 4).map((k) => `${k}: js=${JSON.stringify(a[k])} java=${JSON.stringify(b[k])}`).join(' | ');
+
+console.log('\n[1] the env file');
+const FILES = [
+  '',
+  '# a comment\n; another\n\nHTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy:8080\n  SPACED = a value with = signs and spaces  \n-OTHER_SECRET\n- SPACED_REMOVE\n',
+  'not a line\n=novalue\n9BAD=1\nGOOD=1\r\n-\n-two words\nA.B(C)-D=ok\nEMPTY=\n',
+];
+for (const text of FILES) {
+  const js = sbxParseEnvFile(text), jv2 = java('parse', { text });
+  ok(same(js.set, jv2.set || {}) && JSON.stringify(js.unset) === JSON.stringify(jv2.unset) && JSON.stringify(js.bad) === JSON.stringify(jv2.bad), `parsed alike: ${Object.keys(js.set).length} set, ${js.unset.length} removed, bad lines [${js.bad.join(',')}]${jv2.broken ? ' - JAVA: ' + jv2.broken : ''}`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] the environment');
+const MACHINE = { Path: 'C:\\Windows\\system32;C:\\Tools', SystemRoot: 'C:\\WINDOWS', USERPROFILE: 'C:\\Users\\dev', TEMP: 'C:\\Users\\dev\\AppData\\Local\\Temp', JAVA_HOME: 'C:\\Java\\8', BASE_64_AUTH_TOKEN: 'abc123==', M2_HOME: 'D:\\apps\\maven', MAVEN_OPTS: '-Xmx2g', NODE_OPTIONS: '--max-old-space-size=4096', '=C:': 'C:\\somewhere', PATHEXT: '.COM;.EXE;.BAT;.CMD', ComSpec: 'C:\\WINDOWS\\system32\\cmd.exe' };
+const CASES = [
+  ['a bare machine', { drive: 'Z:', machine: {} }],

+  ['another drive letter and Java 11', { drive: 'Y:', jdk: 'jdk11', machine: MACHINE }],

+ok(e2.JAVA_HOME === 'Z:\\tools\\jdk8' && !('BASE_64_AUTH_TOKEN' in e2) && !('M2_HOME' in e2) && e2.HTTPS_PROXY === 'http://proxy:8080' && e2.MAVEN_OPTS === '-Dmaven.repo.local=Z:\\home\\.m2\\repository -Xmx512m' && /^Z:\\tools\\node;Z:\\tools\\jdk8\\bin;Z:\\tools\\maven\\bin;C:\\WINDOWS\\System32;C:\\WINDOWS$/.test(e2.Path || e2.PATH || '') && e2.CB_JGIT === 'Z:\\tools\\jgit', 'and the Java, read on its own: JAVA_HOME is the container\'s whatever the file says, the removed token is GONE, a removed PATH leaves only ours');

+console.log('\n[3] what is refused');
+for (const input of [{ drive: 'Z:', machine: {}, mockitoAgent: 'Z:\\home\\my repo\\agent.jar' }, { drive: 'agent', machine: {} }, { drive: '', machine: {} }]) {

+  ok(!!jsErr && !!jv2.error && !jv2.env, `refused by both: ${String(jv2.error).slice(0, 80)}`);
+}

+console.log('\n[4] the known limits');
+const OUTPUTS = [
+  ['Cannot run program "git": CreateProcess error=5, java.io.FileNotFoundException: NUL (Access is denied)', 'nul'],
+  ['java.io.IOException: Unable to create temporary file\n\tat java.base/java.io.WinNTFileSystem.getNameMax(WinNTFileSystem.java:1)', 'tempfile'],
+  ['Could not initialize inline Byte Buddy mock maker.\nCould not self-attach to current VM using external process', 'attach'],
+  ['Error: spawn EPERM\n    at ChildProcess.spawn', 'node-spawn'],
+  ['NUL (Access is denied)\n... and later: getNameMax0', 'nul'],
+  ['BUILD SUCCESS', ''],
+  ['', ''],

+for (const [text, want] of OUTPUTS) {

+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * THE SANDBOX'S ENVIRONMENT - what code_boss gives every process it runs inside the AppContainer, and how it names
+ * what the container refuses. Each piece of the recipe was measured on the production box (snapshots #85-#87):
+ *   [1] the layout: tools\, home\ (the sandbox's user folder), projects\ (the project root)
+ *   [2] the environment: node gets the shim, every JVM gets our agent FIRST then Mockito's, user.home and tmp point
+ *       into the tree, the tree's node is first on PATH, and nothing of the developer's own environment comes along
+ *   [3] known limits are recognised from the text the production box actually printed, and said loudly
+ * Run: node test/test-sandbox-env.mjs
+ */
+import { sbxLayout, sbxEnv, sbxParseEnvFile, sbxMavenArgs, sbxLimit, sbxLimitNote, SBX_LIMITS } from '../src/agent/sandbox-env.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const throws = (f) => { try { f(); return false; } catch { return true; } };
+
+console.log('\n[1] the layout');
+const L = sbxLayout('Z:');
+ok(L.home === 'Z:\\home' && L.projects === 'Z:\\projects' && L.tools === 'Z:\\tools', `home, projects and tools under the drive (${L.home}, ${L.projects}, ${L.tools})`);
+ok(L.mavenSettings === 'Z:\\home\\.m2\\settings.xml' && L.mavenRepo === 'Z:\\home\\.m2\\repository' && L.npmrc === 'Z:\\home\\.npmrc', 'the tools\' config and caches live in the sandbox\'s user folder');
+ok(L.jdk() === 'Z:\\tools\\jdk8' && L.jdk('jdk11') === 'Z:\\tools\\jdk11', 'more than one JDK can be in the tree, by name');
+ok(sbxLayout('z:\\').drive === 'z:', 'a trailing slash on the drive is fine');
+ok(throws(() => sbxLayout('D:\\agent')) && throws(() => sbxLayout('')), 'anything but a drive letter is refused (the tree is reached through one inside)');

+ok(/-Duser\.home=Z:\\home\b/.test(e.JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS) && /-Djava\.io\.tmpdir=Z:\\home\\tmp\b/.test(e.JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS), 'and a home and a temp folder inside the tree (the real profile is unreadable in there)');
+ok(!/byte-buddy/.test(e.JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS), 'no Mockito agent unless one is named');
+const m = sbxEnv({ drive: 'Z:', jdk: 'jdk11', mockitoAgent: 'Z:\\home\\.m2\\repository\\net\\bytebuddy\\byte-buddy-agent\\1.12.19\\byte-buddy-agent-1.12.19.jar' });
+const agents = m.JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS.match(/-javaagent:\S+/g) || [];

+ok(m.JAVA_HOME === 'Z:\\tools\\jdk11' && m.PATH.includes('Z:\\tools\\jdk11\\bin'), 'the JDK is chosen per run');
+ok(e.HOME === 'Z:\\home' && e.USERPROFILE === 'Z:\\home' && e.HOMEDRIVE === 'Z:' && e.HOMEPATH === '\\home' && e.TEMP === 'Z:\\home\\tmp', 'HOME, USERPROFILE and TEMP are the sandbox\'s own');
+ok(e.NPM_CONFIG_USERCONFIG === 'Z:\\home\\.npmrc' && e.NPM_CONFIG_CACHE === 'Z:\\home\\npm-cache' && e.GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM === '1', 'npm reads the shipped .npmrc; git looks for no system config');
+ok(e.CB_JGIT === 'Z:\\tools\\jgit' && !/git/i.test(e.PATH), 'git is JGit (CB_JGIT, which the node shim sends `git` to) - no git.exe on PATH: it cannot start in here');

+ok(!('USERNAME' in e) && Object.keys(e).length < 30, 'with no machine environment given, the result is just what the launcher controls');
+
+console.log('\n[2b] three layers: the machine, the env file, the launcher');
+const machine = { Path: 'C:\\Program Files\\Git\\cmd;C:\\Users\\dev\\bin', USERPROFILE: 'C:\\Users\\dev', TEMP: 'C:\\Users\\dev\\AppData\\Local\\Temp', JAVA_HOME: 'C:\\Java\\JDK\\8\\64',

+const filed = sbxEnv({ drive: 'Z:', machine, file: '# mine\nHTTPS_PROXY=http://other:3128\nNEW_ONE=yes\n-OTHER_SECRET\n-base_64_auth_token\njava_tool_options=-Dmine=1\nTEMP=C:\\somewhere\n' });
+ok(filed.HTTPS_PROXY === 'http://other:3128' && filed.NEW_ONE === 'yes', 'the env file overrides a machine variable, and adds its own');

new c0b1b387475102ea0e010029
+/**
+ * THE IMAGE CREATOR, END TO END - against a local bare repository standing in for GitLab, with an existing `main`.
+ *   [1] a first run builds agent/ on the `sandbox` branch and pushes it with the test-channel tag; main is untouched
+ *   [2] what is in it: the tools, the generic .npmrc, settings.xml, the launch config, the compiled launcher, our agent
+ *   [3] a second run with nothing changed commits nothing
+ *   [4] somebody else's work on the branch - knowledge/, and a folder BESIDE agent/ - survives a creator run
+ *   [5] a changed tool is copied and committed again; an unchanged one is not
+ *   [6] an .npmrc with a token, a JRE, a node without npm.cmd: refused, and nothing is written or pushed
+ *   [7] --prod moves the production tag; --no-push pushes nothing
+ * Needs a JDK (the launcher is really compiled) and git. Run: node test/test-sandbox-image-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, readdirSync, chmodSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { spawn, spawnSync, execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { runImageCreator, sbiParseArgs, sbiJavaMajor, sbiFingerprint, sbiChannelTag } from '../src/agent/sandbox-image-cli.mjs';
+import { sblFindJdk } from '../src/agent/sandbox-launcher.mjs';
+import { sbiRePath } from '../src/agent/sandbox-image.mjs';
+import { runSandbox } from '../src/agent/sandbox-cmd.mjs';
+import { jgitLib } from './_jgit-lib.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const git = (cwd, ...args) => { const r = spawnSync('git', args, { cwd, encoding: 'utf8' }); return { code: r.status, out: String(r.stdout || '').trim(), err: String(r.stderr || '').trim() }; };
+
+console.log('\n[0] the small parts');
+const a = sbiParseArgs(['--keep', '--maven', 'D:\\mvn', '--java-project', 'D:\\proj', '--jre', 'C:\\Java\\JDK\\64', '--prod', '--git-remote', 'https://x/y.git']);
+ok(a.unknown.length === 0, 'the removed probes\' switches are still known here (a command line kept from then does not fail)');
+const bad = sbiParseArgs(['--run', '--tree', 'D:/agent', '--uninstall', '-yes', '--uninstal', '--maven', '--no-push', 'stray word']);
+ok(bad.unknown.length === 4 && /^-yes is not a switch of this command \(did you mean --yes\?\)$/.test(bad.unknown[0]) && /^--uninstal is not a switch/.test(bad.unknown[1]) && /^--maven needs a value after it$/.test(bad.unknown[2]) && /^"stray word" is not attached to any switch/.test(bad.unknown[3]), `a single dash, a misspelling, a switch with no value and a stray word are each named: ${JSON.stringify(bad.unknown[0])}`);
+ok(a.maven === 'D:\\mvn' && a.jre === 'C:\\Java\\JDK\\64' && a.flags.has('prod') && a.flags.has('keep'), 'the probes\' switches parse as they are');
+ok(a['java-project'] === 'D:\\proj' && !a.flags.has('D:\\proj') && a['git-remote'] === 'https://x/y.git', 'and a switch the creator ignores still takes its value with it');
+ok(sbiJavaMajor('java version "1.8.0_461"') === 8 && sbiJavaMajor('openjdk version "11.0.31" 2026-04-21 LTS') === 11 && sbiJavaMajor('nope') === 0, 'a Java\'s version is read from java -version');
+ok(sbiChannelTag(false) === 'sandbox-devtest' && sbiChannelTag(true) === 'sandbox-production', 'the image has its own channel tags');
+
+if (process.platform !== 'win32') { console.log('\nSKIP the end-to-end part: robocopy and the launcher are Windows'); console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`); process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0); }
+if (!sblFindJdk().ok) { console.log('\nSKIP the end-to-end part: no JDK to compile the launcher with'); console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`); process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0); }
+
+const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-image-'));
+const mk = async (file, text = 'x') => { await mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true }); await writeFile(file, text); };
+// the reference box's installs, in miniature
+const J8 = path.join(root, 'java', '8'), NODE = path.join(root, 'node'), MVN = path.join(root, 'mvn'), PROFILE = path.join(root, 'profile');
+await mk(path.join(J8, 'bin', 'java.exe')); await mk(path.join(J8, 'bin', 'javac.exe')); await mk(path.join(J8, 'lib', 'rt.jar'), 'rt');

+const create = async (extra = [], args = base) => { const lines = []; const code = await runImageCreator([...args, ...extra], { log: (s) => lines.push(s), env, home: HOME }); return { code, text: lines.join('\n') }; };
+const show = (ref, file) => git(REMOTE, 'show', `${ref}:${file}`);

+ok(git(REMOTE, 'rev-parse', 'main').out === mainBefore && git(REMOTE, 'rev-parse', 'production').out === prodBefore, 'main and the app\'s production tag are exactly where they were');
+ok(git(REMOTE, 'merge-base', 'main', 'sandbox').code !== 0, 'the sandbox branch shares no history with main');

+
+console.log('\n[2] what is in the image');
+const files = tree('sandbox');
+ok(files.every((f) => f.startsWith('agent/') || f === '.gitattributes'), `everything is under agent/ (${files.length} files)`);
+for (const f of ['agent/tools/jdk8/bin/javac.exe', 'agent/tools/node/npm.cmd', 'agent/tools/node/node_modules/npm/bin/npm-cli.js', 'agent/tools/maven/bin/mvn.cmd', 'agent/tools/shim/cb-shim.cjs', 'agent/tools/agents/cbfix.jar', 'agent/tools/agents/cbdump.jar', 'agent/tools/app/code-boss.mjs', 'agent/tools/launcher/classes/cb/sandbox/Server.class', 'agent/tools/launcher/classes/cb/sandbox/Bridge.class', 'agent/tools/launcher/classes/cb/sandbox/NativeLib.class','agent/tools/launcher/classes/cb/sandbox/Manager.class', 'agent/tools/launcher/classes/cb/sandbox/Setup.class', 'agent/tools/launcher/classes/cb/sandbox/manager.html', 'agent/tools/launcher/lib/jna-5.17.0.jar', 'agent/knowledge/README.md'])

+console.log('\n[10] --run: ONE command - make the image, pull it into the agent folder, start the manager');
+{
+  const { default: http } = await import('node:http');
+  const ask = (url, method, p) => new Promise((resolve) => {
+    const u = new URL(url);
+    const r = http.request({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: u.port, method, path: p, timeout: 3000, headers: { Host: u.host, 'X-CB-Token': u.searchParams.get('t'), 'Content-Length': 0 } }, (res) => { let t = ''; res.on('data', (d) => { t += d; }); res.on('end', () => { try { resolve(JSON.parse(t)); } catch { resolve(null); } }); });
+    r.on('error', () => resolve(null)); r.on('timeout', () => { r.destroy(); resolve(null); }); r.end();
+  });
+  const TREE = path.join(root, 'agent-run');
+  const head10 = git(REMOTE, 'rev-parse', 'sandbox').out;
+  await writeFile(path.join(J8, 'lib', 'rt.jar'), 'rt for the --run test');

+  // nobody at a window (--yes --no-browser) and a home that needs a login this "machine" does not have: it stops

+  await mk(path.join(PROFILE, '.m2', 'settings.xml'), '<settings><servers><server><id>nexus</id><username>shared.build</username><password>hunter2-SHARED</password></server></servers></settings>\n');
+  const r10 = await create(['--no-push', '--run', '--yes', '--tree', TREE, '--no-browser', '--no-tray']);
+  let url2 = '';
+  const url =(r10.text.match(/the manager is running - (http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:\d+\/\?t=[0-9a-f]+)/) || [])[1];
+  try {
+    ok(r10.code === 0 && /push SKIPPED/.test(r10.text) && /--run: now the developer's side, on this box \(from the local image just built\)/.test(r10.text) && !!url, `one command built the image and started the manager: ${(r10.text.match(/sandbox: the manager is running.*/) || ['(no manager line)'])[0].slice(0, 70)}`);
+    ok(git(REMOTE, 'rev-parse', 'sandbox').out === head10, 'with --no-push NOTHING reached the server: the pull was from the local image');
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'tools', 'jdk8', 'lib', 'rt.jar')) && (await readFile(path.join(TREE, 'tools', 'jdk8', 'lib', 'rt.jar'), 'utf8')) === 'rt for the --run test' && !existsSync(path.join(TREE, '.git')), 'the agent folder holds the image JUST built');
+    const st = url ? await ask(url, 'GET', '/api/status') : null;
+    ok(st && st.ok && path.resolve(st.tree) === path.resolve(TREE) && st.image && st.image.tools.jdk8 && st.homeFiles.some((f) => f.file === '.npmrc') && st.homeSetUp === true, 'and the manager is up on it: the image, its tools, and a home that is ALREADY set up');
+    const hs = existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'home', '.m2', 'settings.xml')) ? await readFile(path.join(TREE, 'home', '.m2', 'settings.xml'), 'utf8') : '';
+    ok(hs.includes('<password>hunter2-SHARED</password>') && !/@@CB_/.test(hs) && !/hunter2|shared\.build/.test(r10.text), 'the home\'s settings.xml has this machine\'s own login (and the run printed none of it)');
+
+    console.log('\n[10b] --uninstall on the same command: the old container goes FIRST, then the image is built and installed afresh');
+    await mk(path.join(TREE, 'projects', 'old-work.txt'), 'left from the last install');
+    const noTree = await create(['--no-push', '--run', '--yes', '--uninstall', '--no-browser', '--no-tray']);
+    ok(noTree.code === 2 && /--uninstall needs --tree <folder>/.test(noTree.text) && existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'projects', 'old-work.txt')) && !/copied|unchanged since/.test(noTree.text), '--uninstall without --tree: refused before anything is done (it must be SAID which folder)');
+    const r10b = await create(['--no-push', '--run', '--yes', '--uninstall', '--tree', TREE, '--no-browser', '--no-tray']);
+    url2 = (r10b.text.match(/the manager is running - (http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:\d+\/\?t=[0-9a-f]+)/) || [])[1];
+    const at = (re) => r10b.text.search(re);
+    ok(r10b.code === 0 && at(/--uninstall: first, the container in /) >= 0 && at(/sandbox: uninstalled\./) > at(/--uninstall: first/) && at(/sandbox: uninstalled\./) < at(/(copied|unchanged since)/) && at(/--run: now the developer's side/) > at(/sandbox: uninstalled\./), `one command: it SAYS it uninstalled, before building, and then installs (${(r10b.text.match(/sandbox: uninstalled\..*/) || ['(no uninstall line)'])[0].slice(0, 70)}...)`);
+    ok(!!url2 && url2 !== url && (await ask(url, 'GET', '/api/status')) === null && !existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'projects', 'old-work.txt')) && existsSync(path.join(TREE, 'image.json')), 'the old manager is gone, what was in the folder is gone, and a fresh install is running in the same folder');
+  } finally {
+    // leave nothing on this machine: an install gives a real container access to the folder - uninstall takes it back
+    const gone = await runSandbox(['--uninstall', '--yes', '--tree', TREE, '--no-tray'], { log: () => {}, env, home: HOME }).catch(() => ({ code: 1 }));
+    ok(gone.code === 0 && !existsSync(TREE), 'afterwards: uninstalled (the container, its access and the folder are gone)');
+    for (const u of [url, url2]) if (u) { await ask(u, 'POST', '/api/quit'); for (let i = 0; i < 40 && (await ask(u, 'GET', '/api/status')); i++) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150)); }
+    delete env.CB_SANDBOX_JAVA;
+  }
+}

+console.log('\n[11] WebLogic: a plain copy whose recorded paths become the sandbox\'s own');

+  const CMDF = path.join(root, 'my-weblogic-start.txt');
+  await writeFile(CMDF, '# ours\r\nset USER_MEM_ARGS=-Xmx1g\r\n"Z:\\tools\\jdk8\\bin\\java.exe"\r\n-Dweblogic.Name=server\r\n-cp "Z:\\tools\\wls\\wlserver\\server\\lib\\weblogic.jar"\r\nweblogic.Server\r\n');

+  ok(withCmd.code === 0 && /wls-start WebLogic's default start command, from .*my-weblogic-start\.txt \(6 lines\)/.test(withCmd.text) && (await readFile(START, 'utf8')).split('\n')[3] === '-Dweblogic.Name=server', '--wls-command <file>: the command is in the image (tools\\wls-start.txt), one argument a line as it was written');

+  // REGION PROPERTIES (the developer, 2026-10-10/11): the applications' common config from this machine

+  // pristine copy every update puts back - committed with it. The developer's files are the ones one level up, copied
+  // from default\ only when missing (Container.java).
+  const IMG = path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-image');
+  const PP = path.join(root, 'commonconfig-src');
+  await mk(path.join(PP, 'teb', 'app.properties'), 'db.url=jdbc:oracle:thin:@host:1521/x\n');
+  await mk(path.join(PP, 'common.properties'), 'env=local\n');
+  const withPp = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--commonconfig', PP]);
+  const PPD = path.join(IMG, 'agent', 'wls', 'regionproperties', 'default');
+  const ppCommitted = execFileSync('git', ['-C', IMG, 'ls-files', '--', 'agent/tools/regionproperties', 'agent/wls'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+  ok(withPp.code === 0 && /regionproperties .*commonconfig-src -> wls\\regionproperties\\default \(the pristine copy; a file not in Z:\\wls\\regionproperties is copied up from it\)/.test(withPp.text) && (await readFile(path.join(PPD, 'teb', 'app.properties'), 'utf8')).includes('db.url') && existsSync(path.join(PPD, 'common.properties')) && /agent\/wls\/regionproperties\/default\/teb\/app\.properties/.test(ppCommitted),
+    `--commonconfig <dir>: the folder is in the image as wls\\regionproperties\\default, and committed (${ppCommitted.trim().split('\n').length} file(s))`);
+  ok(ppCommitted.trim().split('\n').every((l) => l.startsWith('agent/wls/regionproperties/default/')), 'the image has nothing else under wls\\ (projprops is the container\'s, made empty)');
+  const badPp = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--commonconfig', path.join(root, 'no-such-commonconfig')]).catch((e) => ({ code: 1, text: String(e.message) }));
+  ok(badPp.code !== 0 && /--commonconfig .*no-such-commonconfig: there is no such folder/.test(badPp.text), 'a --commonconfig folder that is not there stops the creator');
+  // TNSNAMES (2026-10-11): D:\TNSNAMES, or --tnsnames, goes into the image as TNSNAMES\default, committed
+  const TN = path.join(root, 'tnsnames-src');
+  await mk(path.join(TN, 'tnsnames.ora'), 'DEV = (DESCRIPTION = (ADDRESS = (HOST = h)))\n');
+  // an image made before JGit left tools\git in the creator's working clone, and every run committed it again:
+  // the creator removes it, and the commit has it removed
+  await mk(path.join(IMG, 'agent', 'tools', 'git', 'cmd', 'git.exe'), 'old');
+  // Git for Windows ships read-only files (the reference box, build 2234: "ENOTEMPTY ... tools\git\usr\lib\perl5\...")
+  const RO = path.join(IMG, 'agent', 'tools', 'git', 'usr', 'lib', 'perl5', 'ro.pm');
+  await mk(RO, 'read only'); chmodSync(RO, 0o444);
+  execFileSync('git', ['-C', IMG, 'add', '--', 'agent/tools/git']);
+  execFileSync('git', ['-C', IMG, '-c', 'user.name=t', '-c', 'user.email=t@t', 'commit', '-q', '-m', 'an image with git.exe'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+  // A FILE HELD OPEN while the creator removes a folder (an antivirus reading it): sbiRemoveTree removes everything
+  // else, says why the rest is left, and never throws - so the image is not stopped. Held by Java, which opens a file
+  // without letting it be deleted (node and cmd let it go).
+  {
+    const jdk = sblFindJdk(process.env);
+    if (!jdk.ok) console.log('  SKIP the held-file check: no JDK');
+    else {
+      const HD = path.join(root, 'held-tree'), HF = path.join(HD, 'perl5', 'held.pm'), OTHER = path.join(HD, 'perl5', 'other.pm'), ROF = path.join(HD, 'ro.pm');
+      await mk(HF, 'held'); await mk(OTHER, 'other'); await mk(ROF, 'ro'); chmodSync(ROF, 0o444);
+      const jsrc = path.join(root, 'holdsrc'); await mkdir(jsrc, { recursive: true });
+      await writeFile(path.join(jsrc, 'Hold.java'), 'public class Hold { public static void main(String[] a) throws Exception { java.io.RandomAccessFile f = new java.io.RandomAccessFile(a[0], "r"); System.out.println("held"); Thread.sleep(Long.parseLong(a[1])); f.close(); } }\n');
+      execFileSync(jdk.javac, ['-d', jsrc, path.join(jsrc, 'Hold.java')]);
+      const java = path.join(path.dirname(jdk.javac), 'java.exe');
+      const holder = spawn(java, ['-cp', jsrc, 'Hold', HF, '20000'], { stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'], windowsHide: true });
+      await new Promise((r) => { holder.stdout.once('data', r); setTimeout(r, 8000); });
+      const { sbiRemoveTree } = await import('../src/agent/sandbox-image-cli.mjs');
+      let threw = null, r = null;
+      try { r = sbiRemoveTree(HD); } catch (e) { threw = e; }
+      spawnSync('taskkill', ['/PID', String(holder.pid), '/T', '/F'], { stdio: 'ignore' });   // by PID
+      await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 500));
+      ok(!threw && r && r.gone === false && /EBUSY|ENOTEMPTY|EPERM|still there/.test(r.why) && existsSync(HF) && !existsSync(OTHER) && !existsSync(ROF),
+        `a held file: everything else is removed (a read-only file too), the held one is left and said (${r && r.why}), nothing thrown`);
+      const r2 = sbiRemoveTree(HD);
+      ok(r2.gone && !existsSync(HD), 'once it is let go, the next try removes the rest');
+    }
+  }
+  const withTn = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--commonconfig', PP, '--tnsnames', TN]);
+  const tnCommitted = execFileSync('git', ['-C', IMG, 'ls-files', '--', 'agent/TNSNAMES'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+  const gitLeft = execFileSync('git', ['-C', IMG, 'ls-files', '--', 'agent/tools/git'], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();
+  ok(/git\s+removed tools\\git, from an image made before JGit/.test(withTn.text) && !existsSync(path.join(IMG, 'agent', 'tools', 'git')) && gitLeft === '',
+    `tools\\git from an image made before JGit: removed, and the commit has it gone (${gitLeft ? 'still committed: ' + gitLeft : 'not in the image'})`);
+  ok(withTn.code === 0 && /tnsnames\s+.*tnsnames-src -> TNSNAMES\\default \(the pristine copy; a file not in Z:\\TNSNAMES is copied up from it\)/.test(withTn.text) && (await readFile(path.join(IMG, 'agent', 'TNSNAMES', 'default', 'tnsnames.ora'), 'utf8')).includes('DEV') && tnCommitted.trim() === 'agent/TNSNAMES/default/tnsnames.ora',
+    `--tnsnames <dir>: the folder is in the image as TNSNAMES\\default, and committed (${tnCommitted.trim() || 'nothing'})`);
+  const badTn = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--tnsnames', path.join(root, 'no-such-tnsnames')]).catch((e) => ({ code: 1, text: String(e.message) }));
+  ok(badTn.code !== 0 && /--tnsnames .*no-such-tnsnames: there is no such folder/.test(badTn.text), 'a --tnsnames folder that is not there stops the creator');
+  const oldSwitch = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--projprops', PP]);
+  ok(oldSwitch.code === 2 && /--projprops/.test(oldSwitch.text), '--projprops is no longer a switch: refused, not ignored');
+  // GIT IS JGIT (2026-10-11: git.exe cannot start in the container on the production box - it opens NUL first). The
+  // creator fetches JGit through the reference box's Maven and compiles our git command line against it: tools\jgit.
+  // --no-jgit (the runs above) says the container has no git; --jgit <folder> uses jars already on the box.
+  ok(/jgit\s+NO git in this image \(--no-jgit\): clone\(\) and git\(\) fail in the container/.test(withPp.text), '--no-jgit: the creator says the container will have no git');
+  const oldGit = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--git', path.join(root, 'x')]);
+  ok(oldGit.code === 2 && /--git is not a switch/.test(oldGit.text), '--git (Git for Windows, 2210) is no longer a switch: refused, not ignored');
+  const jlib = jgitLib({ why: (s) => console.log('  SKIP the --jgit runs: ' + s) });
+  if (jlib) {
+    const withJgit = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--commonconfig', PP, '--jgit', jlib], base.filter((x) => x !== '--no-jgit'));
+    const JG = path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-image', 'agent', 'tools', 'jgit');
+    const jgCommitted = execFileSync('git', ['-C', path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-image'), 'ls-files', '--', 'agent/tools/jgit'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+    const imgJson = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'sandbox-image', 'agent', 'image.json'), 'utf8'));
+    ok(withJgit.code === 0 && existsSync(path.join(JG, 'classes', 'cb', 'jgit', 'Git.class')) && existsSync(path.join(JG, 'lib', 'org.eclipse.jgit-4.5.4.201711221230-r.jar'))
+      && /agent\/tools\/jgit\/classes\/cb\/jgit\/Git\.class/.test(jgCommitted) && imgJson.tools.jgit && imgJson.tools.jgit.version === '4.5.4.201711221230-r',
+      `--jgit <jars>: JGit and our git command line over it are in the image as tools\\jgit, committed, recorded (${(withJgit.text.match(/jgit\s+JGit.*/) || ['(no line)'])[0].slice(0, 100)})`);
+    // the image's git answers: run it the way the shim does
+    const v = spawnSync(process.env.JAVA_HOME ? path.join(process.env.JAVA_HOME, 'bin', 'java.exe') : 'java', ['-cp', path.join(JG, 'classes') + ';' + path.join(JG, 'lib', '*'), 'cb.jgit.Git', '--version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
+    ok(/^git version 4\.5\.4\.jgit/.test(v.stdout), `the image's tools\\jgit runs: ${v.stdout.trim().slice(0, 60)}`);
+    const again = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--commonconfig', PP, '--jgit', jlib], base.filter((x) => x !== '--no-jgit'));
+    ok(again.code === 0 && /jgit\s+JGit 4\.5\.4\.201711221230-r unchanged since the last image - not replaced/.test(again.text), 'the same jars and sources again: not replaced, not committed again');
+  }
+  await mk(path.join(root, 'no-jars-here', 'readme.txt'), 'nothing');
+  const badJgit = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push', '--jgit', path.join(root, 'no-jars-here')], base.filter((x) => x !== '--no-jgit')).catch((e) => ({ code: 1, text: String(e.message) }));
+  ok(badJgit.code !== 0 && /--jgit .*no-jars-here: there is no org\.eclipse\.jgit-\*\.jar in that folder/.test(badJgit.text), 'a --jgit folder without JGit in it stops the creator');
+  // without --jgit, Maven fetches it: a Maven that cannot says so, and how to get past it
+  const viaMvn = await create(['--weblogic', WLS, '--no-push'], base.filter((x) => x !== '--no-jgit')).catch((e) => ({ code: 1, text: String(e.message) }));
+  ok(viaMvn.code !== 0 && /Maven could not fetch JGit 4\.5\.4\.201711221230-r[\s\S]*--jgit <a folder with the jars> uses jars you have; --no-jgit makes an image without git/.test(viaMvn.text),
+    `with no --jgit the creator asks this box's Maven, and a Maven that fails is said with the way out: ${(viaMvn.text.match(/Maven could not fetch.*/) || ['?'])[0].slice(0, 90)}`);
+  ok(sbiRePath('a=d:\\JAVA\\wls\\12c\\x b=D:\\java\\wls\\12c-old', [['D:\\java\\wls\\12c\\', 'Z:\\tools\\wls']]) === 'a=Z:\\tools\\wls\\x b=D:\\java\\wls\\12c-old', 'the rewrite ignores letter case and a trailing slash, and respects where a name ends');
+}

+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
