=== add test/test-server-hardening-probe.mjs
new a1d06949fc5bea9fc7500044
+/**
+ * Regression probes for server.mjs production hardening (confirmed bugs):
+ *  - conv-atomic : a corrupt conversation.json is recovered from .bak, not
+ *                  silently dropped; the corrupt file is left for salvage.
+ *  - conv-flush  : the debounced save can be flushed synchronously (the path
+ *                  the SIGINT/SIGTERM handlers use) so the tail isn't lost.
+ *  - pause-deadlock : cancelling during a git-needs-branch pause unblocks the
+ *                  chat (status leaves 'running'); close-project then works.
+ *  - rev-pending : an aborted reviewer leaves the review row in a terminal
+ *                  state, not a forever-spinning 'pending'.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-server-hardening-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+const runGit = async (dir, args) => { try { execFileSync('git', args, { cwd: dir, stdio: 'ignore' }); } catch {} };  // was git-workflow.runGit (that module was removed); local helper for test setup only
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function mockAdapter(scripts) {
+  let i = 0;
+  return { async complete() {
+    if (i >= scripts.length) throw new Error('script exhausted');
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: scripts[i++] } }] };
+  }};
+}
+async function getState(port) { return (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/state`)).json(); }
+async function waitFor(port, pred, ms = 8000) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) {
+    const s = await getState(port); if (pred(s)) return s;
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
+  }
+  throw new Error('waitFor timeout');
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  // ── conv-atomic: recover from .bak when the main file is corrupt ──
+  console.log('\n[A] corrupt legacy conversation.json recovers from .bak (then migrates to .jsonl)');
+  {
+    const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-srv-'));
+    await mkdir(path.join(dir, '.code_boss'), { recursive: true });
+    const good = JSON.stringify({ version: 1, nextEventId: 3, chatEvents: [
+      { id: 'e1', type: 'user-message', content: 'hello from backup' },
+      { id: 'e2', type: 'assistant-text', content: 'hi there' },
+    ]});
+    // Main file is truncated/corrupt; .bak holds the last good copy.
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'conversation.json'), good.slice(0, good.length - 12), 'utf8');
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'conversation.json.bak'), good, 'utf8');
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: 30101, adapter: mockAdapter([]), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+    });
+    try {
+      const s = await getState(30101);
+      ok(s.events.some((e) => e.content === 'hello from backup'), `recovered events from .bak (got ${s.events.length})`);
+      // The legacy salvage is migrated into the new JSONL log; the old corrupt
+      // file is retired to .migrated (not deleted).
+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl')), 'recovered events migrated into conversation.jsonl');
+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'conversation.json.migrated')), 'corrupt legacy file retired to .migrated');
+    } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+  }
+
+  // ── conv-flush: synchronous flush persists the tail ──
+  console.log('\n[B] flushConversationSave persists immediately (no 300ms wait)');
+  {
+    const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-srv2-'));
+    const { server, flushConversationSave } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: 30102, adapter: mockAdapter(['Just answering.', '<review-pass/>']), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+    });
+    try {
+      await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:30102/api/message', {
+        method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'hi', model: 'm' }),
+      });
+      await waitFor(30102, (s) => s.status === 'done');
+      // Immediately flush (what the SIGINT handler does) — do NOT wait for the debounce.
+      ok(typeof flushConversationSave === 'function', 'startServer exposes flushConversationSave');
+      flushConversationSave();
+      const raw = await readFile(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl'), 'utf8');
+      const chatEvents = raw.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean).map((l) => JSON.parse(l));   // one event per line
+      ok(chatEvents.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && e.content === 'hi'), 'user message persisted by sync flush');
+      ok(chatEvents.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text'), 'assistant tail persisted by sync flush');
+    } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+  }
+
+  // ── dirty-at-open repo: DIRECT mode (the branch-pause wizard is retired) ──
+  console.log('\n[C] dirty-at-open repo runs DIRECT — no pause, nothing deadlocks');
+  {
+    const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-srv3-'));
+    await runGit(dir, ['init', '-q', '-b', 'main']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'tester']);
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'README.md'), '# r\n');
+    await runGit(dir, ['add', 'README.md']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['commit', '-q', '-m', 'init']);
+    // PRE-EXISTING uncommitted change → repo is dirty-at-open. Two-mode git
+    // gives this DIRECT mode (work on the current branch, no auto-branch, no
+    // pause); the old wizard that parked the run on session.gitPending is gone.
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'wip.txt'), 'user work in progress\n');
+    const scripts = [
+      `Writing.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await write("foo.txt", "hi");</js></remote-workspace>`,
+      `done.`, `<review-pass/>`,
+    ];
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: 30103, adapter: mockAdapter(scripts), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+    });
+    try {
+      fetch('http://127.0.0.1:30103/api/message', {
+        method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'write a file', model: 'm' }),
+      });
+      // The git-branch pause wizard is RETIRED (two-mode git, commits 8c008ce/4567ed6):
+      // a dirty-at-open repo runs in DIRECT mode with no pause to resolve — the run must
+      // simply COMPLETE, with no gitPending state ever parking it, and nothing left blocked.
+      const s = await waitFor(30103, (st) => st.status === 'error' || st.status === 'done', 15000);
+      ok(s.status === 'done', `dirty-at-open write-run completed in DIRECT mode without pausing (status=${s.status})`);
+      ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'foo.txt')), 'and the write actually landed — the run was not a no-op');
+      ok(!s.gitPending || Object.keys(s.gitPending).length === 0, 'no gitPending pause state was exposed (the wizard is retired)');
+      // And close-project must NOT 409.
+      const close = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:30103/api/close-project', { method: 'POST' });
+      ok(close.status === 200, `close-project succeeds after the run (got ${close.status})`);
+    } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+  }
+
+  // ── rev-pending: aborted reviewer finalizes the review row ──
+  console.log('\n[D] aborted reviewer leaves no forever-pending review row');
+  {
+    const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-srv4-'));
+    await runGit(dir, ['init', '-q', '-b', 'main']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'tester']);
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'README.md'), '# r\n');
+    await runGit(dir, ['add', 'README.md']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['commit', '-q', '-m', 'init']);
+    await runGit(dir, ['checkout', '-q', '-b', 'CODE_BOSS_test']);
+    // Adapter: agent writes (so the tree is dirty / HEAD advances → reviewer
+    // runs), then the reviewer's complete() REJECTS (simulating an abort /
+    // network failure mid-review). (The <next-task> tag that used to ride
+    // along was a stripped verb even then — no task store — and is gone.)
+    let calls = 0;
+    const adapter = {
+      async complete() {
+        calls++;
+        if (calls === 1) return { choices: [{ message: { content: `Writing.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await write("a.txt", "x");</js></remote-workspace>` } }] };
+        if (calls === 2) return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'new task' } }] };
+        // reviewer call → reject (must NOT match /aborted/i or server treats it as user-cancel)
+        throw new Error('reviewer network failure');
+      },
+    };
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: 30104, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+    });
+    try {
+      await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:30104/api/message', {
+        method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'write a file', model: 'm' }),
+      });
+      const s = await waitFor(30104, (st) => st.status === 'error' || st.status === 'done', 8000);
+      // Review was removed; the hardening point that survives is: an adapter that THROWS mid-run must
+      // not crash the server — the run reaches a terminal state and /api/state still responds.
+      ok(s.status === 'error' || s.status === 'done', `server survived an adapter that throws mid-run (status ${s.status})`);
+    } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });
+

+// Server liveness observability + SSE ping (added after a silent overnight hang left NO diag-log trace):
+//  1. a periodic HEARTBEAT line in the global diag.log so a dead server vs. an idle one is distinguishable;
+//  2. the SSE keepalive is a DATA ping (type:'ping'), not a `:` comment, so the client's onmessage fires and it
+//     can detect a dead idle connection. Run: node test/test-server-heartbeat.mjs
+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import http from 'node:http';
+
+const HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-hb-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+process.env.CB_HEARTBEAT_MS = '250';       // fast diag heartbeat for the test
+process.env.CB_SSE_HEARTBEAT_MS = '150';   // fast SSE ping for the test
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PORT = 29188;
+
+const { server } = await startServer({
+  html: '<!doctype html><body>x</body>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+  defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'hi' } }] }; } }, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }],
+});
+try {
+  // ── 1. the diag.log gets periodic HEARTBEAT lines ──
+  await sleep(700);   // ~2-3 heartbeats at 250ms
+  const diag = readFileSync(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'diag.log'), 'utf8');
+  const hbLines = diag.split('\n').filter((l) => l.includes('HEARTBEAT'));
+  ok(hbLines.length >= 1, `1: diag.log has HEARTBEAT line(s) (${hbLines.length})`);
+  ok(/status=\S+ sse=\d+ lastReq=\d+s/.test(hbLines[0] || ''), '1: the heartbeat carries status + sse-subscriber count + last-request age');
+  ok(/pid=\d+/.test(hbLines[0] || '') && /build=/.test(hbLines[0] || ''), '1: the heartbeat is tagged with pid + build (so a maintainer can place it)');
+
+  // ── 2. the SSE keepalive is a DATA ping the client's onmessage would see (not a `:` comment) ──
+  const sawPing = await new Promise((resolve) => {
+    const req = http.get({ host: '127.0.0.1', port: PORT, path: '/api/stream' }, (res) => {
+      let buf = '';
+      res.on('data', (d) => {
+        buf += d.toString();
+        if (/data: \{"type":"ping"\}/.test(buf)) { try { req.destroy(); } catch {} resolve(true); }
+      });
+    });
+    req.on('error', () => resolve(false));
+    setTimeout(() => { try { req.destroy(); } catch {} resolve(false); }, 2000);
+  });
+  ok(sawPing === true, '2: the SSE stream emits a data ping (type:ping) — visible to the client onmessage, refreshing its liveness clock');
+  // A `:` comment heartbeat would NOT match the data-ping regex, so this also asserts we did NOT keep the old form.
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { try { server.close(); } catch {} }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+

=== add test/test-settings-context-and-resubmit.mjs
new 4a085c34595e9c1c8635a1f7
+/**
+ * Two surfaces that replaced things the developer asked to remove:
+ *
+ *   SETTINGS lists the instruction files in play. The chat used to announce them on every open as a <context>
+ *   tool row — a call the agent never made and the developer never asked for, which history then rebuilt into an
+ *   assistant turn. Which files are loaded is a fact about the session, so it belongs where you look for facts
+ *   about the session.
+ *
+ *   RESUBMIT offers to send a message that never got an answer. A cancelled stream, a failed unlock, an adapter
+ *   error — the run ends with the developer's message sitting there as the last thing in the chat and nothing to
+ *   click, which is how a developer ends up retyping or wondering whether it took.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-settings-context-and-resubmit.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sctx-home-'));   // hermetic: no real ~/AGENTS.md
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29244;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(String.fromCharCode(92)).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+// An adapter that FAILS, so the run ends leaving the developer's message unanswered — the resubmit case.
+let failCalls = 0;
+const failing = { async *completeStream() { failCalls++; throw new Error('upstream is down'); }, async complete() { throw new Error('upstream is down'); } };
+// A run whose first reply is ONLY a tool block, then a pause before the answer. When that first call is logged,
+// the developer's message is patched with its id; with a tool-only reply nothing answering the message is on
+// screen yet, and the re-render that patch causes is exactly when the offer used to appear mid-run.
+const toolFirst = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const full = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    const body = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf(String.fromCharCode(10) + '--- developer'));
+    if (/<remote-workspace-result/.test(body)) { await sleep(2500); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Listed it: one file.' } }] }; return; }
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: '<remote-workspace><list path="."/></remote-workspace>' } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sctx-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'AGENTS.md'), 'Use tabs. Never commit to main.\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(process.env.CB_TEST_UI_HTML || path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: failing, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(1200);   // long enough that the old open-row would have rendered
+
+    console.log('\n[A] no <context> row in the chat on open');
+    const ctxRows = await page.$$eval('#log .row.tool', (els) => els.filter((e) => /context\(/.test(e.innerText)).length);
+    ok(ctxRows === 0, `the chat opens clean — no tool row for reading AGENTS.md (${ctxRows})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] Settings lists the instruction files instead');
+    await page.evaluate(() => window.openSettings());
+    await sleep(400);
+    const ctxText = await page.$eval('#settingsCtxFiles', (el) => el.innerText.trim());
+    ok(/AGENTS\.md/.test(ctxText), `the file is named (${JSON.stringify(ctxText.slice(0, 70))})`);
+    ok(/chars/.test(ctxText), 'with its size, so a huge instruction file is visible as one');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[C] a message that never got a reply offers to resubmit');
+    await page.type('#input', 'please refactor the parser');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => ['done', 'idle', 'error'].includes(window.__sessionRef?.status), { timeout: 20000 });
+    await sleep(600);
+    const banner = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const el = document.querySelector('.cb-banner[data-bkey="resubmit"]');
+      return el ? { text: el.innerText.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(), hasBtn: !!el.querySelector('.cbb-action') } : null;
+    });
+    ok(!!banner, `the banner appears after a run that answered nothing (${JSON.stringify(banner)})`);
+    ok(banner && banner.hasBtn, 'and it carries a resubmit button rather than just describing the problem');
+
+    console.log('\n[D] clicking it runs the same message again - not a copy of it');
+    const before = await page.$$eval('#log .bubble.user', (els) => els.length), callsBefore = failCalls;
+    await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelector('.cb-banner[data-bkey="resubmit"] .cbb-action').click());
+    await sleep(900);
+    const after = await page.$$eval('#log .bubble.user', (els) => els.length);
+    const lastText = await page.$$eval('#log .bubble.user .bubble-content', (els) => els[els.length - 1]?.textContent.trim() || '');
+    // (the developer, 2026-10-30: "it submits a second copy of your message instead of resubmitting the same message")
+    ok(failCalls > callsBefore, `the message was run again (${callsBefore} -> ${failCalls} model calls)`);
+    ok(after === before, `as the same message, not a second copy (${before} -> ${after} user bubbles)`);
+    ok(/refactor the parser/.test(lastText), 'with the same text, not an empty send');
+    console.log('');
+    console.log('[E] clearing the conversation takes the offer with it');
+    // Reported: the banner survived a clear, still offering to resend a message that no longer existed — and
+    // clicking it would have sent that text into the fresh conversation. The offer is a statement ABOUT the
+    // rendered log, so it has to be re-decided whenever the log changes underneath it.
+    await page.evaluate(() => { window.confirm = () => true; window.showConfirm = async () => true; });   // (the app's own dialog since 2446)
+    await page.evaluate(() => clearConversation());
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !document.querySelector('#log .bubble.user'), { timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(500);
+    const afterClear = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+      banner: !!document.querySelector('.cb-banner[data-bkey="resubmit"]'),
+      bubbles: document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user').length,
+    }));
+    ok(afterClear.bubbles === 0, `the conversation is empty (${afterClear.bubbles} user bubbles)`);
+    ok(!afterClear.banner, 'and the resubmit offer is gone with it');
+
+    console.log('\n[F] the offer never appears while the run is still going');
+    // Reported from production: "That message did not get a reply - send it again?" popped up while the agent was
+    // working, and then it carried on. The question is only true once the run has ended.
+    {
+      const projectDir2 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sctx2-'));
+      const { server: server2 } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT + 1, adapter: toolFirst, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir2 });
+      try {
+        const p2 = await browser.newPage();
+        await p2.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+        p2.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+        await p2.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 1}/`);
+        await p2.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+        await sleep(600);
+        await p2.type('#input', 'list the project');
+        await p2.keyboard.press('Enter');
+        const seen = await p2.evaluate(async () => {
+          const out = { running: 0, bannerWhileRunning: 0, endedBanner: null, patched: false };
+          const t0 = Date.now();
+          while (Date.now() - t0 < 20000) {
+            const st = window.__sessionRef?.status;
+            const b = !!document.querySelector('.cb-banner[data-bkey="resubmit"]');
+            if (st === 'running') { out.running++; if (b) out.bannerWhileRunning++; }
+            else if (out.running) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600)); out.endedBanner = !!document.querySelector('.cb-banner[data-bkey="resubmit"]'); break; }
+            out.patched = out.patched || !!(window.__sessionRef?.events || []).find((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && e.rawId);
+            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+          }
+          return out;
+        });
+        ok(seen.running > 10, `sampled the run while it was going (${seen.running} samples)`);
+        ok(seen.patched, 'the message was patched with its call id mid-run - the re-render that used to raise the offer happened');
+        ok(seen.bannerWhileRunning === 0, `no offer to resend while the agent was still working (${seen.bannerWhileRunning} samples showed one)`);
+        ok(seen.endedBanner === false, 'and none once it answered');
+        await p2.close();
+      } finally { server2.close(); await cleanupDir(projectDir2); }
+    }
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); server.close(); await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-settings-shared-across-windows.mjs
new 9e976fa029a55480ec6b8d37
+// EVERY WINDOW IS ITS OWN SERVER PROCESS (the launcher starts one per window, on its own port), and the settings are
+// shared - they live in ~/.code_boss. Each instance used to read them once at startup and keep them in memory, so a key
+// set in one window reached the file and that one process, and every other window went on USING the old one until it
+// was closed and reopened. Not a display problem: a run in the other window really did go out with the old key.
+//
+// Two servers here stand in for two windows, sharing one config dir exactly as they do in production.
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const ROOT = path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-settings-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;              // before the server module is loaded: the config dir is resolved at import
+delete process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS;
+delete process.env.CB_LLM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log(`  ok ${label}`); } else { fail++; console.log(`  FAIL ${label}`); } };
+
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'model-a' }, { id: 'model-b' }]; },
+};
+
+const PORT_A = 17700 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 300);
+const PORT_B = PORT_A + 1;
+const boot = (port) => startServer({
+  html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: '', gitlabCreds: null, defaultModel: 'model-a',
+  port, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'model-a' }, { id: 'model-b' }], initialProject: null,
+});
+const A = await boot(PORT_A);
+const B = await boot(PORT_B);
+const at = (port, p) => `http://127.0.0.1:${port}${p}`;
+const getJson = async (port, p) => (await fetch(at(port, p))).json();
+const postJson = async (port, p, body) => (await fetch(at(port, p), { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) })).json();
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] the API key: set it in one window, the other must USE it, not just show it');
+  const before = await getJson(PORT_B, '/api/api-key');
+  ok(before.hasKey === false, 'window B starts with no key (neither window has one)');
+  const set = await postJson(PORT_A, '/api/api-key', { key: 'sk-shared-abcdefghijklmno' });
+  ok(set.ok === true, 'window A accepts the key');
+  const after = await getJson(PORT_B, '/api/api-key');
+  ok(after.hasKey === true && after.prefix === 'sk-share' + '…', `window B reports the new key without a restart (${after.prefix})`);
+  ok(B.session ? B.session.apiKey === 'sk-shared-abcdefghijklmno' : true, 'and B\'s session really holds it, so its next run goes out with the new key');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the tuning settings follow too');
+  await postJson(PORT_A, '/api/tuning', { stallCutMs: 1234, llmIdleTimeoutMs: 45000, showLlmRows: true, model: 'model-b' });
+  const sB = await getJson(PORT_B, '/api/state');
+  ok(sB.stallCutMs === 1234, `stall cutoff reached window B (${sB.stallCutMs})`);
+  ok(sB.llmIdleTimeoutMs === 45000, `idle timeout reached window B (${sB.llmIdleTimeoutMs})`);
+  ok(sB.showLlmRows === true, 'the llm-rows toggle reached window B');
+  ok(sB.defaultModel === 'model-b' || sB.model === 'model-b', `the chosen model reached window B (${sB.defaultModel || sB.model})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] GitLab credentials follow');
+  await postJson(PORT_A, '/api/gitlab-creds', { user: 'someone', token: 'glpat-xyz' });
+  const cB = await getJson(PORT_B, '/api/gitlab-creds');
+  ok(cB.hasCreds === true && cB.user === 'someone', 'window B sees the credentials set in window A');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] a window that changed nothing does not fight the one that did');
+  await postJson(PORT_B, '/api/tuning', { stallCutMs: 4321 });
+  const sA = await getJson(PORT_A, '/api/state');
+  ok(sA.stallCutMs === 4321, 'the change flows the other way as well (B to A)');
+  const sB2 = await getJson(PORT_B, '/api/state');
+  ok(sB2.stallCutMs === 4321, 'and the window that made the change still has its own value');
+} finally {
+  try { A.server.close(); } catch {}
+  try { B.server.close(); } catch {}
+  await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+

+ * delete). Read-only files, a path past 260 characters and a junction (removed, its target kept) are deleted; a file a

+import { tmpdir, homedir } from 'node:os';

+import path from 'node:path';

+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };

+const ROOT = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-delete-'));
+const run = async (argv, answer) => { const lines = []; const code = await runDeleteFolderCommand({ argv, log: (s) => lines.push(s), ask: answer === undefined ? null : async () => answer }); return { code, text: lines.join('\n') }; };

+  const yes = await run([b.d], 'y');
+  ok(yes.code === 0 && !existsSync(b.d), 'answered y: gone');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a file a program holds open: named, with the reason; the next run finishes');

+  const held = path.join(c.d, 'home', 'held.txt');
+  // cmd's redirection holds its file open without letting it be deleted, for as long as the command runs
+  const holder = spawn(process.env.ComSpec || 'cmd.exe', [`/d /s /c "ping -n 6 127.0.0.1 >> "${held}""`], { stdio: 'ignore', windowsHide: true, windowsVerbatimArguments: true });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 50 && !existsSync(held); i++) await sleep(100);
+  await sleep(300);
+  const busy = await run([c.d, '--yes']);
+  ok(busy.code === 1 && existsSync(held) && !existsSync(c.ro) && /could not be:\n\s+(EBUSY|EPERM)\s+.*held\.txt/.test(busy.text) && /(EBUSY|EPERM): .*Quit the sandbox manager/.test(busy.text),
+    `the held file is listed with Windows' reason, everything else is deleted: ${(busy.text.match(/(EBUSY|EPERM)\s+\S+/) || [''])[0].slice(0, 90)}`);
+  await new Promise((r) => { if (holder.exitCode !== null) r(); else holder.on('exit', r); });
+  await sleep(300);
+  const again = await run([c.d, '--yes']);
+  ok(again.code === 0 && !existsSync(c.d), 'once the program let go, the same command finishes it');

+  await refuse([path.parse(ROOT).root], /is a whole drive\. Nothing was deleted/, 'a drive\'s root');

+  await refuse([path.dirname(homedir())], /holds .*\. Nothing was deleted/, 'a folder that holds the profile');

+  await refuse([], /which folder\?/, 'no folder');
+  await refuse([path.join(ROOT, 'x'), path.join(ROOT, 'y')], /one folder at a time/, 'two words (a path with a space and no quotes)');
+  await refuse([path.join(ROOT, 'x'), '--force'], /--force is not a switch/, 'an unknown switch');

+  if (!existsSync(SETUP)) console.log('  SKIP: no dist/code-boss-setup.mjs (build first)');
+  else {

+  }
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }

+/**
+ * Setup repo-URL handling: normalizeRepoUrl accepts whatever shape a user pastes
+ * (with or without .git, trailing slash, a GitLab browser tail, scp-style,
+ * surrounding quotes/whitespace) and yields the clean canonical web URL — and
+ * crucially, the .git suffix works EITHER way (the original bug report: setup
+ * "always gives this error" because the URL never reached the config).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-setup-url.mjs
+ */
+import { normalizeRepoUrl } from '../src/deploy-cmd.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const eq = (got, want, label) => {
+  if (got === want) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label, `\n     got:  ${JSON.stringify(got)}\n     want: ${JSON.stringify(want)}`); }
+};
+
+const WEB = 'https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss';
+
+console.log('\n[A] with OR without .git both canonicalize to the same web URL');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss'), WEB, 'plain web URL unchanged');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss.git'), WEB, 'trailing .git stripped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss/'), WEB, 'trailing slash stripped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss.git/'), WEB, '.git + slash stripped');
+
+console.log('\n[B] paste noise: whitespace, quotes, browser tail, query/hash');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('  https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss  '), WEB, 'surrounding whitespace trimmed');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('"https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss"'), WEB, 'surrounding double-quotes stripped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl("'https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss.git'"), WEB, 'single-quotes + .git stripped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss/-/tree/main'), WEB, 'GitLab /-/tree/<branch> browser tail dropped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss/-/blob/main/README.md'), WEB, '/-/blob/... browser tail dropped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss?ref=x#frag'), WEB, 'query + hash dropped');
+
+console.log('\n[C] embedded auth is stripped (never persist a token in the stored URL)');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://user:tok123@gitlab.example.mil/group/code_boss.git'), WEB, 'userinfo stripped from the canonical URL');
+
+console.log('\n[D] scp-style git@host:group/repo → https');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('git@gitlab.example.mil:group/code_boss.git'), WEB, 'scp-style converted to https + .git stripped');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('git@gitlab.example.mil:group/code_boss'), WEB, 'scp-style without .git converted');
+
+console.log('\n[E] subgroups preserved; empty input → empty');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('https://gitlab.example.mil/group/sub/code_boss.git'), 'https://gitlab.example.mil/group/sub/code_boss', 'nested subgroup path kept');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl(''), '', 'empty string stays empty');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('   '), '', 'whitespace-only stays empty');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl(null), '', 'null → empty');
+
+console.log('\n[F] non-http (local bare repo path used in tests) passes through untouched');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('/tmp/remote.git'), '/tmp/remote.git', 'absolute local .git path preserved');
+eq(normalizeRepoUrl('C:\\repos\\remote.git'), 'C:\\repos\\remote.git', 'windows local .git path preserved');
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-show-once.mjs
new 75b439c14a06902b770f14f5
+/**
+ * A PLUGIN'S BIG OUTPUT, GIVEN TO THE AGENT ONCE (src/agent/show-once.mjs; the developer, 2026-11-02: a browser plugin's
+ * text snapshots bloated the context - send the snapshot whole only when there is not one in the context already,
+ * otherwise a diff or "the same as before"; and the design: host.showOnce wraps the text in marks, and the hiding or
+ * diff happens only when the history is rendered for a request).
+ *   [1] the renderer: whole the first time; the same again is one line; changed with diff is the changed lines; a
+ *       change too big for a diff, or no diff asked, is whole; inside a JSON string too; a cut-off mark is taken out
+ *   [2] a request: what decides is what is IN it - the earlier one faded (not there), the later one is whole again
+ *   [3] a session's new turns are weighed against what the session already has
+ *   [4] through the real worker: a plugin's snapshot returned by two snippets - the second request carries it once
+ * Run: node test/test-show-once.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-once-home-'));
+const { showOnceWrap, showOnceStrip, showOnceRender } = await import('../src/agent/show-once.mjs');
+const { assembleConvo, assembleDelta, markFiltered } = await import('../src/agent/engine.mjs');
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const page = (n, extra = '') => Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => `row ${i}: item ${i}${i === 7 ? extra : ''}`).join('\n') + `\nfooter ${n}`;
+
+console.log('\n[1] the renderer');
+{
+  const a = showOnceWrap(page(1), { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true });
+  const b = showOnceWrap(page(1), { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true });
+  const c = showOnceWrap(page(1, ' (selected)'), { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true });
+  const d = showOnceWrap(page(1, ' (selected)'), { key: 'snapshot:other' });
+  const r = showOnceRender(['before ' + a, 'again ' + b, c, d]);
+  ok(r[0] === 'before ' + page(1), 'the first: the whole text, without its marks');
+  ok(r[1] === 'again (unchanged: the same as the last snapshot:main above)', `the same again: one line (${r[1]})`);
+  ok(/^\(changed since the last snapshot:main above/.test(r[2]) && /-row 7: item 7\n\+row 7: item 7 \(selected\)/.test(r[2]) && r[2].length < page(1).length / 2, `changed, with diff: only the lines that changed (${r[2].length} chars against ${page(1).length})`);
+  ok(r[3] === page(1, ' (selected)'), 'another key: its own first, whole');
+  const nodiff = showOnceRender([showOnceWrap('x\ny', { key: 'k' }), showOnceWrap('x\nz', { key: 'k' })]);
+  ok(nodiff[1] === 'x\nz', 'changed, no diff asked: whole');
+  const big = showOnceRender([showOnceWrap(page(1), { key: 'k', diff: true }), showOnceWrap(page(1).replace(/item/g, 'thing'), { key: 'k', diff: true })]);
+  ok(big[1] === page(1).replace(/item/g, 'thing'), 'a change too big to be worth a diff: whole');
+  const j1 = JSON.stringify({ snap: showOnceWrap('line 1\nline 2', { key: 's' }) }), j2 = JSON.stringify({ snap: showOnceWrap('line 1\nline 2', { key: 's' }), n: 2 });
+  const rj = showOnceRender([j1, j2]);
+  ok(JSON.parse(rj[0]).snap === 'line 1\nline 2' && /unchanged/.test(JSON.parse(rj[1]).snap), `inside a JSON string (a snippet's return): rendered, and still JSON (${rj[1]})`);
+  ok(showOnceRender(['cut ' + a.slice(0, 60)])[0] === 'cut ' + page(1).slice(0, 60 - '⟦once diff snapshot:main⟧\n'.length), 'a mark cut off (a slice): the marks go, the text goes as it is');
+  ok(showOnceStrip(a) === page(1) && showOnceStrip(j1) === JSON.stringify({ snap: 'line 1\nline 2' }), 'for the screen: the text without the marks');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] a request: what is IN it decides');
+{
+  const S = (n) => markFiltered({ role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result>' + showOnceWrap(page(n), { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true }) + '</remote-workspace-result>' });
+  const one = S(1), two = S(1);
+  const both = assembleConvo({ staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: '', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'a' }, one, { role: 'assistant', content: 'b' }, two] })[0].content;
+  ok((both.match(/footer 1/g) || []).length === 1 && /unchanged: the same as the last snapshot:main above/.test(both) && !/⟦/.test(both), 'both in the request: the snapshot once, then "unchanged"; no marks reach the model');
+  const faded = assembleConvo({ staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: '', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'go (the earlier part has faded)' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'b' }, two] })[0].content;
+  ok((faded.match(/footer 1/g) || []).length === 1 && !/unchanged/.test(faded), 'the earlier one faded - not in the request: the later one is whole');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] a session\'s new turns, against what the session has');
+{
+  const base = { role: 'user', content: showOnceWrap(page(2), { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true }) };
+  const next = { role: 'user', content: showOnceWrap(page(2, ' (open)'), { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true }) };
+  const withBase = assembleDelta({ tag: 'ab12', messages: [next], before: [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'x' }, base] })[0].content;
+  const without = assembleDelta({ tag: 'ab12', messages: [next], before: [] })[0].content;
+  ok(/changed since the last snapshot:main above/.test(withBase) && !/footer 2/.test(withBase), 'the session has the earlier one: only the lines that changed');
+  ok(/footer 2/.test(without) && !/changed since/.test(without), 'it does not: whole');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] through the real worker');
+{
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-once-'));
+  const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+  const snaps = [page(3), page(3), page(3, ' (checked)')];
+  let k = 0;
+  const extraTools = { browser_snapshot: { description: 'The page as text.', readOnly: true, impl: async () => ({ content: showOnceWrap(snaps[Math.min(k++, 2)], { key: 'snapshot:main', diff: true }) }) } };
+  const replies = ['<remote-workspace><js>return await browser_snapshot({});</js></remote-workspace>', '<remote-workspace><js>return await browser_snapshot({});</js></remote-workspace>', '<remote-workspace><js>const s = await browser_snapshot({}); return { snap: s, at: "after the click" };</js></remote-workspace>', 'Done.\n' + C];
+  let i = 0; const sent = [];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { sent.push(req.messages.map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n')); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };
+  const events = [];
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'look at the page' }], projectDir: dir, extraTools,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {} });
+  const last = sent[sent.length - 1];
+  ok(sent.length === 4 && (last.match(/footer 3/g) || []).length === 1, `the last request carries the page once, not three times (${(last.match(/footer 3/g) || []).length})`);
+  ok(/unchanged: the same as the last snapshot:main above/.test(last) && /changed since the last snapshot:main above/.test(last) && /\+row 7: item 7 \(checked\)/.test(last), 'the second as "unchanged", the third (returned as a field) as the line that changed');

new cd48ace673cb81adccbb6d4c
+/**
+ * THE SIDE CHAT AND THE BACKGROUND AGENTS' SESSIONS AT ONCE - EDGE CASES AND VOLUME (side-session.mjs createSideLine's
+ * drop; token-budget.mjs's pause; server.mjs: per-chat lines and views, Clear in either chat, a Clear or a project switch
+ * while a pass is out, the budget with both chats, a restart, 200 messages). The happy paths are test-side-chat.mjs,
+ * test-side-sessions-edges.mjs, test-token-budget-edges.mjs.
+ *   [U1] a line dropped while its call is out stays dropped: the reply does not open it again (whole or follow-up)
+ *   [U2] a background call that waits long on one signal leaves no abort listener behind a pause
+ * Through the real server, a stand-in model "g" that keeps sessions (each reply carries one):
+ *   [S1] the main chat and the side chat at once: each its own Heads up, on its own session, reading its own chat;
+ *        the side chat has no bookkeeper
+ *   [S2] Clear in the side chat leaves the main chat's sessions alone; Clear in the main chat leaves the side chat's
+ *   [S3] Clear while the bookkeeper's call is out: the next pass goes whole, not on the session from before the Clear
+ *   [S4] Clear and drop what stands while the bookkeeper's call is out: the pass's todos from the cleared conversation are
+ *        not put back, and the agent is not called back to them
+ *   [S5] Clear while a Heads up check is out: the next check goes whole
+ *   [S6] the token budget with both chats: their agents go at once while the background calls wait, and all go in time
+ *   [S7] a project switch while the bookkeeper's call is out: the new project's first pass is whole, with nothing of the
+ *        old project in it
+ *   [V]  200 messages: the bookkeeper's follow-ups stay small, its session is kept (never past the follow-up limit), its
+ *        memo does not grow, Heads up checks every tenth message only, the raw log is capped
+ *   [R]  a restart: Heads up's count is kept, the first pass and check go whole, and then on their new sessions
+ * Run: node test/test-side-chat-edges.mjs
+ */

+let sidN = 0;
+const contentKind = (t) => (/Bring the record up to date/.test(t) ? 'bk' : /You watch a working session|<no-heads-up\/>/.test(t) ? 'hu' : /THE AGENT ASKS:/.test(t) ? 'history' : 'other');
+const chatOf = (t) => (/pear list/.test(t) && !/apple list/.test(t) ? 'side' : /apple list/.test(t) && !/pear list/.test(t) ? 'main' : /pear list/.test(t) ? 'both' : '?');
+const newDevs = (all) => [...all.slice(Math.max(0, all.lastIndexOf('=== NEW SINCE THE LAST PASS'))).matchAll(/\[(e\d+)\] developer[^\n]*\n([^\n]*)/g)].map((m) => ({ id: m[1], text: m[2] }));
+function bkReply(all) {
+  const ds = newDevs(all);
+  const d = ds[ds.length - 1] || { id: '', text: '' };
+  if (/please write the report generator/.test(d.text)) return `<stream-ops><mark item="${d.id}" kind="request"/><todo from="${d.id}">write the report generator</todo><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>`;
+  return '<stream-ops><needs-developer/></stream-ops>';
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const text = (req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n');
+    agentCalls.push({ at: Date.now(), side: /pear list/.test(text) && !/apple list/.test(text), text });
+    // ([H]: the side agent asks the history helper, and answers from what it says)
+    const lastAll = String((req.messages || []).slice(-1)[0]?.content || ''), lastMsg = lastAll.slice(lastAll.lastIndexOf('\n--- developer '));   // (the newest turn: the prompt's own examples hold a result)
+    if (/pear list history check/.test(lastMsg) && !/remote-workspace-result/.test(lastMsg)) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Asking.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await historyAsk("which apple list requests were there?");</js></remote-workspace>' } }] }; return; }
+    if (/HELPER SAYS/.test(lastMsg)) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'The helper says: apple list requests. <hand-over/>' } }] }; return; }
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Done. <hand-over/>' } }] };
+  },
+  async complete(req) {
+    const text = (req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content)).join('\n');
+    const sent = req.gemini_enterprise?.session || null;
+    const kind = sent && sessKind.has(sent) ? sessKind.get(sent) : contentKind(text);
+    const r = { kind, chat: sent && sessChat.has(sent) ? sessChat.get(sent) : chatOf(text), sent, got: null, lost: false, text, chars: text.length, at: Date.now(), done: false };
+    reqs.push(r);
+    if (holds[kind]) await holds[kind].p;
+    if (failNext[kind] && (failNext[kind] === 'delta') === !!sent) { failNext[kind] = null; r.done = true; r.error = true; throw Object.assign(new Error('500 Internal Server Error: the endpoint failed'), { status: 500 }); }
+    let sid = sent && alive.has(sent) ? sent : null;
+    if (!sid) { sid = `S${++sidN}`; alive.add(sid); sessKind.set(sid, kind); sessChat.set(sid, r.chat); }
+    r.got = sid; r.lost = !!sent && sid !== sent; r.done = true;
+    const content = kind === 'history' ? '<answer>HELPER SAYS: the apple list requests, from the main record.</answer>' : kind === 'hu' ? '<no-heads-up/>' : kind === 'bk' ? (/Bring the record up to date/.test(text) ? bkReply(text) : '<stream-ops><needs-developer/></stream-ops>') : '';
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content } }], ...(req.model === 'g' ? { gemini_enterprise: { session: sid, assist_token: 't' } } : {}) };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'g' }]; },
+};
+const hold = (kind) => { let release; const p = new Promise((r) => { release = r; }); holds[kind] = { p, release: () => { holds[kind] = null; release(); } }; return holds[kind]; };
+const boot = (port, project) => startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'g', port, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'g' }], initialProject: project });
+let { server, session } = await boot(PORT, proj);
+let curProj = proj;
+const q = (side) => (side ? '?chat=side' : '');
+const post = async (p, body) => { const r = await fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return { status: r.status, json: await r.json().catch(() => ({})) }; };
+const get = async (p) => (await fetch(B + p)).json();
+const logText = async () => readFile(path.join(curProj, '.code_boss', 'code-boss.log'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+const marks = async () => JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(curProj, '.code_boss', 'stream-marks.json'), 'utf8').catch(() => 'null'));
+const until = async (f, ms = 15000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const v = await f(); if (v) return v; await sleep(40); } return null; };
+const idle = (side) => until(async () => (await get('/api/state?limit=3' + (side ? '&chat=side' : ''))).status !== 'running', 20000);
+const settle = async (side = false) => {   // the run, then the bookkeeper's pass and any Heads up check after it
+  await sleep(60);
+  await idle(side);
+  const quiet = Number(process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS) || 0;
+  if (quiet) await sleep(quiet + 250);
+  await Promise.race([get('/api/heads-up?wait=1' + (side ? '&chat=side' : '')), sleep(30000)]);
+  await Promise.race([get('/api/heads-up?wait=1' + (side ? '&chat=side' : '')), sleep(30000)]);
+};
+const say = async (text, side = false) => { const n0 = reqs.length; await post('/api/message' + q(side), { text, model: 'g' }); await settle(side); return reqs.slice(n0); };
+const bkOf = (rs) => rs.filter((r) => r.kind === 'bk');
+const huOf = (rs, chat) => rs.filter((r) => r.kind === 'hu' && (!chat || r.chat === chat));
+const desc = (rs) => rs.map((r) => (r.sent ? `on ${r.sent}${r.lost ? ' (lost)' : ''}` : `whole->${r.got}`)).join(', ') || 'none';

+  return got;
+};

=== add test/test-side-session.mjs
new 0d9419bd5701609a39ec638c
+/**
+ * SIDE SESSIONS (side-session.mjs; the developer, 2026-10-30: on a session "it only charges for new messages added";
+ * "standardize everything on 4 messages per session (maybe use common code)"). Against a stand-in endpoint that keeps
+ * sessions as the gateway's plain models do:
+ *   [A] the whole record opens a session; a later call sends only the new turns and its ask, on the handle
+ *   [B] the 4-follow-up limit, idle, another model, other rules, a record rewritten: the whole again, a new session
+ *   [C] a model whose replies carry no handle: whole every time, nothing sent with a handle
+ *   [D] the endpoint answers on another session, or the call fails: taken again whole, once
+ *   [E] `whole`: a send-back on the session is only the send-back; whole, it is everything
+ *   [F] Heads up's check and its retry, and the bookkeeper's send-back, through their callParts
+ * Run: node test/test-side-session.mjs
+ */
+import { createSideLine, sideCall } from '../src/agent/side-session.mjs';
+import { runHeadsUpCheck, newHeadsUpState } from '../src/agent/heads-up.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// the endpoint: no handle = a new session; a handle = appended to it. `reply(text, req)` answers.
+function endpoint({ keeps = true, reply = () => 'fine' } = {}) {
+  const sessions = new Map(), reqs = [];
+  let n = 0, forget = false, failNext = false;
+  return {
+    reqs, sessions,
+    forgetAll() { forget = true; },
+    failOnce() { failNext = true; },
+    adapter: {
+      async complete(req) {
+        reqs.push(req);
+        if (failNext) { failNext = false; throw new Error('socket hang up'); }
+        const text = req.messages.map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+        let id = req.gemini_enterprise && req.gemini_enterprise.session;
+        if (forget) { sessions.clear(); forget = false; }
+        if (!id || !sessions.has(id)) { id = `s${++n}`; sessions.set(id, []); }
+        sessions.get(id).push(text);
+        const content = reply(text, req);
+        return keeps ? { choices: [{ message: { content } }], gemini_enterprise: { session: id, assist_token: 't' } } : { choices: [{ message: { content } }] };
+      },
+    },
+  };
+}
+const turn = (role, content) => ({ role, content });
+const S = 'You are a test side agent.';
+let clock = 1_000_000;
+const line = (lim = {}) => createSideLine({ name: 't', limits: () => ({ maxFollowups: 4, maxChars: 320000, idleMs: 600000, ...lim }), now: () => clock });
+
+console.log('\n[A] the whole record opens a session; later calls send only what is new');
+{
+  const ep = endpoint(), l = line();
+  const rec = [turn('user', 'asked: build the add command'), turn('assistant', 'starting on add')];

+/**

+ *   [U1] a bookkeeper pass on its session with nothing new: one more record turn saying so, on the session
+ *   [U2] a follow-up stopped mid-call (signal): not resent whole by sideCall; the next call opens a new session
+ *   [U3] limits() that throws: the built-in limits (4 follow-ups)

+ *   [H] Heads up's quiet check at its default (HEADS_UP_IDLE_MIN 3): one message, two - no check; the third - a check.

+ *   [V] the bookkeeper marks an OLD todo done and takes back an old rule between two Heads up checks: the second check
+ *       still goes on Heads up's session (the frozen snapshot of the marks)
+ *   [L] Settings' follow-up limit (POST /api/wire-limits { maxFollowups }): a whole send, and the next pass goes on the
+ *       session that whole send opened
+ *   [F] the endpoint forgets the bookkeeper's session: the pass is taken whole, the next continues on the new session
+ *   [T] every tenth message (HEADS_UP_EVERY) a check, with the quiet check off
+ *   [C] Clear (POST /api/clear-conversation, as the page does): the next bookkeeper pass and Heads up check go whole

+// a stand-in endpoint for the unit cases: no handle = a new session; a known handle = appended to it
+function endpoint({ keeps = () => true, reply = () => '<stream-ops></stream-ops>', slowMs = 0 } = {}) {
+  const sessions = new Map(), reqs = [];
+  let n = 0;
+  return {
+    reqs, sessions,
+    adapter: {
+      async complete(req) {
+        reqs.push(req);
+        if (slowMs) await new Promise((res, rej) => { const t = setTimeout(res, slowMs); req.signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => { clearTimeout(t); rej(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' })); }, { once: true }); });
+        const text = req.messages.map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+        let id = req.gemini_enterprise && req.gemini_enterprise.session;
+        if (!id || !sessions.has(id)) { id = `s${++n}`; sessions.set(id, []); }
+        sessions.get(id).push(text);
+        const content = reply(text, req);
+        return keeps(reqs.length) ? { choices: [{ message: { content } }], gemini_enterprise: { session: id, assist_token: 't' } } : { choices: [{ message: { content } }] };
+      },
+    },
+  };
+}

+const contentKind = (t) => (/Bring the record up to date/.test(t) ? 'bk' : /You watch a working session|<no-heads-up\/>/.test(t) ? 'hu' : 'other');

+function bkReply(all) {

+  const ops = [];

+  }
+  return `<stream-ops>${ops.join('')}<needs-developer/></stream-ops>`;

+// The simulation's own correctness. This is the only probe where the tools REALLY execute and the answer is the
+// repository rather than a rubric, so what has to be right is the machinery: that the two conversations stay
+// apart, that a stale commit is refused, that the outcome check cannot be talked into passing, and that every
+// design switch actually reaches the prompt. A simulation that scores itself generously is worse than none.
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { runSim, formatSimReport, runSimSweep, formatSimSweepReport, SIM_SWEEP, SIM_TASKS, simRepo, SIM_GOAL, SIM_DEFAULTS } from '../src/agent/sim-probe.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+const sha = (b) => createHash('sha256').update(String(b)).digest('hex').slice(0, 12);
+
+// A scriptable pair of sides. `agent` decides what the assistant says; the developer says DONE after `devDone`.
+function pair({ agent, devDone = 99 }) {
+  const seen = { agent: [], dev: [], sessions: [] };
+  let devN = 0;
+  return {
+    seen,
+    baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1',
+    async complete({ messages, ...rest }) {
+      const c = String(messages[0].content);
+      seen.sessions.push(rest.gemini_enterprise?.session || null);
+      if (/You are a working software developer/.test(c)) {
+        seen.dev.push(c);
+        return { choices: [{ message: { content: ++devN >= devDone ? 'DONE' : 'Please carry on.' } }], usage: { prompt_tokens: 20 }, gemini_enterprise: { session: 'DEV', assist_token: 'd' } };
+      }
+      seen.agent.push(c);
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: agent(seen.agent.length, c) } }], usage: { prompt_tokens: 30 }, gemini_enterprise: { session: 'AG', assist_token: 'a' } };
+    },
+  };

+  ok(!ep.seen.dev.some((c) => /<checkout|<commit|-result/.test(c)), 'and never sees the tool machinery — a developer does not read raw results');

+process.stdout.write('\n[3] the stale-base check is real, not decorative\n');
+{
+  const R = simRepo();
+  const stale = sha(R['src/config.ts']);
+  const ep = pair({
+    agent: (n) => (n <= 2 ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="${stale}" escaped="no">${R['src/config.ts']}export const RETRIES = ${n};\n</commit></repo>` : 'DONE'),

+  const run = await runSim({ adapter: ep, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 3 } });
+  // The FIRST commit lands and changes the SHA, so the second — sent against the SHA it originally saw — must be
+  // refused. That is the whole reason a commit carries a base, and it fires here without anyone arranging it.
+  ok(/RETRIES = 1/.test(run.repo['src/config.ts']), 'the first commit lands');
+  ok(!/RETRIES = 2/.test(run.repo['src/config.ts']), 'and the second is REFUSED because the file moved under it');
+  const rep = formatSimReport(run);
+  ok(/REJECTED, stale base/.test(rep), 'with the rejection in the transcript where it happened');
+  ok(ep.seen.agent.some((c) => /status="rejected"/.test(c)), 'and the agent is told, so it can check out again rather than retry blindly');

+process.stdout.write('\n[4] the outcome cannot be talked into passing\n');
+{
+  // An agent that says it is finished and touches nothing must NOT come out looking successful. Every rubric in
+  // the spec is a proxy; this is the rung where the answer is the file itself.
+  const claims = pair({ agent: () => 'I have added the retry count and wired it into the client. DONE', devDone: 1 });

+  const g = SIM_GOAL.check(run.repo);

+  // Corruption is the failure that hides: the change lands and the line beside it is quietly mangled.

+  // about whether corruption is DETECTED and reported when it does get through, so the repair is pinned off.
+  const cr = await runSim({ adapter: corrupt, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 2, repairEscapes: false } });

+  ok(/rest-of-file-intact=CORRUPTED/.test(formatSimReport(cr)), 'and the report calls it CORRUPTED rather than burying it');
+  ok(/BODY ARRIVED ESCAPED/.test(formatSimReport(cr)), 'with the turn it happened on flagged, and the escaped= it declared next to it');

+process.stdout.write('\n[4b] we repair escaping ourselves, judged against the text we sent\n');
+{
+  // A blanket un-escape is not safe: `\|` is corruption in TypeScript and CORRECT inside a regex, so a rule that
+  // strips backslashes would break working code. But we are not guessing — we know exactly what we handed over,
+  // so the repair can be judged rather than assumed: un-escape a candidate, and keep it only if it puts back more
+  // of the lines we sent. And we do the checking OURSELVES, because asking the model to audit its own formatting
+  // measured 3/3 clean without the flag and 0/3 with it.
+  const R = simRepo();
+  const mangled = R['src/config.ts'].replace('/a<b|c>d/g', '/a\\<b\\|c\\>d/g').replace('30_000', '60_000');
+  const commit = (n) => (n === 1 ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="${sha(R['src/config.ts'])}" escaped="no">${mangled}</commit></repo>` : 'DONE');
+  const task = { maxTurns: 2, task: 'one-file' };
+  const one = SIM_TASKS.find((t) => t.id === 'one-file');
+
+  const off = await runSim({ adapter: pair({ agent: commit }), model: 'x', options: { ...task, repairEscapes: false } });
+  ok(one.check(off.repo).added && !one.check(off.repo).intact, 'left alone, the edit lands and the regex beside it is corrupted');
+
+  const on = await runSim({ adapter: pair({ agent: commit }), model: 'x', options: { ...task, repairEscapes: true } });
+  const g = one.check(on.repo);
+  ok(g.added && g.intact, 'repaired, the same commit lands AND the regex survives — the outcome the switch exists to move');
+  ok(/un-escaped src\/config\.ts/.test(formatSimReport(on)), 'and the transcript records that we changed what it sent, rather than doing it silently');
+
+  // The other half, and the one that makes it safe to leave on: a body whose backslashes are the CODE's own must
+  // come through untouched. Here every escape is load-bearing, so un-escaping restores nothing and is refused.
+  const R2 = simRepo();
+  const legit = R2['src/config.ts'].replace('/a<b|c>d/g', '/\\d+\\|\\w+/g');
+  const ep2 = pair({ agent: (n) => (n === 1 ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="${sha(R2['src/config.ts'])}" escaped="no">${legit}</commit></repo>` : 'DONE') });
+  const kept = await runSim({ adapter: ep2, model: 'x', options: { ...task, repairEscapes: true } });
+  ok(kept.repo['src/config.ts'] === legit, 'a body whose backslashes belong to the code is committed byte-for-byte');
+  ok(/left src\/config\.ts alone/.test(formatSimReport(kept)), 'and the report says why it was left alone, so a wrong call can be spotted');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[4c] housekeeping gets its own turn, and the file set actually shrinks\n');
+{
+  // The developer's idea: releasing files and keeping a checklist are both things a weaker model does badly while
+  // it is mid-task, and both can move off the work turn for the price of a round trip. What has to be true for it
+  // to be worth anything is that the open set really shrinks — an agent that says "released" and keeps the file
+  // open has cost us a round trip and bought nothing.
+  const R = simRepo();
+  const ep = pair({
+    agent: (n, c) => {
+      if (/Housekeeping only/.test(c)) return '<release path="src/config.ts"/>\n[x] change the timeout\n[ ] tell the developer';
+      return n === 1 ? '<repo><checkout path="src/config.ts"/><checkout path="src/http-client.ts"/></repo>' : 'Working on it.';
+    },
+  });
+  const run = await runSim({ adapter: ep, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 2, housekeepingTurn: true } });
+  const rep = formatSimReport(run);
+  ok(ep.seen.agent.some((c) => /Housekeeping only/.test(c)), 'the housekeeping turn is really sent');
+  ok(ep.seen.agent.some((c) => /<release path=/.test(c)), 'and the agent is told the verb exists, or it could not comply');
+  ok(/released src\/config\.ts/.test(rep), 'a released file is recorded as released');
+  ok(/checklist: 2 item\(s\)/.test(rep), 'and the checklist it keeps is counted');
+  // The file it did NOT release must still be re-sent; the one it released must not be.
+  const later = ep.seen.agent.filter((c) => /Files you have checked out/.test(c)).slice(-1)[0] || '';
+  ok(/src\/http-client\.ts/.test(later) && !/path="src\/config\.ts"/.test(later), 'the released file stops being re-sent while the other keeps coming');
+
+  // The failure mode worth naming: an agent that does task work in the housekeeping turn has not been relieved of
+  // anything, it has just been handed a second place to do the same job.
+  const sloppy = pair({ agent: (n, c) => (/Housekeeping only/.test(c) ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="${sha(R['src/config.ts'])}" escaped="no">x</commit></repo>` : '<repo><checkout path="src/config.ts"/></repo>') });
+  const sr = await runSim({ adapter: sloppy, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1, housekeepingTurn: true } });
+  ok(/did TASK WORK in the housekeeping turn/.test(formatSimReport(sr)), 'and doing task work in the housekeeping turn is called out rather than passing as compliance');
+
+  // The other new switch: the file bodies go out inside a block asking their record to leave them out.
+  const wrapped = pair({ agent: (n) => (n === 1 ? '<repo><checkout path="src/config.ts"/></repo>' : 'Thinking.') });
+  await runSim({ adapter: wrapped, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 2, ignoreForSummary: true } });
+  const sent = wrapped.seen.agent.filter((c) => /Files you have checked out/.test(c)).slice(-1)[0] || '';
+  ok(/<ignore-for-summary>[\s\S]*Files you have checked out[\s\S]*<\/ignore-for-summary>/.test(sent), 'the re-sent bodies go out inside the ignore block, not beside it');
+  const plain = pair({ agent: (n) => (n === 1 ? '<repo><checkout path="src/config.ts"/></repo>' : 'Thinking.') });
+  await runSim({ adapter: plain, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 2, ignoreForSummary: false } });
+  ok(!/ignore-for-summary/.test(plain.seen.agent.join('\n')), 'and the arm without it sends no such block, so the A/B differs in one thing');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[5] an empty body is refused rather than blanking the file\n');
+{
+  const R = simRepo();
+  const ep = pair({ agent: (n) => (n === 1 ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="${sha(R['src/config.ts'])}" escaped="no"></commit></repo>` : 'DONE') });
+  const run = await runSim({ adapter: ep, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 2 } });
+  ok(run.repo['src/config.ts'] === run.before['src/config.ts'], 'an empty commit body leaves the file alone');
+  ok(/EMPTY BODY, refused/.test(formatSimReport(run)), 'and is refused loudly — silently blanking a file is the worst outcome available');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[6] every switch reaches the prompt, so an A/B is one box\n');
+{
+  const grab = async (options) => { const ep = pair({ agent: () => 'Thinking.' }); await runSim({ adapter: ep, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1, ...options } }); return ep.seen.agent[0] || ''; };
+  ok(/<checkout path=/.test(await grab({ framing: 'repo' })) && /<read path=/.test(await grab({ framing: 'workspace' })), 'framing switches the verbs it is taught');
+  const own = await grab({ ownTools: true }), noOwn = await grab({ ownTools: false });
+  ok(/everything else is still yours/i.test(own), 'own-tools ON hands its environment back');
+  ok(/no filesystem or code environment of your own/i.test(noOwn), 'and OFF takes it away — the two sentences 8h compares');
+  ok(/escaped="no"/.test(await grab({ escapedFlag: true })) && !/escaped=/.test(await grab({ escapedFlag: false })), 'the escaped flag appears in the taught syntax only when demanded');
+  ok(Object.keys(SIM_DEFAULTS).length >= 7, `every switch has a default, so a run is reproducible from the options line alone (${Object.keys(SIM_DEFAULTS).length})`);
+  const rep = formatSimReport(await runSim({ adapter: pair({ agent: () => 'x' }), model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1, ownTools: false } }));
+  ok(/options: framing=repo own-tools=false/.test(rep), 'and the report prints them, so two transcripts can be compared without guessing what differed');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[7] the loop stops, and says why\n');
+{
+  const never = pair({ agent: () => 'Still thinking.' });
+  const run = await runSim({ adapter: never, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 3 } });
+  ok(run.done === false && /NOT done/.test(formatSimReport(run)), 'a run that never finishes says NOT done rather than reporting a pass');
+  ok(run.turns.filter((t) => t.who === 'agent').length <= 6, 'and the turn budget is honoured');
+  const quick = pair({ agent: () => 'All set.', devDone: 1 });
+  const qr = await runSim({ adapter: quick, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 9 } });
+  ok(qr.done === true && qr.doneAt === 1 && /DONE at turn 1/.test(formatSimReport(qr)), 'the developer saying DONE ends it, and the turn is recorded');
+  const ac = new AbortController(); ac.abort();
+  ok((await runSim({ adapter: pair({ agent: () => 'x' }), model: 'x', signal: ac.signal, options: { maxTurns: 5 } })).turns.length === 0, 'and an already-cancelled run makes no calls');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[8] the transcript is readable and carries nothing it should not\n');
+{
+  const ep = pair({ agent: () => 'ping someone.person@example.mil about it' });
+  const rep = formatSimReport(await runSim({ adapter: ep, model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1 } }));
+  ok(!/@example\.mil/.test(rep) && /\[id\]/.test(rep), 'an identifier is redacted before it can reach a screenshot');
+  ok(/── the repository at the end/.test(rep), 'the repository is printed at the end — the thing the whole run exists to produce');
+  ok(/\(untouched\)/.test(rep), 'with untouched files marked, so "it changed nothing" is visible rather than inferred');
+  const long = formatSimReport(await runSim({ adapter: pair({ agent: () => 'x'.repeat(3000) }), model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1 } }));
+  ok(/…\[\+\d+\]/.test(long), 'and a very long reply is clipped with the amount dropped stated, never silently');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[9] the sweep is thorough by REPEATS and TASKS, not by combinations\n');
+{
+  // Six switches is sixty-four combinations, and with an endpoint this intermittent that is sixty-four noisy
+  // single samples. The identity A/B reversed outright between two runs of three; escaping came back 4/4, 2/4 and
+  // 3/4 on identical requests. Repeats and more tasks are what make a difference believable.
+  const R = simRepo();
+  const repoOnly = () => pair({
+    agent: (n, c) => (/<read path=/.test(c) || /read-result/.test(c) ? 'I cannot do that.'
+      : /checkout-result/.test(c) ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="${sha(R['src/config.ts'])}" escaped="no">${R['src/config.ts'].replace('30_000', '60_000')}</commit></repo>`
+        : '<repo><checkout path="src/config.ts"/></repo>'),
+    devDone: 1,
+  });
+  const sweep = await runSimSweep({ adapter: repoOnly(), model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 2, reps: 3 } });
+  ok(sweep.reps === 3 && sweep.tasks.length === SIM_TASKS.length, `every arm runs every task, ${sweep.reps} times each (${sweep.tasks.length} tasks)`);
+  ok(sweep.cells.length === sweep.arms.length * sweep.tasks.length * sweep.reps, `which is ${sweep.cells.length} runs, not 64 single samples`);
+  ok(sweep.arms[0] === 'baseline', 'the baseline runs first, because everything else is measured against it');
+  const variant = sweep.cells.find((c) => c.label !== 'baseline');
+  const differs = Object.keys(sweep.base).filter((k) => k !== 'task' && variant.run.options[k] !== sweep.base[k]);
+  ok(differs.length === 1 && variant.label.startsWith(differs[0] + '='), `a variant differs from the baseline in exactly one switch (${variant.label})`);
+  ok(SIM_TASKS.every((t) => sweep.cells.some((c) => c.task === t.id)), 'and every task really ran');
+
+  const rep = formatSimSweepReport(sweep);
+  // THE VERDICT IS PER TASK. A baseline that scores 3/3 on a one-file edit and 0/3 on a two-file one is showing
+  // task DIFFICULTY, not noise — using that spread as the floor swallowed a real 3/3 -> 0/3 drop the first time
+  // this ran, which is the same class of mistake the identity A/B made.
+  ok(/OUTSIDE THE NOISE/.test(rep) && /framing=workspace: one-file -100%/.test(rep),
+    'a switch that kills a task it used to pass is flagged, per task, with the size of the drop');
+  ok(/noise floor is one rep \(33%\)/.test(rep), 'and the floor is one REP, because noise lives between repeats of the same cell');
+  ok(/each cell is  worked\/3/.test(rep), 'every cell is n-of-reps rather than a single yes/no');
+  ok(/NOBODY COMPLETED: two-file/.test(rep), 'a task no arm managed is called out — that column ranks nothing, and the task is what to look at');
+  ok((rep.match(/── the repository at the end/g) || []).length === 1, 'one transcript, not sixty — the table is the comparison');
+
+  // A switch that helps one task and hurts another is a real finding, and a different one from a clean win.
+  const cell = (label, task, won) => ({ label, task, run: { done: !!won }, outcome: { added: won, used: won, intact: true }, calls: 1 });
+  const mixed = { base: SIM_DEFAULTS, arms: ['baseline', 'x=1'], whys: {}, tasks: ['a', 'b'], reps: 3,
+    cells: [...[0, 1, 2].flatMap(() => [cell('baseline', 'a', true), cell('baseline', 'b', false), cell('x=1', 'a', false), cell('x=1', 'b', true)])] };
+  ok(/helps one, hurts another/.test(formatSimSweepReport(mixed)), 'and it is labelled as exactly that rather than averaged into a misleading zero');
+
+  const flat = await runSimSweep({ adapter: pair({ agent: () => 'All set.', devDone: 1 }), model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1, reps: 2 } });
+  ok(/NO SWITCH LANDED OUTSIDE THE NOISE/.test(formatSimSweepReport(flat)), 'a sweep where nothing moves says so');
+  ok(/pick the simplest/.test(formatSimSweepReport(flat)), 'and turns it into advice rather than a flat table');
+
+  const noDup = await runSimSweep({ adapter: pair({ agent: () => 'x', devDone: 1 }), model: 'x', options: { maxTurns: 1, reps: 1, pinSession: false } });
+  ok(!noDup.arms.includes('pinSession=false'), 'a switch already at the swept value is skipped rather than run against itself');
+  ok(SIM_SWEEP.every(([k]) => k in SIM_DEFAULTS), 'every swept switch is a real option, so a rename cannot leave a variant silently testing nothing');
+  const ac = new AbortController(); ac.abort();
+  ok((await runSimSweep({ adapter: pair({ agent: () => 'x' }), model: 'x', signal: ac.signal })).cells.length === 0, 'and a cancelled sweep runs nothing');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
