=== add test/test-skills-composition.mjs
new e65dfdeab9fccb67a18b1d57
+/**
+ * SKILLS BUILT OUT OF SKILLS — the shape a long process actually takes.
+ *
+ * The case this feature was asked for: a twelve-step run through a web app that the agent cannot get all the way
+ * through without breaking something in the middle. The answer is not one twelve-step skill — it is four short ones
+ * that call each other, each refusing to start unless the step before it really happened. What has to hold for that
+ * to be worth anything:
+ *   A. a top-level skill drives the chain, and each link's narration comes back in order.
+ *   B. a link REFUSES when its precondition is not met, in its own words — so an out-of-order call cannot half-run.
+ *   C. when a link fails inside a chain, the agent is told WHICH link stopped it — not just that something did.
+ *   D. the chain is one call in the record, and what it actually did is on disk.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-skills-composition.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, mkdir, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const RECORD_HEAD = '\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n';
+const resultOnly = (s) => { const i = String(s).indexOf(RECORD_HEAD); return i < 0 ? String(s) : String(s).slice(0, i); };
+
+// Four skills standing in for a run through a web app. Nothing here touches a browser — the point is the SHAPE:
+// each step leaves a trace the next step checks for, and the checks are about the process, never about the data.
+const SKILLS = {
+  app_login: `export const purpose = 'Signs in and leaves the app on the case list.';
+export const params = '{ user: string }';
+export const returns = '{ user: string } — who is signed in';
+export const notFor = 'Not for the production instance.';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(args.user, 'pass { user: "…" } — who to sign in as');
+  api.assert(!(await api.exists('session.json')), 'a session is already open — sign out first, or carry on with the one you have');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('signing in as ' + args.user);
+  await api.write('session.json', JSON.stringify({ user: args.user }));
+  return { user: args.user };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(await api.exists('session.json'), 'no session was left behind, so the sign-in did not take');
+}
+`,
+  app_open_case: `export const purpose = 'Opens a case and leaves the app on its detail page.';
+export const params = '{ caseId: string }';
+export const returns = '{ caseId: string }';
+export const notFor = 'Not for a closed case.';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(args.caseId, 'pass { caseId: "…" } — which case to open');
+  api.assert(await api.exists('session.json'), 'nobody is signed in — call app_login first');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('opening case ' + args.caseId);
+  await api.write('case-' + args.caseId + '.txt', 'open\\n');
+  return { caseId: args.caseId };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(await api.exists('case-' + args.caseId + '.txt'), 'the case page never came up');
+}
+`,
+  app_add_note: `export const purpose = 'Adds a note to the case that is open.';
+export const params = '{ caseId: string, note: string }';
+export const returns = '{ lines: number } — how many lines the case now has';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(args.caseId && args.note, 'pass { caseId, note }');
+  api.assert(await api.exists('case-' + args.caseId + '.txt'), 'case ' + args.caseId + ' is not open — call app_open_case first');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('adding a note to ' + args.caseId);
+  const f = 'case-' + args.caseId + '.txt';
+  const t = await api.read(f);
+  await api.write(f, t + 'note: ' + args.note + '\\n');
+  return { lines: (t + 'x').split('\\n').length };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  const t = await api.read('case-' + args.caseId + '.txt');
+  api.assert(t.includes('note: '), 'the note is not on the case after saving it');
+}
+`,
+  app_note_flow: `export const purpose = 'Signs in, opens a case and leaves a note on it.';
+export const params = '{ user: string, caseId: string, note: string }';
+export const returns = '{ caseId: string, lines: number }';
+export const notFor = 'Not for bulk notes — call it once per case.';
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('starting the note flow');
+  await api.app_login({ user: args.user });
+  const c = await api.app_open_case({ caseId: args.caseId });
+  const n = await api.app_add_note({ caseId: c.caseId, note: args.note });
+  return { caseId: c.caseId, lines: n.lines };
+}
+`,
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skcomp-'));
+  const proj = path.join(root, 'proj');
+  const sdir = path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'skills');
+  await mkdir(sdir, { recursive: true });
+  for (const [name, body] of Object.entries(SKILLS)) await writeFile(path.join(sdir, name + '.mjs'), body);
+  const prevHome = process.env.CB_HOME;
+  process.env.CB_HOME = path.join(root, 'home');
+  const ctx = () => ({ cwd: proj, projectDir: proj });
+  const run = async (code) => resultOnly(extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, ctx())));
+  const runFull = async (code) => extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, { ...ctx(), keepRecord: true }));
+  try {
+    // ── A. the chain runs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('A. four short skills, one call');
+    {
+      const full = await runFull('return await app_note_flow({ user: "tester", caseId: "123", note: "checked the fee" });');
+      ok(/"caseId": *"123"/.test(full), 'the flow returns what it promised');
+      const order = ['starting the note flow', 'signing in as tester', 'opening case 123', 'adding a note to 123']
+        .map((s) => full.indexOf('console: ' + s));
+      ok(order.every((i) => i >= 0), `every link narrated (${order.join(',')})`);
+      ok(order.every((v, i) => i === 0 || v > order[i - 1]), 'in the order they happened, so a long run can be followed');
+      ok((full.match(/^\[\d+\] app_/gm) || []).length === 1, 'and the whole chain is ONE call in the record, not four');
+      ok(/\[1\] app_note_flow\(user[:=]tester[ ,]+caseId[:=]123/.test(full), 'named with the arguments it was given');
+    }
+    {
+      ok((await readFile(path.join(proj, 'case-123.txt'), 'utf8')).includes('note: checked the fee'), 'and the work really landed');
+    }
+
+    // ── B. a link refuses out of order ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('B. a link refuses when its step has not happened');
+    {
+      const out = await run('return await app_add_note({ caseId: "999", note: "x" });');
+      ok(/DID NOT RUN/.test(out) && /case 999 is not open/.test(out), 'a note on a case that is not open is refused in the skill\'s own words');
+      ok(/call app_open_case first/.test(out), 'and says which skill to call instead — the agent can act on that');
+      ok(!/note: x/.test(await readFile(path.join(proj, 'case-123.txt'), 'utf8')), 'and nothing was written anywhere');
+    }
+    {
+      const out = await run('return await app_login({ user: "tester" });');
+      ok(/DID NOT RUN/.test(out) && /already open/.test(out), 'a second sign-in is refused — a re-runnable skill says so rather than doubling up');
+    }
+
+    // ── C. a failure INSIDE a chain names the link ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('C. which link stopped the chain');
+    {
+      await rm(path.join(proj, 'session.json'));   // the app "signed us out" between runs
+      const out = await run('return await app_note_flow({ user: "tester", caseId: "", note: "x" });');
+      ok(/app_note_flow/.test(out) && /app_open_case/.test(out), 'both the flow and the link that stopped it are named');
+      ok(/stopped at the skill it called/.test(out), 'the message says the chain stopped at a skill, not that the flow itself broke');
+      ok(/pass \{ caseId/.test(out), "carrying the inner skill's own message");
+      ok(/got as far as calling "app_open_case" and no further/.test(out), 'and says how far the flow got — which is the question being asked');
+      ok(/app_note_flow\.mjs/.test(out), 'and points at the flow to read');
+    }
+    {
+      // the chain's earlier steps DID happen — the agent must not be told nothing did
+      ok(await readFile(path.join(proj, 'session.json'), 'utf8').then(() => true).catch(() => false), 'the sign-in that succeeded before the failure stands');
+    }
+
+    // ── D. a fixed skill takes effect, and the flow completes ───────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('D. after the fix');
+    {
+      const out = await run('return await app_note_flow({ user: "tester", caseId: "456", note: "second pass" });');
+      ok(/DID NOT RUN/.test(out) && /already open/.test(out), 'the flow now trips on the session it left behind — which is the skill being honest, not a bug');
+      await rm(path.join(proj, 'session.json'));
+      const out2 = await run('return await app_note_flow({ user: "tester", caseId: "456", note: "second pass" });');
+      ok(/"caseId": *"456"/.test(out2), 'and with the state cleared it runs the whole chain again');
+      ok((await readFile(path.join(proj, 'case-456.txt'), 'utf8')).includes('second pass'), 'leaving the second case noted too');
+    }
+  } finally {
+    if (prevHome === undefined) delete process.env.CB_HOME; else process.env.CB_HOME = prevHome;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { try { await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200)); } }
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

=== add test/test-skills-e2e.mjs
new a96cb26212320e05d2e01aa4
+/**
+ * SKILLS + THE RUN RECORD, through the real UI and the real server.
+ *
+ * The unit tests prove the pieces; this proves the loop the feature exists for, in the app the developer actually
+ * uses: the agent's saved functions are listed in what the server SENDS the model, a snippet calls one, a skill the
+ * agent writes mid-session is listed and callable on the NEXT turn, and what a snippet did comes back to the model
+ * after its result — including a run that failed. A scripted model plays the agent's part so the checks are exact;
+ * the server, the project, the sandbox and the browser are all real.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-skills-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skl-home-'));   // isolate prefs AND the global skills folder
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { loadSkills, skillsDoc } = await import('../src/agent/skills.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29244;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// A skill an earlier session saved: it stamps a release into a file. The kind of thing that used to be worked out
+// again from scratch every time.
+const STAMP_SKILL = `export const purpose = 'Stamps a release number into RELEASE.txt for this project.';
+export const params = '{ release: string } — the release number to stamp';
+export const returns = '{ stamped: string } — what was written';
+export const notFor = 'Not for tagging git; it only writes the file.';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(args.release, 'pass { release: "3" } — the release to stamp');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('stamping release ' + args.release);
+  const line = 'release ' + args.release + '\\n';
+  await api.write('RELEASE.txt', line);
+  return { stamped: line.trim() };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(await api.exists('RELEASE.txt'), 'RELEASE.txt is not there after stamping, so the write did not land');
+}
+`;
+
+// The skill the agent writes DURING the session — the whole point of the feature. Written through a heredoc, the
+// way the model writes a file in a snippet.
+const SAVE_TURN = `Saving that as a skill so it is not worked out again.
+<remote-workspace><js>
+const body = <<<SKILL
+export const purpose = 'Counts the endpoints declared in app.js.';
+export const params = '{}';
+export const returns = '{ endpoints: number }';
+export const notFor = 'Not for routes registered at runtime.';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(await api.exists('app.js'), 'app.js is not there — this skill is about this project\\'s app.js');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  const t = await api.read('app.js');
+  api.log('scanning app.js for endpoints');
+  return { endpoints: (t.match(/req\\.url === /g) || []).length };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(typeof result.endpoints === 'number', 'the count did not come out as a number');
+}
+SKILL
+const w = await write(".code_boss/skills/count_endpoints.mjs", body);
+return { saved: w.ok };
+</js></remote-workspace>`;
+
+const TURNS = [
+  // 1. call the skill that already exists
+  `Stamping the release with the skill.
+<remote-workspace><js>const r = await stamp_release({ release: "3" }); console.log("stamped " + r.stamped); return r;</js></remote-workspace>`,
+  // 2. save a new skill
+  SAVE_TURN,
+  // 3. call the skill just saved — it must be bound on the very next turn
+  `Using it now.
+<remote-workspace><js>return await count_endpoints({});</js></remote-workspace>`,
+  // 4. a run that FAILS: the non-zero exit throws, and the record still says what got done first
+  `Checking the build.
+<remote-workspace><js>await write("checked.txt", "before the failure\\n"); console.log("wrote the marker, now the build"); await node(["-e", "console.error('BUILD_BROKE_HERE'); process.exit(7)"]); return "unreachable";</js></remote-workspace>`,
+  // 5. the skill refuses: its before-check fails and the agent is told nothing ran
+  `Trying it without the release.
+<remote-workspace><js>return await stamp_release({});</js></remote-workspace>`,
+  'All done. FINAL_ANSWER_MARKER',
+];
+
+const sent = [];   // what the server actually SENT the model, turn by turn
+let call = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    sent.push((req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n'));
+    const t = TURNS[Math.min(call++, TURNS.length - 1)];
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skl-proj-'));
+  const sdir = path.join(projectDir, '.code_boss', 'skills');
+  await mkdir(sdir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'app.js'), "const http = require('http');\nhttp.createServer((req, res) => {\n  if (req.url === '/') { res.end('home'); return; }\n  if (req.url === '/health') { res.end('ok'); return; }\n  res.end('404');\n}).listen(3000);\n");
+  await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'stamp_release.mjs'), STAMP_SKILL);
+  await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'not-a-name.mjs'), 'export default async function () { return 1; }\n');   // one broken file, deliberately
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    // Send one developer message; the scripted agent then works through all six turns.
+    await page.type('#input', 'Stamp release 3, then save what you worked out as a skill and use it.');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('FINAL_ANSWER_MARKER'), { timeout: 120000, polling: 500 });
+    ok(true, 'the session ran to its final answer through the real UI');
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(__dirname, 'skills-e2e.png') });
+
+    // ── what the SERVER SENT the model ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('\n[A] the roster reaches the model');
+    const first = sent[0] || '';
+    ok(/YOUR SKILLS/.test(first), 'the very first prompt carries the skills listing');
+    ok(/stamp_release\(\{ release: string \}\)/.test(first), 'with the skill shown as the call to write');
+    ok(/Stamps a release number/.test(first) && /NOT for: .*tagging git/.test(first), 'with what it is for and what it is not for');
+    ok(/not-a-name\.mjs/.test(first) && /could NOT be loaded/.test(first), 'and the broken file named, so it gets fixed rather than silently missed');
+    ok(/PREFER A SKILL/.test(first), 'and the instruction to prefer a skill over working it out again');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] a skill saved mid-session is listed on the next turn');
+    const afterSave = sent[2] || '';   // the prompt for turn 3, built after turn 2 saved the file
+    ok(/count_endpoints/.test(afterSave), 'the newly written skill is in the next prompt');
+    ok(/Counts the endpoints declared in app\.js/.test(afterSave), 'with the purpose it declared');
+    ok(!/count_endpoints/.test(first), 'and was genuinely not there before it was written');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] the record comes back to the model');
+    const afterStamp = sent[1] || '';
+    ok(/What the snippet did, in order:/.test(afterStamp), 'the snippet result is followed by the record');
+    ok(/\[1\] stamp_release\(release[:=]3\)/.test(afterStamp), 'the skill call is in it, with its arguments');
+    ok(/console: stamping release 3/.test(afterStamp), "the skill's own narration is in it");
+    ok(/console: stamped release 3/.test(afterStamp), "and the snippet's");
+    if (process.env.CB_DUMP) { const { writeFile: wf3 } = await import('node:fs/promises'); await wf3(path.join(__dirname, '_skills-e2e-turn4.txt'), (sent[3] || '').slice(-3000), 'utf8'); }
+    const afterFail = sent[4] || '';
+    ok(/exited with code 7/.test(afterFail) && /BUILD_BROKE_HERE/.test(afterFail), 'a failed run comes back with its exit code and the end of its stderr');
+    ok(/\[1\] write\(checked\.txt\)/.test(afterFail), 'and the record still shows what the snippet HAD done before it failed');
+    ok(/\[2\] node[^\n]*(FAILED|the error above)/.test(afterFail), 'with the failing call marked as the one that failed');
+    const failRec = afterFail.slice(afterFail.lastIndexOf('What the snippet did'));   // the failing turn's own record, last in this prompt
+    ok(!/\[3\] /.test(failRec), 'while the step after the failing one never ran — there is no third call');
+    const afterRefuse = sent[5] || '';
+    ok(/DID NOT RUN/.test(afterRefuse) && /pass \{ release/.test(afterRefuse), "a skill's before-check refusal says nothing ran, in the skill's own words");
+
+    // ── what really happened on disk ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('\n[D] the work is real');
+    ok((await readFile(path.join(projectDir, 'RELEASE.txt'), 'utf8')).trim() === 'release 3', 'the skill really wrote RELEASE.txt');
+    ok((await readFile(path.join(projectDir, 'checked.txt'), 'utf8')).includes('before the failure'), 'the write before the failing build really landed');
+    const saved = await readFile(path.join(sdir, 'count_endpoints.mjs'), 'utf8');
+    ok(/export default async function/.test(saved) && /export async function pre/.test(saved), 'the skill the agent saved is on disk with its body and its before-check');
+    const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir });
+    ok(l.list.some((e) => e.name === 'count_endpoints') && !l.broken.some((b) => /count_endpoints/.test(b.file)), 'and it loads cleanly');
+    ok(/last worked/.test(skillsDoc(l)), 'a skill that ran is now dated in the listing');
+    // the note is written after the call returns and deliberately not awaited by the run, so give it a moment
+    let runs = {};
+    for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
+      try { runs = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(sdir, '.last-run.json'), 'utf8')); } catch {}
+      if (runs.stamp_release && runs.count_endpoints) break;
+      await sleep(100);
+    }
+    void runs;
+    ok(runs.stamp_release && runs.count_endpoints, `both skills that completed are recorded; the refused call added nothing (${JSON.stringify(runs)})`);
+
+    // ── the developer's view ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('\n[E] the developer sees it');
+    const rows = await page.$$eval('#log .row.tool', (els) => els.length).catch(() => 0);
+    ok(rows >= 5, `each snippet is a tool row in the history (${rows})`);
+    // Open each snippet's RESULT row the way the developer does (a result row is clickable when it has content)
+    // and read what is inside: the record is not only for the model — it is what the developer expands to see what
+    // their agent's script actually did, and a skill's own narration is part of it.
+    const clicked = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      // on this line a snippet is one collapsed line in the chat: open each with a click on the line, then the js
+      // row's result row inside
+      [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .js-group:not(.open) > .js-summary')].forEach((x) => x.click());
+      const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .row-result')];
+      rows.forEach((r) => r.click());
+      return rows.length;
+    });
+    ok(clicked >= 3, `the snippets' result rows are expandable (${clicked} of them)`);
+    await sleep(800);
+    const opened = await page.evaluate(() => document.body.innerText);
+    if (process.env.CB_DUMP) { const { writeFile: wf } = await import('node:fs/promises'); await wf(path.join(__dirname, '_skills-e2e-dump.txt'), opened, 'utf8'); }
+    // on this line the developer's view of the record is one row per call under the snippet's line (the text form is the model's)
+    ok(/What the snippet did, in order:/.test(opened) || (await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .js-group .js-step').length)) >= 1, 'expanding one shows what the snippet did, call by call');
+    ok(/console: stamping release 3/.test(opened), "including the skill's own narration");
+    ok(/console: stamped release 3/.test(opened), "and the snippet's own console line");
+    ok(/\[1\] stamp_release/.test(opened), 'and the call, named the way the developer would name it');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no JS errors in the page (${errs.length}: ${errs.slice(0, 2).join('; ')})`);
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await sleep(300);
+    await cleanupDir(projectDir);
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/test-skills-maintainer-e2e.mjs
new eb0c3dc26da748220d3255ab
+/**
+ * THE SKILLS MAINTAINER through the real server and the real UI (SKILLS-MAINTAINER-SPEC.md). A scripted model plays
+ * the agent and the maintainer: a skill whose after-check is out of date fails in a run; after the run the maintainer
+ * reads what is new, fixes the skill, writes its tests and runs them; the fix lands; the next prompt says the skill
+ * last failed and was changed and not run for real since; the Skills tab shows it with Undo, and Undo puts the old
+ * file back; the Memory tab's Advanced view lists the skill. A run that only talks starts no pass.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-skills-maintainer-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skme-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29273;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const STALE = `export const purpose = 'Stamps a release number into the version file.';
+export const params = '{ release: string }';
+export async function pre(args, api) { api.assert(args.release, 'pass { release: "3" }'); }
+export default async function (args, api) { await api.write('VERSION.txt', 'release ' + args.release + '\\n'); return { stamped: 'release ' + args.release }; }
+export async function post(args, r, api) { api.assert(await api.exists('RELEASE.txt'), 'RELEASE.txt is not there after stamping'); }
+`;
+const FIXED = STALE.replace("api.exists('RELEASE.txt'), 'RELEASE.txt is not there after stamping'", "api.exists('VERSION.txt'), 'VERSION.txt is not there after stamping'");
+const TESTS = `export const tests = {
+  'stamps the version file': async (t) => {
+    const api = t.fakeApi({ write: async () => ({ ok: true }), exists: async (p) => p === 'VERSION.txt' });
+    t.equal((await t.skill({ release: '7' }, api)).stamped, 'release 7');
+  },
+  'refuses with no release': async (t) => { await t.rejects(() => t.skill({}, t.fakeApi()), /pass \\{ release/); },
+};
+`;
+
+const TURNS = [
+  'Stamping with the skill.\n<remote-workspace><js>try { await stamp_release({ release: "8" }); } catch (e) { console.log("skill said: " + String(e.message).slice(0, 160)); } return "tried";</js></remote-workspace>',
+  'The stamp landed in VERSION.txt; the skill\'s after-check looks for RELEASE.txt, which the app no longer writes. DONE_ONE',
+  'Nothing to run for that. DONE_TWO',
+];
+const MAINT = [
+  '<read name="stamp_release"/>',
+  `<write-skill name="stamp_release" why="its after-check looked for RELEASE.txt; the app writes VERSION.txt">${FIXED}</write-skill>\n<write-test name="stamp_release">${TESTS}</write-test>`,
+  '<run-tests name="stamp_release"/>',
+  '<done>stamp_release checks VERSION.txt now</done>',
+];
+const sent = [], maintSeen = [];
+let call = 0;
+const textOf = (req) => (req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n');
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) { sent.push(textOf(req)); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: TURNS[Math.min(call++, TURNS.length - 1)] } }] }; },
+  async complete(req) {
+    const t = textOf(req);
+    if (/You keep the skills of a coding agent/.test(t)) { maintSeen.push(t); return { choices: [{ message: { content: MAINT.shift() ?? '<nothing/>' } }] }; }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skme-proj-'));
+  const sdir = path.join(projectDir, '.code_boss', 'skills');
+  await mkdir(sdir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'stamp_release.mjs'), STALE);
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const errs = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1400, height: 1000 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input'), { timeout: 15000 });
+    await page.type('#input', 'Stamp release 8.');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('DONE_ONE'), { timeout: 60000, polling: 300 });
+
+    console.log('\n[1] the run log, and the pass after the run');
+    let runs = '';
+    for (let i = 0; i < 50 && !/"outcome":"post"/.test(runs); i++) { try { runs = await readFile(path.join(sdir, '.runs.jsonl'), 'utf8'); } catch {} await sleep(100); }
+    ok(/"name":"stamp_release","scope":"project","outcome":"post"/.test(runs) && /"keys":\["release"\]/.test(runs) && !/"8"|args|RELEASE\.txt/.test(runs), 'the failed call is logged at its after-check, with no values');
+    let code = '';
+    for (let i = 0; i < 200 && code !== FIXED; i++) { code = await readFile(path.join(sdir, 'stamp_release.mjs'), 'utf8'); await sleep(150); }
+    ok(code === FIXED, 'the maintainer fixed the skill after the run');
+    ok(maintSeen.length === 4 && /RELEASE\.txt is not there after stamping/.test(maintSeen[0]) && /called 1 time: 0 worked, 1 failed/.test(maintSeen[0]), `it read the run's failure and the skill's runs (${maintSeen.length} replies)`);
+    ok(/stamp_release: 2 passed, 0 failed/.test(maintSeen[3]), 'its tests ran and passed before it was applied');
+    ok((await readFile(path.join(sdir, 'tests', 'stamp_release.test.mjs'), 'utf8')) === TESTS, 'its tests are kept beside it');
+    const ms = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(sdir, '.maintainer.json'), 'utf8'));
+    ok(ms.changes.length === 1 && ms.changes[0].kind === 'changed' && ms.untried.stamp_release, 'the change is recorded, and the skill marked not run for real since');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] the Skills tab');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { setSidePanelCollapsed(false); } catch {} });
+    await page.waitForSelector('#tabbtn-plugin-_skills', { timeout: 8000 });
+    // (under the full suite's load a click on the tab was once lost - the pane stayed hidden: clicked until it shows)
+    const openSkillsTab = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { await page.click('#tabbtn-plugin-_skills'); for (let j = 0; j < 10; j++) { if (await page.evaluate(() => { const p = document.getElementById('pane-plugin-_skills'); return !!p && !p.classList.contains('hidden'); })) return; await sleep(200); } } };
+    await openSkillsTab();
+    const paneText = async () => page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('pane-plugin-_skills')?.innerText || '');
+    let t = '';
+    for (let i = 0; i < 60 && !/Undo the change/.test(t); i++) { t = await paneText(); await sleep(150); }
+    if (!/Undo the change/.test(t)) console.log('PANE: ' + JSON.stringify(t.slice(0, 600)) + ' ' + JSON.stringify(await page.evaluate(() => { const p = document.getElementById('pane-plugin-_skills'); return p ? { hidden: p.className, html: p.innerHTML.slice(0, 300) } : null; })));
+    ok(/stamp_release/.test(t) && /Stamps a release number/.test(t), 'the skill, and what it is for');
+    ok(/1 · 0 worked, 1 failed/.test(t) && /Last failed/.test(t) && /at its after-check/.test(t), 'its runs, and where it last failed');
+    ok(/changed: its after-check looked for RELEASE\.txt/.test(t) && /not run for real since/.test(t), 'its latest change, and that it has not run for real since');
+    ok(/Last looked .*stamp_release checks VERSION\.txt now/.test(t), 'what the maintainer last did');
+    ok(/Undo the change/.test(t) && /Retire/.test(t), 'Undo and Retire');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] the Memory tab, Advanced: the skills the agent is given');
+    await page.click('#tabbtn-plugin-home');
+    // (the pane is redrawn when a pass or the bookkeeper finishes: a button found a moment ago can be gone - clicked again)
+    const clickRetry = async (sel) => { for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { try { await page.waitForSelector(sel, { timeout: 4000 }); await page.click(sel); return; } catch { await sleep(300); } } };
+    let kl = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {

+    }

+    ok(!/stamp_release\(\)/.test(simple), 'and not in Simple');
+
+    console.log('\n[4] the next run: what the agent is told; a run that only talks starts no pass');
+    await page.type('#input', 'Thanks - anything else?');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('DONE_TWO'), { timeout: 60000, polling: 300 });
+    const p2 = sent[sent.length - 1] || '';

+    ok(/skills maintainer reads this conversation after the run and fixes the skill/.test(p2) && !/FIX THE FILE/.test(p2), 'and that a wrong skill is the maintainer\'s to fix');
+    await sleep(1500);
+    ok(maintSeen.length === 4, `no pass after a run with nothing run in it (${maintSeen.length} replies in all)`);
+
+    console.log('\n[5] Undo, from the Skills tab');
+    await openSkillsTab();
+    await page.waitForSelector('#pane-plugin-_skills button[data-ppid="sk:undo:1"]', { timeout: 8000 });
+    await page.click('#pane-plugin-_skills button[data-ppid="sk:undo:1"]');
+    let back = '';
+    for (let i = 0; i < 60 && back !== STALE; i++) { back = await readFile(path.join(sdir, 'stamp_release.mjs'), 'utf8'); await sleep(150); }
+    ok(back === STALE && !existsSync(path.join(sdir, 'tests', 'stamp_release.test.mjs')), 'the old file is back, and the tests it added gone');
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !/undone/.test(t); i++) { t = await paneText(); await sleep(150); }
+    ok(/\(undone\)/.test(t) && !/Undo the change/.test(t), 'the tab says it was undone');
+    ok(!errs.length, `no page errors (${errs.join(' | ')})`);
+    await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(__dirname, 'skills-maintainer-e2e.png') });
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    cleanupDir(projectDir); cleanupDir(process.env.CB_HOME);
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-skills.mjs
new c439e4f7d830f667f992bb6d
+/**
+ * SKILLS — the agent's own saved functions (src/agent/skills.mjs).
+ *
+ * The thing this feature exists to stop: the agent works a multi-step process out, gets it right, and loses it when
+ * the conversation moves on. A skill is one .mjs file holding one function, bound into <js> by its file name. These
+ * checks cover the four things that decide whether that is trustworthy:
+ *   A. LOADING — a good file loads; a bad one is reported as BROKEN with a reason the agent can act on, and never
+ *      takes the other skills down with it. Project beats global. An edited file is picked up.
+ *   B. RUNNING — args in, value out; and the three failures told APART: the before-check refused (nothing ran),
+ *      the body broke part-way, the after-check failed (it ran and did not achieve it). Skills call skills.
+ *   C. THE BOUNDARY — a skill is not a hole: its reads/writes are the same jailed functions the snippet gets, and
+ *      a non-zero exit throws inside a skill too.
+ *   D. WHAT THE MODEL READS — the listing, the broken files, the empty-folder invitation, and the live tail.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-skills.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, mkdir, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const { loadSkills, skillsDoc, skillDirs, runSkill, noteSkillRun, skillsOn, SKILLS_DIR_REL, SKILL_BRIEF } = await import('../src/agent/skills.mjs');
+const { execute, extractContent } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+const { buildLiveTail } = await import('../src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const RECORD_HEAD = '\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:\n';
+const resultOnly = (s) => { const i = String(s).indexOf(RECORD_HEAD); return i < 0 ? String(s) : String(s).slice(0, i); };
+
+// A skill file, written the way the agent writes one.
+const SKILL = {
+  count_lines: `
+export const purpose = 'Counts the lines in a project file.';
+export const params = '{ file: string } — project-relative path';
+export const returns = '{ lines: number }';
+export const notFor = 'Not for binary files, and it does not create the file.';
+export async function pre(args, api) {
+  api.assert(args.file, 'pass { file: "path" } — the file to count');
+  api.assert(await api.exists(args.file), args.file + ' is not there, so nothing was counted');
+}
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('counting ' + args.file);
+  const t = await api.read(args.file);
+  return { lines: t.split('\\n').length };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(result.lines > 0, 'came out as zero lines, which cannot be right for a file that exists');
+}
+`,
+  // built on another skill — the shape a long process is meant to take
+  describe_file: `
+export const purpose = 'One line about a file, built on count_lines.';
+export const params = '{ file: string }';
+export const returns = '{ text: string }';
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  api.log('describe_file is calling count_lines');
+  const c = await api.count_lines({ file: args.file });
+  return { text: args.file + ' has ' + c.lines + ' lines' };
+}
+`,
+  // the after-check fails although the body DID its work — the file is really written
+  half_done: `
+export const purpose = 'Writes a file and then claims something untrue about it.';
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  await api.write('made-by-skill.txt', 'written before the check failed\\n');
+  return { wrote: 'made-by-skill.txt' };
+}
+export async function post(args, result, api) {
+  api.assert(false, 'the endpoint never came up');
+}
+`,
+  breaks: `
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  await api.read('definitely-not-here.txt');
+  return 'unreachable';
+}
+`,
+  reads_outside: `
+export default async function (args, api) { return await api.read(args.path); }
+`,
+  runs_node: `
+export default async function (args, api) {
+  if (args.allowFail) return await api.node(['-e', 'process.exit(4)'], { allowFail: true });
+  return await api.node(['-e', 'process.exit(4)']);
+}
+`,
+  no_args: `
+export default async function (args, api) { return { got: JSON.stringify(args) }; }
+`,
+  as_object: `
+export default { purpose: 'Declared as an object instead of a bare function.', async run(args, api) { return 'object form works'; } };
+`,
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-skills-'));
+  const proj = path.join(root, 'proj');
+  const home = path.join(root, 'home');                       // stands in for the developer's home (CB_HOME)
+  const sdir = path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'skills');
+  const gdir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'skills');
+  await mkdir(sdir, { recursive: true });
+  await mkdir(gdir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(root, 'SECRET.txt'), 'PII: 555-12-3456');   // OUTSIDE the project
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'f.txt'), 'a\nb\nc\n');
+  const put = (name, body = SKILL[name]) => writeFile(path.join(sdir, name + '.mjs'), body);
+  const prevHome = process.env.CB_HOME;
+  process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+  const ctxOf = (extra = {}) => ({ cwd: proj, projectDir: proj, ...extra });
+  const run = async (code, extra = {}) => resultOnly(extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, ctxOf(extra))));
+  const runFull = async (code, extra = {}) => extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, { ...ctxOf(extra), keepRecord: true }));
+  try {
+    // ── A. LOADING ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('A. loading');
+    {
+      const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj });
+      ok(l.list.length === 0 && l.broken.length === 0, 'an empty folder is no skills and no complaints');
+      ok(skillDirs({ projectDir: proj }).map((d) => d.scope).join(',') === 'project,global', 'the project folder is looked at before the global one');
+    }
+    await put('count_lines');
+    {
+      const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj });
+      ok(l.list.length === 1 && l.list[0].name === 'count_lines', 'a skill is named by its FILE, not by anything inside it');
+      const e = l.list[0];
+      ok(e.purpose.startsWith('Counts') && e.params.includes('file: string') && e.returns.includes('lines') && e.notFor.includes('binary'), 'its four documentation exports are read');
+      ok(typeof e.run === 'function' && typeof e.pre === 'function' && typeof e.post === 'function', 'the body and both checks are picked up');
+      ok(e.rel === '.code_boss/skills/count_lines.mjs', 'it carries the path to read its code');
+    }
+    await writeFile(path.join(gdir, 'from_home.mjs'), "export const purpose = 'A skill that lives in the developer\\'s home.';\nexport default async function () { return 'global'; }\n");
+    {
+      const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj });
+      ok(l.list.some((e) => e.name === 'from_home' && e.scope === 'global'), 'a skill in the global folder loads too, marked as global');
+      ok(await run('return await from_home();') === 'global', 'and is callable');
+    }
+    await writeFile(path.join(gdir, 'count_lines.mjs'), "export const purpose = 'the global one';\nexport default async function () { return 'GLOBAL COPY'; }\n");
+    {
+      const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj });
+      ok(l.list.filter((e) => e.name === 'count_lines').length === 1, 'a name that exists in both folders is loaded once');
+      ok(l.list.find((e) => e.name === 'count_lines').scope === 'project', 'and the PROJECT one wins — a skill about this app beats a general one');
+    }
+    await rm(path.join(gdir, 'count_lines.mjs'));
+    {
+      // one bad file must never take the good ones down
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'has-a-dash.mjs'), 'export default async function () { return 1; }\n');
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'throws_on_import.mjs'), 'throw new Error("boom at import time");\nexport default async function () {};\n');
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'no_default.mjs'), 'export const purpose = "x";\nexport async function helper() {}\n');
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'read.mjs'), 'export default async function () { return "shadow"; }\n');
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'notes.txt'), 'not a skill');
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, '.hidden.mjs'), 'export default async function () {};\n');
+      const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj, reserved: new Set(['read', 'write', 'git']) });
+      const why = (f) => (l.broken.find((b) => b.file.endsWith(f)) || {}).why || '';
+      ok(l.list.some((e) => e.name === 'count_lines'), 'the good skills still load');
+      ok(/legal JavaScript function name/.test(why('has-a-dash.mjs')), 'a file name that could never be called says so');
+      ok(/failed to load: .*boom at import time/.test(why('throws_on_import.mjs')), 'a module that throws on import names the error');
+      ok(/no default export/.test(why('no_default.mjs')), 'a file with no default export is told what to export');
+      ok(/already the name of a built-in/.test(why('read.mjs')), 'a skill that would shadow read() is refused and told to rename');
+      ok(!l.list.some((e) => e.name === 'notes' || e.name === 'hidden'), 'a .txt and a dotfile are not skills');
+      ok(l.broken.length === 4, `exactly the four broken files are reported (${l.broken.length})`);
+      for (const f of ['has-a-dash.mjs', 'throws_on_import.mjs', 'no_default.mjs', 'read.mjs']) await rm(path.join(sdir, f));
+      await rm(path.join(sdir, 'notes.txt')); await rm(path.join(sdir, '.hidden.mjs'));
+    }

+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 12));

+      ok(await run('return await edited();') === 'second', 'and an edit to it is picked up, not served from a module cache');
+      await rm(path.join(sdir, 'edited.mjs'));

+      ok(await run('return typeof count_lines;') === 'THREW: ' || /is not defined/.test(await run('return await count_lines({file:"f.txt"});')), 'and no skill is bound in the sandbox');
+      delete process.env.CB_SKILLS;
+      ok(skillsOn(), 'and it is on again without it');
+    }

+    console.log('B. running');
+    {

+    }

+      ok(/DID NOT RUN/.test(out) && /pass \{ file/.test(out), 'a failed before-check says the skill DID NOT RUN, with the message the skill wrote');
+      ok(/nothing to undo/.test(out), 'and says there is nothing to undo — the thing the agent would otherwise guess at');
+      ok(/count_lines\.mjs/.test(out), 'and points at the file so the agent can read what it wanted');

+    }

+      ok(/still changed/.test(out) && /fix the skill/.test(out), 'and that its effect stands — the opposite advice from a failed before-check');
+      const made = await readFile(path.join(proj, 'made-by-skill.txt'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      ok(made.includes('written before the check failed'), 'and the work it did really is on disk');
+    }
+    {
+      await put('breaks');
+      const out = await run('return await breaks({});');
+      ok(/failed part-way through/.test(out), 'a skill that throws in its body is reported as failing part-way');
+      ok(/may have changed things/.test(out), 'which is not the same claim as either check failing');
+    }
+    {
+      await put('describe_file');
+      const out = await run('return await describe_file({ file: "f.txt" });');

+    }

+      ok(/\{\}/.test(await run('return await no_args("a string");')), 'and a non-object argument is not passed through as one');
+    }
+    {
+      // the date a skill last worked — what makes a stale skill visibly stale
+      await noteSkillRun(proj, 'count_lines', new Date(2026, 0, 2));

+      ok(l.lastRuns.count_lines === '2026-01-02', 'the day a skill last worked is remembered');

+      const l2 = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj });
+      const today = new Date();
+      const ymd = `${today.getFullYear()}-${String(today.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(today.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
+      ok(l2.lastRuns.count_lines === ymd, 'a successful call updates it to today');
+      const before = JSON.stringify(l2.lastRuns);
+      await run('return await count_lines({});');            // refused by its before-check
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
+      ok(JSON.stringify((await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj })).lastRuns) === before, 'a skill that was REFUSED does not get credit for working');
+      ok(!(await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj })).list.some((e) => e.name === 'last-run' || e.name === '.last-run'), 'the bookkeeping file is not itself listed as a skill');
+    }
+
+    // ── C. THE BOUNDARY ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('C. a skill reaches what a snippet reaches');
+    {
+      await put('reads_outside');
+      const outside = path.join(root, 'SECRET.txt');
+      const out = await run(`return await reads_outside({ path: ${JSON.stringify(outside)} });`);
+      ok(/555-12-3456/.test(out), 'a skill reads a file outside the project, as a snippet does - the container decides what is reachable');
+    }
+    {
+      await put('runs_node');
+      const out = await run('return await runs_node({});');
+      ok(/exited with code 4/.test(out), 'a non-zero exit throws inside a skill too');
+      ok(/"code": *4/.test(await run('return await runs_node({ allowFail: true });')), 'and {allowFail: true} still works from inside a skill');
+    }
+    {

+      const w = await run('const r = await write(".code_boss/skills/written_by_agent.mjs", "export default async function () { return 42; }\\n"); return r.ok === true;');
+      ok(w === 'true', 'the agent can WRITE a skill file itself');

+      ok(/Counts the lines in a project file/.test(await run('return await read(".code_boss/skills/count_lines.mjs");')), 'and read a skill back to see what it does');

+      ok(/last worked \d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(doc), 'and when it last worked');

+      ok(/PREFER A SKILL/.test(doc), 'and the listing tells the model to prefer a skill to working it out again');
+      ok(/skillBrief\(\)/.test(doc), 'and how to save a new one');
+      // MEASURED: a description of what is worth saving is not a trigger — the model agreed with the old wording
+      // and saved nothing across five live tasks, one of them explicitly recurring. The two signals are named now.
+      ok(/the developer says the work recurs/i.test(doc) && /every release/i.test(doc), 'the listing names the developer saying the work RECURS as a moment to save');
+      ok(/took more than one attempt/i.test(doc), 'and getting a multi-step process working the hard way as the other');

+      ok(/is live and callable/.test(fixed), 'and an EDIT that repairs a skill says so on the spot');
+    }
+    // ── E3. HOW DO YOU TRY THE ONE YOU JUST WROTE? ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

+    // `.default`, hand it a mock api. Every one of those fails with a parser error about the import, which says

+      ok(/Do not import it, do not use \.default, and do not mock api/.test(wrote), 'and names the three wrong instincts by name');

+    }

+    console.log('E. living with a folder that only grows');

+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, '_shared.mjs'), "export const ENDPOINT_RE = /req\.url === /g;\nexport const twice = (n) => n * 2;\n");
+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'uses_helper.mjs'), "import { twice } from './_shared.mjs';\nexport const purpose = 'Uses a helper module.';\nexport default async function (args, api) { return { got: twice(21) }; }\n");
+      const l = await loadSkills({ projectDir: proj });

+      ok(l.list.some((e) => e.name === 'uses_helper'), 'and the skill beside it still loads');
+      ok(/"got": *42/.test(await run('return await uses_helper({});')), 'a skill can import a helper module directly');
+    }

+      ok(doc.length < 4000, `and the whole section stays small (${doc.length} chars)`);

+      ok(!/are named only/.test(full) && full.length > doc.length, 'while the default budget shows them all in full');
+    }

+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'loops.mjs'), "export const purpose = 'Calls itself.';\nexport default async function (args, api) { return await api.loops({}); }\n");
+      process.env.CB_SKILL_CALL_CEILING = '6';
+      const out = await run('return await loops({});');
+      delete process.env.CB_SKILL_CALL_CEILING;
+      ok(/more than 6 skill calls/.test(out), 'a skill cycle is stopped by the per-snippet ceiling');
+      ok(/calling itself/.test(out) && /break the cycle/.test(out), 'and the message names the likely cause and the fix');
+      await rm(path.join(sdir, 'loops.mjs'));
+    }
+    {
+      // several skills at once: the ceiling must not fire on legitimate parallel work
+      const out = await run('const r = await Promise.all([count_lines({file:"f.txt"}), count_lines({file:"f.txt"}), uses_helper({}), no_args()]); return r.length;');
+      ok(out.trim() === '4', 'four skills called in parallel all complete');

+      await writeFile(path.join(sdir, 'via_plugin.mjs'), "export const purpose = 'Calls a plugin tool.';\nexport default async function (args, api) { return await api.echo_tool({ x: 7 }); }\n");

+      const out = resultOnly(extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code: 'return await via_plugin({});' }, extraTools, ctxOf())));
+      ok(/echo:\{"x":7\}/.test(out), 'a skill can call an installed plugin tool by its name');

+      ok(await run('return await was_broken();') === 'fixed', 'and runs, with no restart');
+    }
+  } finally {
+    if (prevHome === undefined) delete process.env.CB_HOME; else process.env.CB_HOME = prevHome;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { try { await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); break; } catch { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200)); } }
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

=== add test/test-snapshot-bounds.mjs
new 6ded44fe39bc6b1a5edbf4d2
+/**
+ * The ~24h OOM guard (server.mjs). session.events lives for the whole process lifetime, so:
+ *   (1) it is BOUNDED by a ring cap (EVENTS_MAX / CB_EVENTS_MAX) — memory is O(1) in uptime, not O(LLM calls);
+ *   (2) each event's big request/response is TRUNCATED in the snapshot — the request carries the WHOLE growing
+ *       conversation and was the single largest retained field (the leak that OOM'd the box after a busy ~24h).
+ * A provided adapter is wrapped to emit onTransaction, so a mock adapter still drives real logEvent transactions.
+ * Run: node test/test-snapshot-bounds.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bounds-home-'));   // its own home: never the developer's ~/.code_boss
+process.env.CB_EVENTS_MAX = '3';   // tiny cap so a handful of messages exercises the ring
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const BIG = 'x'.repeat(60000);   // >20k so it MUST be truncated in the snapshot
+const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'answer ' + BIG } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+
+async function main() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bounds-'));
+  const PORT = 29471, BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const send = (text) => fetch(BASE + '/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+  const waitIdle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) { const s = (await (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state')).json()).status; if (s === 'idle' || s === 'done') return; await sleep(50); } };
+  try {
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { await send('question ' + i + ' ' + BIG); await waitIdle(); }   // big request too
+    const snap = await (await fetch(BASE + '/api/snapshot')).json();
+    const events = snap.events || [];
+
+    // (1) the ring cap
+    ok(events.length <= 3, `session.events is CAPPED at EVENTS_MAX=3 (got ${events.length} after 6 big messages)`);
+
+    // (2) truncation — the big request/response never survive verbatim in the snapshot
+    const tx = events.filter((e) => e.request || e.response);
+    ok(tx.length > 0, `at least one transaction event was recorded (${tx.length})`);
+    const bigRaw = (v) => { try { return v != null && JSON.stringify(v).length > 25000; } catch { return false; } };
+    ok(!tx.some((e) => bigRaw(e.request)), 'no event retains a >25k raw REQUEST (the OOM driver — truncated)');
+    ok(!tx.some((e) => bigRaw(e.response)), 'no event retains a >25k raw response (truncated)');
+    ok(tx.some((e) => (e.request && e.request.truncated) || (e.response && e.response.truncated)), 'the big request/response is marked {truncated:true} in the snapshot');
+    // The truncation must PRESERVE the request shape — request-inspecting diagnostics read request.messages.
+    const truncReqs = tx.map((e) => e.request).filter((r) => r && r.truncated);
+    ok(truncReqs.length > 0 && truncReqs.every((r) => Array.isArray(r.messages)), 'the truncated request KEEPS messages[] (so request-inspecting diagnostics still work)');
+  } finally { server.close(); await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/test-snapshot-query-input.mjs
new a3c2c1f34e560e14ee849ce5
+/**
+ * Hardening for GET /api/queries grid-dimension input (found by the puppeteer
+ * edge-case hunt). The handler read cols/rows as `Number(x) || default`, which
+ * let through:
+ *   - huge values (cols=rows=99999 -> a ~10^10-cell Array allocation that
+ *     OOM-ABORTED THE WHOLE PROCESS — an unauthenticated single-GET DoS),
+ *   - negatives (new Array(-5) -> RangeError -> HTTP 500 + stack leak),
+ *   - non-integers (new Array(1.5) -> RangeError -> HTTP 500).
+ * The fix clamps both to a positive-integer range. Note: this test runs the
+ * server IN-PROCESS, so before the fix the huge-dims case would crash the test
+ * process itself (exit 134) — a self-proving regression.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-snapshot-query-input.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(c, l) { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } }
+async function q(port, qs) {
+  const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/queries?${qs}`);
+  let body = null; try { body = await r.json(); } catch { /* */ }
+  return { status: r.status, body };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const PORT = 28993;
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-snapq-'));
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, initialProject: dir, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }],
+    adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] } } },
+  });
+  try {
+    // Huge dims: must NOT OOM-crash the process (that would kill THIS test) and
+    // must return 200 with clamped dimensions.
+    const huge = await q(PORT, 'ids=1&cols=99999&rows=99999');
+    ok(huge.status === 200, `huge cols/rows -> 200, no OOM crash (got ${huge.status})`);
+    ok(huge.body && huge.body.pageCols <= 32 && huge.body.pageRows <= 32 && huge.body.pageCols >= 1, `huge dims clamped to <=32 (${huge.body?.pageCols}x${huge.body?.pageRows})`);
+
+    // Negative cols: used to RangeError -> 500.
+    const neg = await q(PORT, 'ids=1&cols=-5&rows=8');
+    ok(neg.status === 200, `negative cols -> 200, no RangeError 500 (got ${neg.status})`);
+
+    // Non-integer cols: used to RangeError -> 500.
+    const frac = await q(PORT, 'ids=1&cols=1.5&rows=8');
+    ok(frac.status === 200, `non-integer cols -> 200 (got ${frac.status})`);
+
+    // Garbage / empty: falls back to the 12x24 defaults.
+    const junk = await q(PORT, 'ids=1&cols=abc&rows=');
+    ok(junk.status === 200 && junk.body?.pageCols === 12 && junk.body?.pageRows === 24,
+      `garbage cols/rows -> default 12x24 (got ${junk.body?.pageCols}x${junk.body?.pageRows})`);
+
+    // A normal request still works.
+    const good = await q(PORT, 'ids=1,24&cols=12&rows=8');
+    ok(good.status === 200 && Array.isArray(good.body?.tiles) && good.body.tiles.length === 2, `normal request still works (${good.body?.tiles?.length} tiles)`);
+
+    // The server is still alive + responsive after all the abuse.
+    const alive = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/diagnostics`);
+    ok(alive.status === 200, 'server still responsive after malicious queries');
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    // Retrying: the server's debounced conversation save can still be writing under <dir>/.code_boss for a beat
+    // after close, and a bare rm then fails ENOTEMPTY (the suite's recurring "flake" on this probe).
+    await cleanupDir(dir);
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * RED IS THE SCRIPT FAILED; GREEN IS IT FINISHED (the developer, 2026-10-30: "only red if the script throws an error that we
+ * catch. If there are failed tools, it's fine" - "if an agent's snippet doesn't catch a failed tool's exception the bullet
+ * should be red" - "So red is the script failed, green is it finished normally (maybe recovering from tool failures)").
+ * Real server and UI, a scripted agent:
+ *   1. a failed call it does not catch - the snippet is red, and so is that call's line
+ *   2. its own code throws - red
+ *   3. a failed call it catches, and it finishes - green; the call's line is muted ("the snippet went on"), not red
+ *   4. one failed call caught, a later one not - red; both calls red (in a snippet that failed, they all stay red)
+ *   5. parallel() fails on two reads while two others finish - red, and both failed reads (live, 2026-10-30: they were
+ *      drawn muted, because the last call to end was a read that worked)

+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-snippet-error-bullet-e2e.mjs

+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });

+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.getElementById('input') && typeof _initialized !== 'undefined' && _initialized === true, { timeout: 20000 });
+    const say = async (text, done) => {
+      await page.type('#input', text); await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+      await page.waitForFunction((d) => document.body.innerText.includes(d), { timeout: 30000 }, done);
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));

+      const own = [...document.querySelectorAll(`#log .row.tool[data-call-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`)].filter((r) => !r.classList.contains('js-step'));
+      const grp = document.querySelector(`#log .js-group[data-call-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`);
+      const steps = grp ? [...grp.querySelectorAll('.js-step')].sort((a, b) => Number(a.dataset.step) - Number(b.dataset.step)).map((s) => (s.classList.contains('tool-err') ? 'red' : s.classList.contains('tool-caught') ? 'caught' : 'ok')) : [];
+      const red = grp ? grp.querySelectorAll('.js-summary .js-failed-part').length : 0, muted = grp ? grp.querySelectorAll('.js-summary .js-caught-part').length : 0;
+      return { red: own.length > 0 && own.some((r) => r.classList.contains('tool-err')), steps: steps.join(','), struckRed: red, struckMuted: muted };
+    }), ids);
+    await say('Run the failing check.', 'TURN_ONE_DONE');
+    await say('Try the other one.', 'TURN_TWO_DONE');
+    await say('And catch it this time.', 'TURN_THREE_DONE');
+    await say('Now both.', 'TURN_FOUR_DONE');
+    await say('Read them all at once.', 'TURN_FIVE_DONE');
+    const st = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json();
+    const ids = st.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'js').map((e) => e.callId);
+    ok(ids.length === 5, `five snippets ran (${ids.length})`);
+    const check = (v, when) => {
+      ok(v[0] && v[0].red && v[0].steps === 'red', `${when}1: a failed call it did not catch - red, and that call red (${JSON.stringify(v[0])})`);
+      ok(v[1] && v[1].red, `${when}2: its own code threw - red`);
+      ok(v[2] && v[2].struckRed === 0 && v[2].struckMuted === 1, `${when}3: its line strikes the caught call through, muted - nothing red on it (${JSON.stringify(v[2])})`);
+      ok(v[3] && v[3].struckRed >= 1 && v[3].struckMuted === 0, `${when}4: a snippet that failed: nothing on its line is muted (${JSON.stringify(v[3])})`);
+      ok(v[2] && !v[2].red && v[2].steps === 'caught,ok', `${when}3: it caught a failed call and finished - green; that call muted, not red (${JSON.stringify(v[2])})`);
+      ok(v[3] && v[3].red && v[3].steps === 'red,red', `${when}4: one caught, the next not - the snippet failed, so both stay red (${JSON.stringify(v[3])})`);
+      ok(v[4] && v[4].red && v[4].steps === 'red,red,ok,ok', `${when}5: parallel() failed on two reads while the others finished - red, and both failed reads red (${JSON.stringify(v[4])})`);
+    };
+    check(await look(ids), '');
+    await page.reload();
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('TURN_FIVE_DONE'), { timeout: 20000 });
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));

+    ok(!errs.length, `no page errors (${errs.join(' | ')})`);
+  } finally {

=== add test/test-snippet-jobs.mjs
new 2ddc24c38b6e146bb1397f4d
+/**
+ * SNIPPETS AS JOBS — background, the write queue, read mode, the developer's ⇱, kill (build 1964).
+ *
+ * In production the agent always writes snippets, and ⇱ could only adopt the PROCESS a snippet was waiting on —
+ * a snippet waiting on a plugin call, a curl or a sleep could not be backgrounded at all. Now every snippet is a
+ * job (snippet-jobs.mjs):
+ *   [1] <js background="true"> returns at once; the result (with the record) is delivered when it finishes
+ *   [2] every snippet starts at once; its first call that changes something takes the project's write slot — held by
+ *       another snippet, it waits there (moving to the background) and runs when that one finishes, in order
+ *   [3] reads never wait (read, list, git status); a process counts as a change and waits; parallel changes in ONE
+ *       snippet do not wait on each other
+ *   [4] ⇱ on a RUNNING foreground snippet (here: one waiting on a sleep, not a process) moves it to the
+ *       background: the call returns "BACKGROUNDED", the run ends (the developer's turn), the result comes later
+ *   [5] kill_background("js-N") stops a background snippet; the delivery says it was stopped
+ *   [6] Esc stops a FOREGROUND snippet, and does NOT stop one already in the background
+ * Run: node test/test-snippet-jobs.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+import { setSnippetJobDoneHandler, backgroundSnippetCall, listSnippetJobs } from '../src/agent/snippet-jobs.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+const delivered = [];
+setSnippetJobDoneHandler((job) => delivered.push({ id: job.id, status: job.result.status, content: job.result.content, at: Date.now() }));
+const waitDelivered = async (n, ms = 15000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (delivered.length < n && Date.now() - t0 < ms) await sleep(50); return delivered.length >= n; };
+
+async function run(dir, replies, { signal, onEvent } = {}) {
+  let i = 0;
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)] } }] }; } };
+  const events = [];
+  const result = await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 't', signal: signal || { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'go' }], projectDir: dir,
+    requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => { events.push(e); onEvent?.(e); }, onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {} });
+  return { events, result, results: events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.name === 'js_eval') };
+}
+const js = (code, attrs = '') => `<remote-workspace><js${attrs}>${code}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-jobs-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'a.txt'), 'hello');
+try { execFileSync('git', ['init', '-q'], { cwd: dir }); } catch {}
+
+console.log('\n[1] background="true"');
+{
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const { results } = await run(dir, [js('await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200)); await write("bg1.txt", "from the background"); return "BG1_DONE"', ' background="true"'), 'Started it.\n' + C]);
+  const r = results[0];
+  ok(r && /Started in the background as js-\d+\./.test(r.content) && Date.now() - t0 < 1100, `the call returned at once (${Date.now() - t0}ms): ${String(r && r.content).slice(0, 70)}`);
+  ok(r && r.background && /^js-\d+$/.test(r.background), `the result carries the job id (${r && r.background})`);
+  ok(await waitDelivered(1), 'the result was delivered when it finished');
+  const d = delivered[0];
+  ok(d && d.status === 'ok' && /^BG1_DONE/.test(d.content) && /What the snippet did/.test(d.content) && /write\(bg1\.txt\)/.test(d.content), `with the value and the record (${String(d && d.content).slice(0, 60).replace(/\n/g, ' ')})`);
+  ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'bg1.txt')), 'and its write happened');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] the write slot: reads run at once; the first change waits for the snippet changing files');
+{
+  delivered.length = 0;
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const { results } = await run(dir, [
+    // holds the slot from its first write, for ~1.5 s
+    js('await write("q.txt", "first-start"); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500)); await write("q.txt", "first"); return "FIRST"', ' background="true"'),
+    // no attribute: its read runs at once; its write waits
+    js('const t = await read("a.txt"); await write("q.txt", "second"); return "SECOND " + t'),
+    'Both sent.\n' + C,
+  ]);
+  const second = results[1];
+  ok(second && /^WAITING, in the background as js-\d+/.test(second.content) && second.background && Date.now() - t0 < 1400, `at its first change it moved to the background to wait (${Date.now() - t0}ms): ${String(second && second.content).slice(0, 60)}`);
+  ok(second && /read\(a\.txt\) · \d+ms → hello/.test(second.content), 'its read had already run, and is in what it reports so far');
+  ok(await waitDelivered(2), 'both were delivered');
+  ok(delivered[0] && /^FIRST/.test(delivered[0].content) && delivered[1] && /^SECOND hello/.test(delivered[1].content), `in order (${delivered.map((d) => d.content.split('\n')[0]).join(', ')})`);
+  ok((await readFile(path.join(dir, 'q.txt'), 'utf8')) === 'second', 'the waiting write ran AFTER the first snippet finished');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] reads never wait; a process does');
+{
+  delivered.length = 0;
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const { results } = await run(dir, [
+    js('await write("r.txt", "x"); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2000)); return "SLOW_WRITE"', ' background="true"'),
+    js('const t = await read("a.txt"); const g = await git(["status", "--short"]); const l = await list("."); return "READ " + t + " git " + (g.code === 0) + " files " + l.length'),
+    js('await node(["-e", "console.log(1)"]); return "RAN NODE"'),
+    'Done.\n' + C,
+  ]);
+  const [, readR, nodeR] = results;
+  ok(readR && /^READ hello git true files \d+/.test(readR.content) && !readR.background && Date.now() - t0 < 1900, `a snippet that only reads (read, git status, list) finished at once in the foreground, beside the write (${String(readR && readR.content).split('\n')[0]}, ${Date.now() - t0}ms)`);
+  ok(nodeR && /^WAITING/.test(nodeR.content), 'a process counts as a change: node() waited for the slot');
+  ok(await waitDelivered(2) && delivered.some((d) => /^RAN NODE/.test(d.content)), 'and ran when the slot freed');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3b] two changes in one snippet (parallel) do not wait on each other');
+{
+  const { results } = await run(dir, [js('const r = await parallel({ a: () => write("p1.txt", "1"), b: () => write("p2.txt", "2") }); return "BOTH " + r.a.ok + " " + r.b.ok'), 'ok\n' + C]);
+  ok(results[0] && /^BOTH true true/.test(results[0].content) && !results[0].background, `finished in the foreground (${String(results[0] && results[0].content).split('\n')[0]})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] ⇱ on a running foreground snippet');
+{
+  delivered.length = 0;
+  let pressed = null;
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const { results, result } = await run(dir, [js('await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500)); await write("fg.txt", "moved"); return "FG_DONE"'), 'SHOULD_NOT_BE_REACHED\n' + C], {
+    onEvent: (e) => { if (e.type === 'tool-call' && e.name === 'js_eval' && !pressed) setTimeout(() => { pressed = backgroundSnippetCall(e.callId); }, 300); },
+  });
+  const r = results[0];
+  ok(pressed && /^js-\d+$/.test(pressed), `⇱ found the running snippet (${pressed}) — it was waiting on a sleep, not a process`);
+  ok(r && /^BACKGROUNDED: the developer pressed the background control/.test(r.content) && Date.now() - t0 < 1400, `the call returned "BACKGROUNDED" without waiting (${Date.now() - t0}ms)`);
+  ok(!/SHOULD_NOT_BE_REACHED/.test(String(result && result.content)), 'the run ended there — the developer\'s turn');
+  ok(await waitDelivered(1) && /^FG_DONE/.test(delivered[0].content), 'and the snippet finished in the background and was delivered');
+  ok(existsSync(path.join(dir, 'fg.txt')), 'its write happened');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] kill_background("js-N")');
+{
+  delivered.length = 0;
+  const { results } = await run(dir, [js('await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000)); await write("never.txt", "x"); return "NEVER"', ' background="true"'), 'Started.\n' + C]);
+  const id = results[0] && results[0].background;
+  // from an ordinary snippet: stopping changes no file, so it does not wait for the snippet it stops
+  const { results: r2 } = await run(dir, [js(`return await kill_background("${id}")`), 'ok\n' + C]);
+  ok(r2[0] && new RegExp('"ok":true,"id":"' + id + '","was":"running"').test(r2[0].content), `kill_background stopped it (${String(r2[0] && r2[0].content).split('\n')[0]})`);
+  ok(await waitDelivered(1) && delivered[0].status === 'killed', `the delivery says it was stopped (${delivered[0] && delivered[0].status})`);
+  await sleep(300);
+  ok(!existsSync(path.join(dir, 'never.txt')), 'and it did not carry on');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] Esc');
+{
+  delivered.length = 0;
+  const ac = new AbortController();
+  // a background one first, then a foreground one; Esc during the foreground one
+  const p = run(dir, [
+    js('await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1500)); return "BG_SURVIVES"', ' background="true"'),
+    js('await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000)); return "FG_FINISHED"'),
+    'x\n' + C,
+  ], { signal: ac.signal, onEvent: (e) => { if (e.type === 'tool-call' && e.name === 'js_eval' && /FG_FINISHED/.test(e.args?.code || '')) setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), 300); } });
+  // an aborted run ends the worker with an AbortError, by design — what matters is what happened to each snippet
+  const results = await p.then((x) => x.results, () => []);
+  const fg = results.find((r) => !r.background);
+  ok(!fg || !/FG_FINISHED/.test(fg.content), `the foreground snippet was stopped (${fg ? String(fg.content).split('\n')[0].slice(0, 60) : 'no result — the run was cancelled'})`);
+  ok(await waitDelivered(1) && /^BG_SURVIVES/.test(delivered[0].content), 'the background one was not — it finished and was delivered');
+}
+
+const live = listSnippetJobs('', { includeDone: false });
+ok(live.length === 0, `nothing left running (${live.map((j) => j.id + ':' + j.state).join(', ')})`);
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== end
